     9	public enum BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind
    10	{
    11	    None = 0,
    12	    StartJob = 1,
    13	    AdvanceStage = 2,
    14	    AddWork = 3,
    15	    CompletePacking = 4,
    16	}
    17	
    18	public static class BuildingBoxPackingExecutionPolicy
    19	{
    20	    public static Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind> Evaluate(
    21	        JobSnapshot? job,
    22	        BuildingSnapshot? building,
    23	        CellId workerCell)
    24	    {
    25	        if (!BuildingBoxPackingJobValidation.Matches(job, building))
    26	        {
    27	            return Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind>.Failure(
    28	                BuildingBoxPackingErrors.JobTypeMismatch);
    29	        }
    30	
    31	        if (job!.IsTerminal
    32	            || (job.Status != JobStatus.Claimed && job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress))
    33	        {
    34	            return Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None);
    35	        }
    36	
    37	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot packing = building!.PackingPlan!;
    38	        if (packing.CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
    39	        {
    40	            return Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind>.Failure(
    41	                BuildingBoxPackingErrors.PackingPlanMissing);
    42	        }
    43	
    44	        BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition definition =
    45	            (BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition)job.Definition;
    46	        if (workerCell.X != definition.WorkPosition.X
    47	            || workerCell.Y != definition.WorkPosition.Y)
    48	        {
    49	            return Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.None);
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Claimed)
    53	        {
    54	            return Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.StartJob);
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        return job.Stage switch
    58	        {
    59	            JobStageKind.TravelToDestination =>
    60	                Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage),
    61	            JobStageKind.PerformWork when packing.CompletedWork
    62	                < building.Definition.BoxPolicy!.PackingWork =>
    63	                Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AddWork),
    64	            JobStageKind.PerformWork =>
    65	                Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.AdvanceStage),
    66	            JobStageKind.Finalize =>
    67	                Success(BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind.CompletePacking),
    68	            _ => Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind>.Failure(
    69	                BuildingBoxPackingErrors.InvalidJobStage),
    70	        };
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    private static Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind> Success(
    74	        BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind step)
    75	    {
    76	        return Result<BuildingBoxPackingExecutionStepKind>.Success(step);
    77	    }
    78	}
    79	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Buildings/ConfirmBuildingBoxPlacementHandler.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     3	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     4	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     5	using Dig.Application.World;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    11	using Dig.Domain.World;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Application.Buildings
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class ConfirmBuildingBoxPlacementHandler
    17	    : ICommandHandler<ConfirmBuildingBoxPlacementCommand, Result>
    18	{
    19	    private readonly BuildingCatalog _catalog;
    20	    private readonly IWorldRepository _worldRepository;
    21	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
    22	    private readonly IInventoryRepository _inventoryRepository;
    23	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
    24	    private readonly BuildingPlacementValidator _validator;
    25	    private readonly PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyValidator _physicalValidator;
    26	    private readonly BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector _surfaceFacts;
    27	    private readonly PackableBuildingContentCatalog _packableCatalog;
    28	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
    29	
    30	    public ConfirmBuildingBoxPlacementHandler(
    31	        BuildingCatalog catalog,
    32	        IWorldRepository worldRepository,
    33	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
    34	        IInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    35	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
    36	        BuildingPlacementValidator validator,
    37	        IEventSink eventSink)
    38	        : this(
    39	            catalog,
    40	            worldRepository,
    41	            buildingsRepository,
    42	            inventoryRepository,
    43	            jobRepository,
    44	            validator,
    45	            new PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyValidator(),
    46	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.Catalog,
    47	            eventSink)
    48	    {
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    public ConfirmBuildingBoxPlacementHandler(
    52	        BuildingCatalog catalog,
    53	        IWorldRepository worldRepository,
    54	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
    55	        IInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    56	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
    57	        BuildingPlacementValidator validator,
    58	        PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyValidator physicalValidator,
    59	        PackableBuildingContentCatalog packableCatalog,
    60	        IEventSink eventSink)
    61	    {
    62	        _catalog = catalog ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(catalog));
    63	        _worldRepository = worldRepository
    64	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(worldRepository));
    65	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository
    66	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(buildingsRepository));
    67	        _inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository
    68	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(inventoryRepository));
    69	        _jobRepository = jobRepository
    70	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobRepository));
    71	        _validator = validator ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(validator));
    72	        _physicalValidator = physicalValidator
    73	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(physicalValidator));
    74	        _surfaceFacts = new BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector(_physicalValidator);
    75	        _packableCatalog = packableCatalog
    76	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(packableCatalog));
    77	        _eventSink = eventSink ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(eventSink));
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    public Result Handle(ConfirmBuildingBoxPlacementCommand command)
    81	    {
    82	        if (command is null)
    83	        {
    84	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        BuildingDefinition definition = _catalog.Get(command.DefinitionId);
    88	        BuildingBoxPolicy? boxPolicy = definition.BoxPolicy;
    89	        if (boxPolicy is null)
    90	        {
    91	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.DefinitionNotBoxEnabled);
    92	        }
    93	
    94	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
    95	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
    96	        InventoryState inventory = _inventoryRepository.Get();
    97	        if (buildings.Get(command.BuildingId) is not null)
    98	        {
    99	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.AlreadyExists);
   100	        }
   101	
   102	        if (jobs.Get(command.JobId) is not null)
   103	        {
   104	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.JobAlreadyExists);
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        ItemStackSnapshot? source = inventory.GetStack(command.SourceStackId);
   108	        if (source is null)
   109	        {
   110	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.SourceStackMissing);
   111	        }
   112	
   113	        if (source.ItemId != boxPolicy.BoxItemId)
   114	        {
   115	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.SourceItemMismatch);
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        ItemDefinition itemDefinition = inventory.Catalog.Get(source.ItemId);
   119	        if (itemDefinition.MaximumStackSize != 1 || source.Quantity != 1)
   120	        {
   121	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.BoxMustBeSingle);
   122	        }
   123	
   124	        WorldSnapshot world = _worldRepository.Get().CreateSnapshot();
   125	        var occupiedCells = buildings.GetOccupiedCells();
   126	        BuildingPlacementResult placement = _validator.Validate(
   127	            definition,
   128	            command.Origin,
   129	            command.Orientation,
   130	            world,
   131	            occupiedCells,
   132	            command.ReachableCells,
   133	            command.EcologyBlockedCells);
   134	        if (!placement.Succeeded)
   135	        {
   136	            return Result.Failure(placement.Error!);
   137	        }
   138	
   139	        if (!BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector.HasSupportingPlane(
   140	            placement.Footprint,
   141	            world))
   142	        {
   143	            return Result.Failure(PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing);
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        if (_packableCatalog.TryGet(
   147	            definition.Id,
   148	            out PackableBuildingContentDefinition? content))
   149	        {
   150	            PackableBuildingSurfacePolicy policy = content!.Placement.ToSurfacePolicy();
   151	            PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyResult physical = _physicalValidator.Validate(
   152	                policy,
   153	                command.Origin,
   154	                _surfaceFacts.Project(policy, command.Origin, world),
   155	                occupiedCells);
   156	            if (!physical.Succeeded)
   157	            {
   158	                return Result.Failure(physical.Error!);
   159	            }
   160	        }
   161	
   162	        Result reserved = inventory.ReserveQuantity(
   163	            command.SourceStackId,
   164	            command.JobId,
   165	            quantity: 1,
   166	            command.Tick);
   167	        if (reserved.IsFailure)
   168	        {
   169	            return reserved;
   170	        }
   171	
   172	        BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition job = new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition(
   173	            command.JobId,
   174	            command.BuildingId,
   175	            command.SourceStackId,
   176	            command.Origin,
   177	            placement.WorkPosition,
   178	            command.Priority,
   179	            command.Tick,
   180	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   181	        Result added = jobs.Add(job);
   182	        if (added.IsFailure)
   183	        {
   184	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   185	            return added;
   186	        }
   187	
   188	        Result placed = buildings.PlaceBoxPlan(
   189	            command.BuildingId,
   190	            command.SourceStackId,
   191	            command.JobId,
   192	            definition,
   193	            command.Origin,
   194	            command.Orientation,
   195	            placement,
   196	            command.Tick);
   197	        if (placed.IsFailure)
   198	        {
   199	            jobs.Cancel(
   200	                command.JobId,
   201	                new JobBlockReason("box_plan_failed", "BuildingBox plan creation failed."),
   202	                command.Tick);
   203	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   204	            return placed;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        Result available = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   208	        if (available.IsFailure)
   209	        {
   210	            buildings.Cancel(command.BuildingId, "box_job_unavailable", command.Tick);
   211	            jobs.Cancel(
   212	                command.JobId,
   213	                new JobBlockReason("box_job_unavailable", "BuildingBox job could not start."),
   214	                command.Tick);
   215	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   216	            return available;
   217	        }
   218	
   219	        if (source.Location.Kind == ItemLocationKind.AgentInventory
   220	            && source.Location.HasOwner)
   221	        {
   222	            Result claimed = jobs.Claim(
   223	                command.JobId,
   224	                source.Location.OwnerId,
   225	                command.Tick);
   226	            if (claimed.IsFailure)
   227	            {
   228	                buildings.Cancel(
   229	                    command.BuildingId,
   230	                    "box_holder_claim_failed",
   231	                    command.Tick);
   232	                jobs.Cancel(
   233	                    command.JobId,
   234	                    new JobBlockReason(
   235	                        "box_holder_claim_failed",
   236	                        "The BuildingBox holder could not own the direct assembly job."),
   237	                    command.Tick);
   238	                inventory.ReleaseReservations(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   239	                return claimed;
   240	            }
   241	        }
   242	
   243	        _inventoryRepository.Save(inventory);
   244	        _buildingsRepository.Save(buildings);
   245	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   246	        _eventSink.Append(inventory.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   247	        _eventSink.Append(buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   248	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   249	        return Result.Success();
   250	    }
   251	}
   252	
   253	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Buildings/ConstructionUseCases.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Application.Buildings
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class CreateConstructionJobHandler
    17	    : ICommandHandler<CreateConstructionJobCommand, Result>
    18	{
    19	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
    20	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
    21	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
    22	
    23	    public CreateConstructionJobHandler(
    24	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
    25	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
    26	        IEventSink eventSink)
    27	    {
    28	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository;
    29	        _jobRepository = jobRepository;
    30	        _eventSink = eventSink;
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    public Result Handle(CreateConstructionJobCommand command)
    34	    {
    35	        if (command is null)
    36	        {
    37	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    38	        }
    39	
    40	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
    41	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
    42	        BuildingSnapshot? building = buildings.Get(command.BuildingId);
    43	        if (building is null)
    44	        {
    45	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
    46	        }
    47	
    48	        if (building.Status != BuildingStatus.ReadyToBuild)
    49	        {
    50	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
    51	        }
    52	
    53	        if (!command.ReachableCells.Contains(building.WorkPosition))
    54	        {
    55	            buildings.Block(
    56	                command.BuildingId,
    57	                $"Work position {building.WorkPosition} is unreachable.");
    58	            _buildingsRepository.Save(buildings);
    59	            _eventSink.Append(buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
    60	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NoReachableWorkPosition);
    61	        }
    62	
    63	        BuildingWorkJobDefinition definition = new BuildingWorkJobDefinition(
    64	            command.JobId,
    65	            command.BuildingId,
    66	            BuildingWorkKind.Construction,
    67	            building.WorkPosition,
    68	            command.Priority,
    69	            command.Tick,
    70	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    71	        Result added = jobs.Add(definition);
    72	        if (added.IsFailure)
    73	        {
    74	            return added;
    75	        }
    76	
    77	        Result available = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
    78	        if (available.IsFailure)
    79	        {
    80	            return available;
    81	        }
    82	
    83	        Result started = buildings.StartConstruction(command.BuildingId);
    84	        if (started.IsFailure)
    85	        {
    86	            jobs.Cancel(
    87	                command.JobId,
    88	                new JobBlockReason("building_state_changed", started.Error!.Message),
    89	                command.Tick);
    90	            return started;
    91	        }
    92	
    93	        SaveAndPublish(buildings, jobs);
    94	        return Result.Success();
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    private void SaveAndPublish(BuildingsState buildings, JobSystem jobs)
    98	    {
    99	        _buildingsRepository.Save(buildings);
   100	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   101	        _eventSink.Append(buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   102	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   103	    }
   104	}
   105	
   106	public sealed class AddConstructionWorkHandler
   107	    : ICommandHandler<AddConstructionWorkCommand, Result>
   108	{
   109	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
   110	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
   111	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
   112	
   113	    public AddConstructionWorkHandler(
   114	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
   115	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
   116	        IEventSink eventSink)
   117	    {
   118	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository;
   119	        _jobRepository = jobRepository;
   120	        _eventSink = eventSink;
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    public Result Handle(AddConstructionWorkCommand command)
   124	    {
   125	        if (command is null)
   126	        {
   127	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
   128	        }
   129	
   130	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
   131	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
   132	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(command.JobId);
   133	        if (job?.Definition is not BuildingWorkJobDefinition work
   134	            || work.BuildingId != command.BuildingId
   135	            || work.Kind != BuildingWorkKind.Construction)
   136	        {
   137	            return Result.Failure(BuildingUseCaseErrors.JobMismatch);
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        if (job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress
   141	            || job.Stage != JobStageKind.PerformWork)
   142	        {
   143	            return Result.Failure(HaulingErrors.InvalidStage);
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        Result result = buildings.AddConstructionWork(
   147	            command.BuildingId,
   148	            command.WorkAmount,
   149	            command.Tick);
   150	        if (result.IsFailure)
   151	        {
   152	            return result;
   153	        }
   154	
   155	        if (buildings.Get(command.BuildingId)!.Status == BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete)
   156	        {
   157	            Result advanced = jobs.AdvanceStage(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   158	            if (advanced.IsFailure)
   159	            {
   160	                throw new InvalidOperationException(
   161	                    "Construction work reached completion but job could not finalize.");
   162	            }
   163	        }
   164	
   165	        _buildingsRepository.Save(buildings);
   166	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   167	        _eventSink.Append(buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   168	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   169	        return Result.Success();
   170	    }
   171	}
   172	
   173	public sealed class CompleteConstructionHandler
   174	    : ICommandHandler<CompleteConstructionCommand, Result>
   175	{
   176	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
   177	    private readonly IInventoryRepository _inventoryRepository;
   178	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
   179	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
   180	    private readonly IAgentSkillGrantService _skillGrants;
   181	
   182	    public CompleteConstructionHandler(
   183	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
   184	        IInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
   185	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
   186	        IEventSink eventSink,
   187	        IAgentSkillGrantService skillGrants)
   188	    {
   189	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository;
   190	        _inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository;
   191	        _jobRepository = jobRepository;
   192	        _eventSink = eventSink;
   193	        _skillGrants = skillGrants
   194	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(skillGrants));
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    public Result Handle(CompleteConstructionCommand command)
   198	    {
   199	        if (command is null)
   200	        {
   201	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
   202	        }
   203	
   204	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
   205	        InventoryState inventory = _inventoryRepository.Get();
   206	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
   207	        BuildingSnapshot? building = buildings.Get(command.BuildingId);
   208	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(command.JobId);
   209	        if (building is null)
   210	        {
   211	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   212	        }
   213	
   214	        if (job?.Definition is not BuildingWorkJobDefinition work
   215	            || work.BuildingId != command.BuildingId
   216	            || work.Kind != BuildingWorkKind.Construction)
   217	        {
   218	            return Result.Failure(BuildingUseCaseErrors.JobMismatch);
   219	        }
   220	
   221	        if (building.Status != BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete
   222	            || job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress
   223	            || job.Stage != JobStageKind.Finalize)
   224	        {
   225	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.WorkIncomplete);
   226	        }
   227	
   228	        if (!job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
   229	        {
   230	            return Result.Failure(BuildingUseCaseErrors.JobMismatch);
   231	        }
   232	
   233	        SkillGrantBundle skillBundle = CreateSkillBundle(job, work, command.Tick);
   234	        Result skillValidation = _skillGrants.Validate(skillBundle);
   235	        if (skillValidation.IsFailure)
   236	        {
   237	            return skillValidation;
   238	        }
   239	
   240	        ItemConsumptionRequest[] materials = building.Definition.Materials
   241	            .Select(value => new ItemConsumptionRequest(value.ItemId, value.Quantity))
   242	            .ToArray();
   243	        Result consumed = inventory.ConsumeAvailableAt(
   244	            ItemLocation.InBuilding(command.BuildingId),
   245	            materials,
   246	            command.Tick);
   247	        if (consumed.IsFailure)
   248	        {
   249	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.MaterialsUnavailable);
   250	        }
   251	
   252	        Result completedBuilding = buildings.Complete(command.BuildingId, command.Tick);
   253	        if (completedBuilding.IsFailure)
   254	        {
   255	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   256	                "Validated building could not complete after material consumption.");
   257	        }
   258	
   259	        Result completedJob = jobs.AdvanceStage(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   260	        if (completedJob.IsFailure)
   261	        {
   262	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   263	                "Validated construction job could not complete its final stage.");
   264	        }
   265	
   266	        ApplyConfirmedSkillResult(skillBundle);
   267	        _buildingsRepository.Save(buildings);
   268	        _inventoryRepository.Save(inventory);
   269	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   270	        _eventSink.Append(buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   271	        _eventSink.Append(inventory.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   272	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   273	        return Result.Success();
   274	    }
   275	
   276	    private static SkillGrantBundle CreateSkillBundle(
   277	        JobSnapshot job,
   278	        BuildingWorkJobDefinition work,
   279	        long tick)
   280	    {
   281	        return new SkillGrantBundle(
   282	            job.AssignedAgentId!.Value,
   283	            SkillGrantSourceKind.JobCompleted,
   284	            job.Id.ToString(),
   285	            tick,
   286	            work.SkillGrantProfile.Multiply(1));
   287	    }
   288	
   289	    private void ApplyConfirmedSkillResult(SkillGrantBundle bundle)
   290	    {
   291	        Result<SkillRedistributionReport> applied = _skillGrants.ApplyConfirmed(bundle);
   292	        if (applied.IsFailure)
   293	        {
   294	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   295	                $"Completed construction skill grant failed: {applied.Error}");
   296	        }
   297	    }
   298	}
   299	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Buildings/StartBuildingBoxPackingHandler.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     3	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     4	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Application.Buildings
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class StartBuildingBoxPackingHandler
    14	    : ICommandHandler<StartBuildingBoxPackingCommand, Result>
    15	{
    16	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
    17	    private readonly IInventoryRepository _inventoryRepository;
    18	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
    19	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
    20	
    21	    public StartBuildingBoxPackingHandler(
    22	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
    23	        IInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    24	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
    25	        IEventSink eventSink)
    26	    {
    27	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository
    28	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(buildingsRepository));
    29	        _inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository
    30	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(inventoryRepository));
    31	        _jobRepository = jobRepository
    32	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobRepository));
    33	        _eventSink = eventSink ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(eventSink));
    34	    }
    35	
    36	    public Result Handle(StartBuildingBoxPackingCommand command)
    37	    {
    38	        if (command is null)
    39	        {
    40	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    41	        }
    42	
    43	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
    44	        BuildingSnapshot? building = buildings.Get(command.BuildingId);
    45	        if (building is null)
    46	        {
    47	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
    48	        }
    49	
    50	        BuildingBoxPolicy? policy = building.Definition.BoxPolicy;
    51	        if (policy is null || !policy.PackingEnabled)
    52	        {
    53	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxPackingErrors.PackingDisabled);
    54	        }
    55	
    56	        if (building.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
    57	            || building.PackingPlan?.CommitState == BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
    58	        {
    59	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
    60	        }
    61	
    62	        InventoryState inventory = _inventoryRepository.Get();
    63	        if (inventory.GetStack(command.OutputStackId) is not null)
    64	        {
    65	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxPackingErrors.OutputStackExists);
    66	        }
    67	
    68	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
    69	        if (jobs.Get(command.JobId) is not null)
    70	        {
    71	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.JobAlreadyExists);
    72	        }
    73	
    74	        ItemDefinition boxItem = inventory.Catalog.Get(policy.BoxItemId);
    75	        if (boxItem.MaximumStackSize != 1)
    76	        {
    77	            return Result.Failure(BuildingBoxErrors.BoxMustBeSingle);
    78	        }
    79	
    80	        BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition definition =
    81	            new BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition(
    82	                command.JobId,
    83	                building.Id,
    84	                command.OutputStackId,
    85	                building.WorkPosition,
    86	                command.Priority,
    87	                command.Tick,
    88	                JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    89	
    90	        Result started = buildings.StartBoxPacking(
    91	            building.Id,
    92	            command.JobId,
    93	            command.OutputStackId,
    94	            command.Tick);
    95	        if (started.IsFailure)
    96	        {
    97	            return started;
    98	        }
    99	
   100	        Result added = jobs.Add(definition);
   101	        if (added.IsFailure)
   102	        {
   103	            return added;
   104	        }
   105	
   106	        Result available = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   107	        if (available.IsFailure)
   108	        {
   109	            return available;
   110	        }
   111	
   112	        _buildingsRepository.Save(buildings);
   113	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   114	        _eventSink.Append(buildings.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   115	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   116	        return Result.Success();
   117	    }
   118	}
   119	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Ecology/MushroomChopCompletionUseCase.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     3	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Application.Ecology
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler
    17	    : ICommandHandler<CompleteMushroomChopCommand, Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>>
    18	{
    19	    private readonly IMushroomRepository _mushrooms;
    20	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobs;
    21	    private readonly IInventoryRepository _inventory;
    22	    private readonly IAgentSkillGrantService _skills;
    23	    private readonly IEventSink _events;
    24	
    25	    public CompleteMushroomChopCommandHandler(
    26	        IMushroomRepository mushrooms,
    27	        IJobRepository jobs,
    28	        IInventoryRepository inventory,
    29	        IAgentSkillGrantService skills,
    30	        IEventSink events)
    31	    {
    32	        _mushrooms = mushrooms ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(mushrooms));
    33	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
    34	        _inventory = inventory ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(inventory));
    35	        _skills = skills ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(skills));
    36	        _events = events ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    public Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult> Handle(CompleteMushroomChopCommand command)
    40	    {
    41	        if (command is null)
    42	        {
    43	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    44	        }
    45	
    46	        JobSystem jobs = _jobs.Get();
    47	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(command.JobId);
    48	        if (job?.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition definition)
    49	        {
    50	            return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Failure(job is null
    51	                ? JobErrors.NotFound
    52	                : MushroomApplicationErrors.JobTypeUnsupported);
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        if (job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress || job.Stage != JobStageKind.Finalize)
    56	        {
    57	            return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Failure(
    58	                MushroomApplicationErrors.JobNotReady);
    59	        }
    60	
    61	        EntityId workerId = job.AssignedAgentId
    62	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("An in-progress mushroom job must retain its worker.");
    63	        MushroomState mushrooms = _mushrooms.Get();
    64	        MushroomSiteSnapshot? site = mushrooms.Get(definition.SiteId);
    65	        if (site is null)
    66	        {
    67	            return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Failure(MushroomErrors.NotFound);
    68	        }
    69	
    70	        if (site.GrowthGeneration != definition.GrowthGeneration
    71	            || site.ActiveChopJobId != command.JobId
    72	            || site.CompletedSwings < site.RequiredSwings)
    73	        {
    74	            return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Failure(
    75	                MushroomApplicationErrors.GenerationConflict);
    76	        }
    77	
    78	        MushroomDefinition mushroomDefinition = mushrooms.Catalog.Get(site.DefinitionId);
    79	        MushroomDropProfile drops = mushroomDefinition.GetDrops(site.Stage);
    80	        MushroomOutputUnits output = MushroomOutputUnits.Create(command.OutputSeedId, drops);
    81	        InventoryState inventory = _inventory.Get();
    82	        Result outputValidation = output.Validate(
    83	            inventory,
    84	            mushroomDefinition.CapItemId,
    85	            mushroomDefinition.LegItemId);
    86	        if (outputValidation.IsFailure)
    87	        {
    88	            return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Failure(outputValidation.Error!);
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        string sourceId = $"mushroom:{site.SiteId}:{site.GrowthGeneration + 1}";
    92	        SkillGrantBundle skillBundle = new SkillGrantBundle(
    93	            workerId,
    94	            SkillGrantSourceKind.JobCompleted,
    95	            sourceId,
    96	            command.Tick,
    97	            new[]
    98	            {
    99	                new SkillGrant(
   100	                    AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking,
   101	                    MushroomDefinition.WoodworkingGrantUnits),
   102	            });
   103	        Result skillValidation = _skills.Validate(skillBundle);
   104	        if (skillValidation.IsFailure)
   105	        {
   106	            return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Failure(skillValidation.Error!);
   107	        }
   108	
   109	        Result<MushroomChopCommit> committed = mushrooms.CommitChop(
   110	            definition.SiteId,
   111	            command.JobId,
   112	            workerId,
   113	            command.Tick);
   114	        EnsureCommitStep(committed.IsSuccess, committed.Error);
   115	        MushroomChopCommit chop = committed.Value;
   116	        if (output.Caps.Length > 0)
   117	        {
   118	            EnsureCommitStep(inventory.AddUnits(
   119	                output.Caps,
   120	                chop.CapItemId,
   121	                ItemLocation.InWorld(chop.Cell),
   122	                command.Tick));
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        if (output.Legs.Length > 0)
   126	        {
   127	            EnsureCommitStep(inventory.AddUnits(
   128	                output.Legs,
   129	                chop.LegItemId,
   130	                ItemLocation.InWorld(chop.Cell),
   131	                command.Tick));
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        EnsureCommitStep(jobs.AdvanceStage(command.JobId, command.Tick));
   135	        Result<SkillRedistributionReport> skill = _skills.ApplyConfirmed(skillBundle);
   136	        EnsureCommitStep(skill.IsSuccess, skill.Error);
   137	        _mushrooms.Save(mushrooms);
   138	        _inventory.Save(inventory);
   139	        _jobs.Save(jobs);
   140	        _events.Append(mushrooms.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   141	        _events.Append(inventory.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   142	        _events.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   143	        return Result<MushroomChopCompletionResult>.Success(
   144	            new MushroomChopCompletionResult(
   145	                command.JobId,
   146	                definition.SiteId,
   147	                chop.ChoppedStage,
   148	                new ReadOnlyCollection<EntityId>(output.Caps),
   149	                new ReadOnlyCollection<EntityId>(output.Legs),
   150	                chop.SkillSourceId));
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    private static void EnsureCommitStep(bool succeeded, DomainError? error)
   154	    {
   155	        if (!succeeded)
   156	        {
   157	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Validated mushroom completion failed: {error}");
   158	        }
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    private static void EnsureCommitStep(Result result) =>
   162	        EnsureCommitStep(result.IsSuccess, result.Error);
   163	}
   164	
   165	public sealed class CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler
   166	    : ICommandHandler<CancelMushroomChopCommand, Result>
   167	{
   168	    private readonly IMushroomRepository _mushrooms;
   169	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobs;
   170	    private readonly IEventSink _events;
   171	
   172	    public CancelMushroomChopCommandHandler(
   173	        IMushroomRepository mushrooms,
   174	        IJobRepository jobs,
   175	        IEventSink events)
   176	    {
   177	        _mushrooms = mushrooms ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(mushrooms));
   178	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
   179	        _events = events ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    public Result Handle(CancelMushroomChopCommand command)
   183	    {
   184	        JobSystem jobs = _jobs.Get();
   185	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(command.JobId);
   186	        if (job?.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition definition)
   187	        {
   188	            return Result.Failure(job is null
   189	                ? JobErrors.NotFound
   190	                : MushroomApplicationErrors.JobTypeUnsupported);
   191	        }
   192	
   193	        if (job.IsTerminal || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue)
   194	        {
   195	            return Result.Failure(MushroomApplicationErrors.JobNotReady);
   196	        }
   197	
   198	        MushroomState mushrooms = _mushrooms.Get();
   199	        Result cancelled = jobs.Cancel(
   200	            command.JobId,
   201	            new JobBlockReason(command.ReasonCode, "Mushroom chopping was interrupted."),
   202	            command.Tick);
   203	        if (cancelled.IsFailure)
   204	        {
   205	            return cancelled;
   206	        }
   207	
   208	        Result released = mushrooms.ReleaseChop(
   209	            definition.SiteId,
   210	            command.JobId,
   211	            job.AssignedAgentId.Value,
   212	            command.Tick);
   213	        if (released.IsFailure)
   214	        {
   215	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   216	                $"Cancelled mushroom job could not release its target: {released.Error}");
   217	        }
   218	
   219	        _mushrooms.Save(mushrooms);
   220	        _jobs.Save(jobs);
   221	        _events.Append(mushrooms.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   222	        _events.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   223	        return Result.Success();
   224	    }
   225	}
   226	
   227	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Ecology/MushroomChopUseCases.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    12	
    13	namespace Dig.Application.Ecology
    14	{
    15	
    16	public sealed class StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler
    17	    : ICommandHandler<StartDirectMushroomChopCommand, Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>>
    18	{
    19	    private readonly IMushroomRepository _mushrooms;
    20	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobs;
    21	    private readonly IAgentSkillLevelReader _skills;
    22	    private readonly IMushroomSwingRandom _random;
    23	    private readonly IEventSink _events;
    24	
    25	    public StartDirectMushroomChopCommandHandler(
    26	        IMushroomRepository mushrooms,
    27	        IJobRepository jobs,
    28	        IAgentSkillLevelReader skills,
    29	        IMushroomSwingRandom random,
    30	        IEventSink events)
    31	    {
    32	        _mushrooms = mushrooms ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(mushrooms));
    33	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
    34	        _skills = skills ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(skills));
    35	        _random = random ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(random));
    36	        _events = events ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    public Result<MushroomChopStartedResult> Handle(StartDirectMushroomChopCommand command)
    40	    {
    41	        if (command is null)
    42	        {
    43	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    44	        }
    45	
    46	        MushroomState mushrooms = _mushrooms.Get();
    47	        MushroomSiteSnapshot? site = mushrooms.Get(command.SiteId);
    48	        if (site is null)
    49	        {
    50	            return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(MushroomErrors.NotFound);
    51	        }
    52	
    53	        if (!site.IsVisible)
    54	        {
    55	            return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(MushroomErrors.NotVisible);
    56	        }
    57	
    58	        Result<int> skill = _skills.GetSkillUnits(command.WorkerId, AgentSkillCatalog.Woodworking);
    59	        if (skill.IsFailure)
    60	        {
    61	            return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(skill.Error!);
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        JobSystem jobs = _jobs.Get();
    65	        ReservationSnapshot? workerReservation = jobs.GetReservations()
    66	            .FirstOrDefault(reservation =>
    67	                reservation.Key == ReservationKey.ForAgent(command.WorkerId));
    68	        if (workerReservation != null
    69	            && (!site.ActiveChopJobId.HasValue
    70	                || workerReservation.JobId != site.ActiveChopJobId.Value))
    71	        {
    72	            return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(
    73	                JobErrors.AgentUnavailable);
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        (int minimum, int maximum) = MushroomDefinition.GetRequiredSwingBand(skill.Value);
    77	        int requiredSwings = _random.SelectRequiredSwings(
    78	            command.SiteId,
    79	            command.WorkerId,
    80	            minimum,
    81	            maximum);
    82	        EntityId? replacedJobId = site.ActiveChopJobId;
    83	        if (replacedJobId.HasValue)
    84	        {
    85	            Result takeover = CancelExistingAttempt(
    86	                mushrooms,
    87	                jobs,
    88	                site,
    89	                command.Tick);
    90	            if (takeover.IsFailure)
    91	            {
    92	                return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(takeover.Error!);
    93	            }
    94	        }
    95	
    96	        MushroomChopJobDefinition definition = new MushroomChopJobDefinition(
    97	            command.JobId,
    98	            command.SiteId,
    99	            site.Cell,
   100	            command.WorkPosition,
   101	            site.GrowthGeneration,
   102	            requiredSwings,
   103	            command.Priority,
   104	            command.Tick,
   105	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   106	        Result added = jobs.Add(definition);
   107	        if (added.IsFailure)
   108	        {
   109	            return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(added.Error!);
   110	        }
   111	
   112	        Result available = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   113	        if (available.IsFailure)
   114	        {
   115	            return RejectNewAttempt(
   116	                mushrooms,
   117	                jobs,
   118	                command.JobId,
   119	                available.Error!,
   120	                command.Tick);
   121	        }
   122	
   123	        Result canClaim = jobs.CanClaim(
   124	            command.JobId,
   125	            command.WorkerId,
   126	            toolStackId: null,
   127	            command.Tick);
   128	        if (canClaim.IsFailure)
   129	        {
   130	            return RejectNewAttempt(
   131	                mushrooms,
   132	                jobs,
   133	                command.JobId,
   134	                canClaim.Error!,
   135	                command.Tick);
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        Result claimed = jobs.Claim(command.JobId, command.WorkerId, command.Tick);
   139	        if (claimed.IsFailure)
   140	        {
   141	            return RejectNewAttempt(
   142	                mushrooms,
   143	                jobs,
   144	                command.JobId,
   145	                claimed.Error!,
   146	                command.Tick);
   147	        }
   148	
   149	        Result started = jobs.Start(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   150	        if (started.IsFailure)
   151	        {
   152	            return RejectNewAttempt(
   153	                mushrooms,
   154	                jobs,
   155	                command.JobId,
   156	                started.Error!,
   157	                command.Tick);
   158	        }
   159	
   160	        Result begun = mushrooms.BeginChop(
   161	            command.SiteId,
   162	            command.JobId,
   163	            command.WorkerId,
   164	            requiredSwings,
   165	            command.Tick);
   166	        if (begun.IsFailure)
   167	        {
   168	            return RejectNewAttempt(
   169	                mushrooms,
   170	                jobs,
   171	                command.JobId,
   172	                begun.Error!,
   173	                command.Tick);
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        SaveAndPublish(mushrooms, jobs);
   177	        return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Success(new MushroomChopStartedResult(
   178	            command.JobId,
   179	            command.SiteId,
   180	            command.WorkerId,
   181	            requiredSwings,
   182	            replacedJobId));
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    private static Result CancelExistingAttempt(
   186	        MushroomState mushrooms,
   187	        JobSystem jobs,
   188	        MushroomSiteSnapshot site,
   189	        long tick)
   190	    {
   191	        EntityId oldJobId = site.ActiveChopJobId!.Value;
   192	        EntityId oldWorkerId = site.ActiveWorkerId!.Value;
   193	        JobSnapshot? oldJob = jobs.Get(oldJobId);
   194	        if (oldJob is null || oldJob.IsTerminal)
   195	        {
   196	            return Result.Failure(MushroomApplicationErrors.GenerationConflict);
   197	        }
   198	
   199	        Result cancelled = jobs.Cancel(
   200	            oldJobId,
   201	            new JobBlockReason(
   202	                "mushroom_direct_takeover",
   203	                "A new direct mushroom order replaced this worker."),
   204	            tick);
   205	        if (cancelled.IsFailure)
   206	        {
   207	            return cancelled;
   208	        }
   209	
   210	        return mushrooms.ReleaseChop(site.SiteId, oldJobId, oldWorkerId, tick);
   211	    }
   212	    private void SaveAndPublish(MushroomState mushrooms, JobSystem jobs)
   213	    {
   214	        _mushrooms.Save(mushrooms);
   215	        _jobs.Save(jobs);
   216	        _events.Append(mushrooms.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   217	        _events.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   218	    }
   219	
   220	    private Result<MushroomChopStartedResult> RejectNewAttempt(
   221	        MushroomState mushrooms,
   222	        JobSystem jobs,
   223	        EntityId jobId,
   224	        DomainError error,
   225	        long tick)
   226	    {
   227	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(jobId);
   228	        if (job != null && !job.IsTerminal)
   229	        {
   230	            Result cancelled = jobs.Cancel(
   231	                jobId,
   232	                new JobBlockReason(
   233	                    "mushroom_start_rejected",
   234	                    error.Message),
   235	                tick);
   236	            if (cancelled.IsFailure)
   237	            {
   238	                error = cancelled.Error!;
   239	            }
   240	        }
   241	
   242	        SaveAndPublish(mushrooms, jobs);
   243	        return Result<MushroomChopStartedResult>.Failure(error);
   244	    }
   245	}
   246	
   247	public sealed class ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler
   248	    : ICommandHandler<ArriveAtMushroomCommand, Result>
   249	{
   250	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobs;
   251	    private readonly IEventSink _events;
   252	
   253	    public ArriveAtMushroomCommandHandler(IJobRepository jobs, IEventSink events)
   254	    {
   255	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
   256	        _events = events ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    public Result Handle(ArriveAtMushroomCommand command)
   260	    {
   261	        JobSystem jobs = _jobs.Get();
   262	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(command.JobId);
   263	        if (job?.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition
   264	            || job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress
   265	            || job.Stage != JobStageKind.TravelToTarget)
   266	        {
   267	            return Result.Failure(job is null
   268	                ? JobErrors.NotFound
   269	                : MushroomApplicationErrors.JobNotReady);
   270	        }
   271	
   272	        Result result = jobs.AdvanceStage(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   273	        if (result.IsSuccess)
   274	        {
   275	            _jobs.Save(jobs);
   276	            _events.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   277	        }
   278	
   279	        return result;
   280	    }
   281	}
   282	
   283	public sealed class CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler
   284	    : ICommandHandler<CompleteMushroomSwingCommand, Result<bool>>
   285	{
   286	    private readonly IMushroomRepository _mushrooms;
   287	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobs;
   288	    private readonly IEventSink _events;
   289	
   290	    public CompleteMushroomSwingCommandHandler(
   291	        IMushroomRepository mushrooms,
   292	        IJobRepository jobs,
   293	        IEventSink events)
   294	    {
   295	        _mushrooms = mushrooms ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(mushrooms));
   296	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
   297	        _events = events ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    public Result<bool> Handle(CompleteMushroomSwingCommand command)
   301	    {
   302	        JobSystem jobs = _jobs.Get();
   303	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(command.JobId);
   304	        if (job?.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition definition)
   305	        {
   306	            return Result<bool>.Failure(job is null
   307	                ? JobErrors.NotFound
   308	                : MushroomApplicationErrors.JobTypeUnsupported);
   309	        }
   310	
   311	        if (job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress || job.Stage != JobStageKind.PerformWork)
   312	        {
   313	            return Result<bool>.Failure(MushroomApplicationErrors.JobNotReady);
   314	        }
   315	
   316	        EntityId workerId = job.AssignedAgentId
   317	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("An in-progress mushroom job must retain its worker.");
   318	        MushroomState mushrooms = _mushrooms.Get();
   319	        Result<bool> swing = mushrooms.CompleteSwing(
   320	            definition.SiteId,
   321	            command.JobId,
   322	            workerId,
   323	            command.Tick);
   324	        if (swing.IsFailure)
   325	        {
   326	            return swing;
   327	        }
   328	
   329	        if (swing.Value)
   330	        {
   331	            EnsureCommitStep(jobs.AdvanceStage(command.JobId, command.Tick));
   332	        }
   333	
   334	        _mushrooms.Save(mushrooms);
   335	        _jobs.Save(jobs);
   336	        _events.Append(mushrooms.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   337	        _events.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   338	        return swing;
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    private static void EnsureCommitStep(Result result)
   342	    {
   343	        if (result.IsFailure)
   344	        {
   345	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Validated mushroom swing failed: {result.Error}");
   346	        }
   347	    }
   348	}
   349	
   350	}
