===== src/Dig.Application/Navigation/UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlanner.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Navigation;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Application.Navigation
     8	{
     9	
    10	public sealed class UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan
    11	{
    12	    public UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan(
    13	        CellId destination,
    14	        NavigationPath path,
    15	        int shaftGapCount)
    16	    {
    17	        if (shaftGapCount < 0)
    18	        {
    19	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(shaftGapCount));
    20	        }
    21	
    22	        Destination = destination;
    23	        Path = path ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(path));
    24	        ShaftGapCount = shaftGapCount;
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    public CellId Destination { get; }
    28	    public NavigationPath Path { get; }
    29	    public int ShaftGapCount { get; }
    30	}
    31	
    32	public sealed class UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlanner
    33	{
    34	    private readonly NavigationPathfinder _pathfinder;
    35	
    36	    public UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlanner(NavigationPathfinder pathfinder)
    37	    {
    38	        _pathfinder = pathfinder ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(pathfinder));
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    public UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan? Plan(
    42	        CellId start,
    43	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
    44	        WorldSnapshot world)
    45	    {
    46	        if (navigation == null)
    47	        {
    48	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(navigation));
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        if (world == null)
    52	        {
    53	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(world));
    54	        }
    55	
    56	        Dictionary<CellId, CellSnapshot> cells = world.Chunks
    57	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
    58	            .ToDictionary(cell => cell.Id);
    59	        if (!navigation.IsWalkable(start) || HasFullSupport(start, cells))
    60	        {
    61	            return null;
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        List<UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan> reachable =
    65	            new List<UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan>();
    66	        foreach (CellId candidate in navigation.Chunks
    67	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.WalkableCells)
    68	            .Distinct()
    69	            .Where(cell => HasFullSupport(cell, cells))
    70	            .OrderBy(cell => cell))
    71	        {
    72	            PathResult path = _pathfinder.FindPath(
    73	                navigation,
    74	                new PathRequest(start, candidate, navigation.NavigationVersion));
    75	            if (path.Succeeded)
    76	            {
    77	                reachable.Add(new UnsupportedResidentRecoveryPlan(
    78	                    candidate,
    79	                    path.Path!,
    80	                    CountShaftGaps(navigation, path.Path!)));
    81	            }
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        return reachable
    85	            .OrderBy(plan => plan.ShaftGapCount)
    86	            .ThenBy(plan => plan.Path.TotalCost)
    87	            .ThenBy(plan => plan.Destination)
    88	            .FirstOrDefault();
    89	    }
    90	
    91	    private static int CountShaftGaps(
    92	        NavigationSnapshot navigation,
    93	        NavigationPath path)
    94	    {
    95	        int count = 0;
    96	        for (int index = 1; index < path.Cells.Count; index++)
    97	        {
    98	            CellId target = path.Cells[index];
    99	            NavigationTransition transition = navigation.GetTransitions(
   100	                    path.Cells[index - 1])
   101	                .Single(value => value.Target == target);
   102	            if (transition.TraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse)
   103	            {
   104	                count++;
   105	            }
   106	        }
   107	
   108	        return count;
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    public static bool HasFullSupport(
   112	        CellId cell,
   113	        IReadOnlyDictionary<CellId, CellSnapshot> worldCells)
   114	    {
   115	        CellId below = new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y + 1, cell.Z);
   116	        return worldCells.TryGetValue(below, out CellSnapshot support)
   117	            && support.IsSolid
   118	            && support.State.CompletedExcavationQuarters == ExcavationQuarter.None;
   119	    }
   120	}
   121	
   122	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Production/BuildingSupplyUseCases.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Content;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    12	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    13	using Dig.Domain.Production;
    14	
    15	namespace Dig.Application.Production
    16	{
    17	
    18	public sealed class CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler
    19	    : ICommandHandler<CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand, Result>
    20	{
    21	    private readonly ProductionContentCatalog _content;
    22	    private readonly IBuildingSupplyRepository _supplyRepository;
    23	    private readonly IProductionRepository _productionRepository;
    24	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
    25	    private readonly IInventoryRepository _inventoryRepository;
    26	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
    27	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
    28	
    29	    public CreateBuildingSupplyJobHandler(
    30	        ProductionContentCatalog content,
    31	        IBuildingSupplyRepository supplyRepository,
    32	        IProductionRepository productionRepository,
    33	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
    34	        IInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    35	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
    36	        IEventSink eventSink)
    37	    {
    38	        _content = content;
    39	        _supplyRepository = supplyRepository;
    40	        _productionRepository = productionRepository;
    41	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository;
    42	        _inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository;
    43	        _jobRepository = jobRepository;
    44	        _eventSink = eventSink;
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    public Result Handle(CreateBuildingSupplyJobCommand command)
    48	    {
    49	        if (command is null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    50	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
    51	        BuildingSnapshot? building = buildings.Get(command.BuildingId);
    52	        if (building is null || building.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
    53	            || !_content.ContainsWorkstation(building.Definition.Id))
    54	        {
    55	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.WorkstationMismatch);
    56	        }
    57	
    58	        BuildingSupplyState supply = _supplyRepository.Get();
    59	        ProductionState production = _productionRepository.Get();
    60	        InventoryState inventory = _inventoryRepository.Get();
    61	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
    62	        supply.Register(
    63	            building.Id,
    64	            _content.GetWorkstation(building.Definition.Id),
    65	            command.Tick);
    66	        InventorySnapshot inventorySnapshot = inventory.CreateSnapshot();
    67	        BuildingSupplySnapshot snapshot = supply.Get(building.Id, inventorySnapshot)!;
    68	        ResidentInventoryLayoutSnapshot layout =
    69	            inventory.GetResidentInventoryLayout(command.ResidentId);
    70	        int freeSlots = layout.Slots.Count(value => value.IsEmpty);
    71	        BuildingSupplyPlan plan = BuildingSupplyPlanner.Plan(
    72	            snapshot,
    73	            inventory.GetAvailableWorldStacks(),
    74	            command.RevealedCells,
    75	            command.ReachableCells,
    76	            building.WorkPosition,
    77	            freeSlots,
    78	            production.HasActiveOrder(building.Id));
    79	        if (plan.Allocations.Count == 0)
    80	        {
    81	            return Result.Failure(InventoryErrors.InsufficientAvailableQuantity);
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        ItemReservationAllocation[] allocations = plan.Allocations
    85	            .Select(value => new ItemReservationAllocation(
    86	                value.SourceStackId,
    87	                value.ItemId,
    88	                value.Quantity))
    89	            .ToArray();
    90	        Result reserved = ReserveSources(inventory, command.JobId, allocations, command.Tick);
    91	        if (reserved.IsFailure)
    92	        {
    93	            return reserved;
    94	        }
    95	
    96	        ItemConsumptionRequest[] requests = allocations
    97	            .GroupBy(value => value.ItemId)
    98	            .Select(group => new ItemConsumptionRequest(
    99	                group.Key,
   100	                group.Sum(value => value.Quantity)))
   101	            .ToArray();
   102	        Result<IReadOnlyList<ResidentInventorySlotClaimSnapshot>> slotClaims =
   103	            inventory.ReserveResidentBatchSlotCapacity(
   104	                command.JobId,
   105	                command.ResidentId,
   106	                requests,
   107	                command.Tick);
   108	        if (slotClaims.IsFailure)
   109	        {
   110	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   111	            return Result.Failure(slotClaims.Error!);
   112	        }
   113	
   114	        Dictionary<ItemId, int> current = snapshot.Stocks.ToDictionary(
   115	            value => value.ItemId,
   116	            value => value.Current);
   117	        Result incoming = supply.ReserveIncoming(
   118	            building.Id,
   119	            command.JobId,
   120	            requests,
   121	            current,
   122	            command.Tick);
   123	        if (incoming.IsFailure)
   124	        {
   125	            Rollback(inventory, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   126	            return incoming;
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        int requiredTransitIds = slotClaims.Value.Count(value =>
   130	            layout.Slots.First(slot => slot.Slot == value.Slot).IsEmpty);
   131	        if (command.TransitStackIds.Count < requiredTransitIds)
   132	        {
   133	            Rollback(inventory, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   134	            supply.ReleaseSupply(building.Id, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   135	            return Result.Failure(InventoryErrors.SplitIdRequired);
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        int requiredDepositIds = requests.Length;
   139	        if (command.DepositStackIds.Count < requiredDepositIds)
   140	        {
   141	            Rollback(inventory, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   142	            supply.ReleaseSupply(building.Id, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   143	            return Result.Failure(InventoryErrors.SplitIdRequired);
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition definition = new BuildingSupplyJobDefinition(
   147	            command.JobId,
   148	            building.Id,
   149	            building.WorkPosition,
   150	            allocations,
   151	            command.TransitStackIds.Take(requiredTransitIds),
   152	            command.DepositStackIds.Take(requiredDepositIds),
   153	            command.Priority,
   154	            command.Tick,
   155	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   156	        Result created = jobs.Add(definition);
   157	        if (created.IsSuccess) created = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   158	        if (created.IsSuccess) created = jobs.Claim(command.JobId, command.ResidentId, command.Tick);
   159	        if (created.IsFailure)
   160	        {
   161	            Rollback(inventory, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   162	            supply.ReleaseSupply(building.Id, command.JobId, command.Tick);
   163	            return created;
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        Save(supply, inventory, jobs);
   167	        return Result.Success();
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    private static Result ReserveSources(
   171	        InventoryState inventory,
   172	        EntityId jobId,
   173	        IEnumerable<ItemReservationAllocation> allocations,
   174	        long tick)
   175	    {
   176	        foreach (ItemReservationAllocation allocation in allocations)
   177	        {
   178	            Result reserved = inventory.ReserveQuantity(
   179	                allocation.StackId,
   180	                jobId,
   181	                allocation.Quantity,
   182	                tick);
   183	            if (reserved.IsFailure)
   184	            {
   185	                inventory.ReleaseReservations(jobId, tick);
   186	                return reserved;
   187	            }
   188	        }
   189	
   190	        return Result.Success();
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    private static void Rollback(InventoryState inventory, EntityId jobId, long tick)
   194	    {
   195	        inventory.ReleaseReservations(jobId, tick);
   196	        inventory.ReleaseResidentSlotClaims(jobId, tick);
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    private void Save(BuildingSupplyState supply, InventoryState inventory, JobSystem jobs)
   200	    {
   201	        _supplyRepository.Save(supply);
   202	        _inventoryRepository.Save(inventory);
   203	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   204	        _eventSink.Append(inventory.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   205	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   206	    }
   207	}
   208	
   209	public sealed class SetBuildingStockDeliveryHandler
   210	    : ICommandHandler<SetBuildingStockDeliveryCommand, Result>
   211	{
   212	    private readonly IBuildingSupplyRepository _repository;
   213	
   214	    public SetBuildingStockDeliveryHandler(IBuildingSupplyRepository repository)
   215	    {
   216	        _repository = repository;
   217	    }
   218	
   219	    public Result Handle(SetBuildingStockDeliveryCommand command)
   220	    {
   221	        BuildingSupplyState supply = _repository.Get();
   222	        Result result = supply.SetDeliveryEnabled(
   223	            command.BuildingId,
   224	            command.ItemId,
   225	            command.Enabled,
   226	            command.Tick);
   227	        if (result.IsSuccess) _repository.Save(supply);
   228	        return result;
   229	    }
   230	}
   231	
   232	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Production/ProductionPreparationUseCases.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Content;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    12	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    13	using Dig.Domain.Production;
    14	using Dig.Domain.Technology;
    15	
    16	namespace Dig.Application.Production
    17	{
    18	
    19	public sealed class EnqueueProductionOrderHandler
    20	    : ICommandHandler<EnqueueProductionOrderCommand, Result>
    21	{
    22	    private readonly ProductionContentCatalog _content;
    23	    private readonly IProductionRepository _repository;
    24	
    25	    public EnqueueProductionOrderHandler(
    26	        ProductionContentCatalog content,
    27	        IProductionRepository repository)
    28	    {
    29	        _content = content ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(content));
    30	        _repository = repository ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(repository));
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    public Result Handle(EnqueueProductionOrderCommand command)
    34	    {
    35	        if (command is null)
    36	        {
    37	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    38	        }
    39	
    40	        ProductionState production = _repository.Get();
    41	        Result result = production.Enqueue(
    42	            command.OrderId,
    43	            _content.GetRecipe(command.RecipeId),
    44	            command.BuildingId,
    45	            command.Tick);
    46	        if (result.IsSuccess)
    47	        {
    48	            _repository.Save(production);
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        return result;
    52	    }
    53	}
    54	
    55	public sealed class PrepareProductionOrderHandler
    56	    : ICommandHandler<PrepareProductionOrderCommand, Result>
    57	{
    58	    private readonly IProductionRepository _productionRepository;
    59	    private readonly ITechnologyRepository _technologyRepository;
    60	    private readonly IBuildingsRepository _buildingsRepository;
    61	    private readonly IInventoryRepository _inventoryRepository;
    62	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobRepository;
    63	    private readonly IEnergyAvailability _energy;
    64	    private readonly IEventSink _eventSink;
    65	
    66	    public PrepareProductionOrderHandler(
    67	        IProductionRepository productionRepository,
    68	        ITechnologyRepository technologyRepository,
    69	        IBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository,
    70	        IInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    71	        IJobRepository jobRepository,
    72	        IEnergyAvailability energy,
    73	        IEventSink eventSink)
    74	    {
    75	        _productionRepository = productionRepository;
    76	        _technologyRepository = technologyRepository;
    77	        _buildingsRepository = buildingsRepository;
    78	        _inventoryRepository = inventoryRepository;
    79	        _jobRepository = jobRepository;
    80	        _energy = energy;
    81	        _eventSink = eventSink;
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    public Result Handle(PrepareProductionOrderCommand command)
    85	    {
    86	        if (command is null)
    87	        {
    88	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        ProductionState production = _productionRepository.Get();
    92	        TechnologyState technology = _technologyRepository.Get();
    93	        BuildingsState buildings = _buildingsRepository.Get();
    94	        InventoryState inventory = _inventoryRepository.Get();
    95	        JobSystem jobs = _jobRepository.Get();
    96	        ProductionOrderSnapshot? order = production.GetNextQueued(command.BuildingId);
    97	        if (order is null)
    98	        {
    99	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.QueueBlocked);
   100	        }
   101	
   102	        BuildingSnapshot? building = buildings.Get(command.BuildingId);
   103	        Result validation = ValidatePrerequisites(
   104	            order,
   105	            building,
   106	            technology,
   107	            inventory,
   108	            command.ReachableCells);
   109	        if (validation.IsFailure)
   110	        {
   111	            return validation;
   112	        }
   113	
   114	        ItemLocation workstationInventory = ItemLocation.InBuilding(command.BuildingId);
   115	        ItemConsumptionRequest[] inputs = order.Recipe.Inputs
   116	            .Select(value => new ItemConsumptionRequest(value.ItemId, value.Quantity))
   117	            .ToArray();
   118	        Result<IReadOnlyList<ItemReservationAllocation>> reserved =
   119	            inventory.ReserveProductionInputs(
   120	                order.Id,
   121	                workstationInventory,
   122	                inputs,
   123	                command.Tick);
   124	        if (reserved.IsFailure)
   125	        {
   126	            return Result.Failure(reserved.Error!);
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        ProductionWorkJobDefinition job = new ProductionWorkJobDefinition(
   130	            command.JobId,
   131	            order.Id,
   132	            command.BuildingId,
   133	            order.Recipe.Id,
   134	            building!.WorkPosition,
   135	            command.Priority,
   136	            command.Tick,
   137	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   138	        Result jobAdded = jobs.Add(job);
   139	        if (jobAdded.IsSuccess)
   140	        {
   141	            jobAdded = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
   142	        }
   143	
   144	        if (jobAdded.IsFailure)
   145	        {
   146	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(order.Id, command.Tick);
   147	            SaveAndPublish(production, inventory, jobs);
   148	            return jobAdded;
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        Result orderReserved = production.ReserveInputs(
   152	            order.Id,
   153	            reserved.Value,
   154	            command.Tick);
   155	        if (orderReserved.IsFailure)
   156	        {
   157	            inventory.ReleaseReservations(order.Id, command.Tick);
   158	            jobs.Cancel(
   159	                command.JobId,
   160	                new JobBlockReason("production_queue_changed", orderReserved.Error!.Message),
   161	                command.Tick);
   162	            SaveAndPublish(production, inventory, jobs);
   163	            return orderReserved;
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        SaveAndPublish(production, inventory, jobs);
   167	        return Result.Success();
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    private Result ValidatePrerequisites(
   171	        ProductionOrderSnapshot order,
   172	        BuildingSnapshot? building,
   173	        TechnologyState technology,
   174	        InventoryState inventory,
   175	        IReadOnlyCollection<Dig.Domain.World.CellId> reachableCells)
   176	    {
   177	        if (building is null
   178	            || building.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   179	            || building.Definition.Id != order.Recipe.WorkstationId)
   180	        {
   181	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.WorkstationMismatch);
   182	        }
   183	
   184	        if (!technology.IsRecipeUnlocked(order.Recipe))
   185	        {
   186	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.TechnologyLocked);
   187	        }
   188	
   189	        if (!reachableCells.Contains(building.WorkPosition))
   190	        {
   191	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.WorkPositionUnavailable);
   192	        }
   193	
   194	        if (!_energy.CanSupply(building.Id, order.Recipe.EnergyPerWorkTick))
   195	        {
   196	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.EnergyUnavailable);
   197	        }
   198	
   199	        if (order.Recipe.RequiredToolItemId.HasValue
   200	            && inventory.GetAvailableQuantityAt(
   201	                order.Recipe.RequiredToolItemId.Value,
   202	                ItemLocation.InBuilding(building.Id)) < 1)
   203	        {
   204	            return Result.Failure(ProductionErrors.ToolUnavailable);
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return Result.Success();
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    private void SaveAndPublish(
   211	        ProductionState production,
   212	        InventoryState inventory,
   213	        JobSystem jobs)
   214	    {
   215	        _productionRepository.Save(production);
   216	        _inventoryRepository.Save(inventory);
   217	        _jobRepository.Save(jobs);
   218	        _eventSink.Append(production.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   219	        _eventSink.Append(inventory.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   220	        _eventSink.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   221	    }
   222	}
   223	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/BarrelAttackJobSaveCodec.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Globalization;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class BarrelAttackJobSaveCodec : IJobDefinitionSaveCodec
    13	{
    14	    public const string StableTypeId = "job.barrel_attack.v1";
    15	    public string TypeId => StableTypeId;
    16	    public bool CanEncode(JobDefinition definition) => definition is BarrelAttackJobDefinition;
    17	
    18	    public JobDefinitionSaveData Encode(JobDefinition definition)
    19	    {
    20	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition job = definition as BarrelAttackJobDefinition
    21	            ?? throw new ArgumentException("Expected a barrel attack job.", nameof(definition));
    22	        return new JobDefinitionSaveData
    23	        {
    24	            TypeId = TypeId,
    25	            JobId = job.Id.ToString(),
    26	            Priority = job.Priority,
    27	            CreatedTick = job.CreatedTick,
    28	            MaximumRetries = job.RetryPolicy.MaximumRetries,
    29	            RetryDelayTicks = job.RetryPolicy.RetryDelayTicks,
    30	            Dependencies = job.Dependencies.Select(value => value.ToString()).ToList(),
    31	            Properties = new List<SavePropertyData>
    32	            {
    33	                Property("barrel.id", job.BarrelId.ToString()),
    34	                Property("target.x", job.TargetCell.X),
    35	                Property("target.y", job.TargetCell.Y),
    36	                Property("target.z", job.TargetCell.Z),
    37	                Property("work.x", job.WorkPosition.X),
    38	                Property("work.y", job.WorkPosition.Y),
    39	                Property("work.z", job.WorkPosition.Z),
    40	                Property("barrel.version", job.BarrelVersion),
    41	                Property("contents.generation", job.ContentsGeneration),
    42	            },
    43	        };
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    public JobDefinition Decode(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    47	    {
    48	        Dictionary<string, string> properties = data.Properties.ToDictionary(
    49	            item => item.Key,
    50	            item => item.Value,
    51	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
    52	        return new BarrelAttackJobDefinition(
    53	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    54	            EntityId.Parse(Get(properties, "barrel.id")),
    55	            Cell(properties, "target"),
    56	            Cell(properties, "work"),
    57	            ParseLong(properties, "barrel.version"),
    58	            ParseLong(properties, "contents.generation"),
    59	            data.Priority,
    60	            data.CreatedTick,
    61	            new JobRetryPolicy(data.MaximumRetries, data.RetryDelayTicks),
    62	            data.Dependencies.Select(EntityId.Parse));
    63	    }
    64	
    65	    private static CellId Cell(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string prefix) =>
    66	        new CellId(
    67	            ParseInt(values, prefix + ".x"),
    68	            ParseInt(values, prefix + ".y"),
    69	            ParseInt(values, prefix + ".z"));
    70	
    71	    private static SavePropertyData Property(string key, object value) =>
    72	        new SavePropertyData
    73	        {
    74	            Key = key,
    75	            Value = Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? string.Empty,
    76	        };
    77	
    78	    private static string Get(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key)
    79	    {
    80	        if (!values.TryGetValue(key, out string? value) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
    81	        {
    82	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Saved barrel job property '{key}' is invalid.");
    83	        }
    84	
    85	        return value;
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    private static int ParseInt(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key) =>
    89	        int.Parse(Get(values, key), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    90	
    91	    private static long ParseLong(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key) =>
    92	        long.Parse(Get(values, key), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
    93	}
    94	
    95	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Globalization;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec : IJobDefinitionSaveCodec
    13	{
    14	    public string TypeId => "job.building_box_assembly.v1";
    15	
    16	    public bool CanEncode(JobDefinition definition)
    17	    {
    18	        return definition is BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition;
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    public JobDefinitionSaveData Encode(JobDefinition definition)
    22	    {
    23	        BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition box =
    24	            (BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition)definition;
    25	        return new JobDefinitionSaveData
    26	        {
    27	            JobId = box.Id.ToString(),
    28	            Priority = box.Priority,
    29	            CreatedTick = box.CreatedTick,
    30	            MaximumRetries = box.RetryPolicy.MaximumRetries,
    31	            RetryDelayTicks = box.RetryPolicy.RetryDelayTicks,
    32	            Dependencies = box.Dependencies
    33	                .Select(value => value.ToString())
    34	                .ToList(),
    35	            Properties = new List<SavePropertyData>
    36	            {
    37	                Property("building_id", box.BuildingId.ToString()),
    38	                Property("source_stack_id", box.SourceStackId.ToString()),
    39	                Property("site_x", box.SiteCell.X),
    40	                Property("site_y", box.SiteCell.Y),
    41	                Property("site_z", box.SiteCell.Z),
    42	                Property("work_x", box.WorkPosition.X),
    43	                Property("work_y", box.WorkPosition.Y),
    44	                Property("work_z", box.WorkPosition.Z),
    45	            },
    46	        };
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    public JobDefinition Decode(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    50	    {
    51	        Dictionary<string, string> properties = data.Properties
    52	            .ToDictionary(value => value.Key, value => value.Value, StringComparer.Ordinal);
    53	        return new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition(
    54	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    55	            EntityId.Parse(Required(properties, "building_id")),
    56	            EntityId.Parse(Required(properties, "source_stack_id")),
    57	            new CellId(
    58	                Integer(properties, "site_x"),
    59	                Integer(properties, "site_y"),
    60	                Integer(properties, "site_z")),
    61	            new CellId(
    62	                Integer(properties, "work_x"),
    63	                Integer(properties, "work_y"),
    64	                Integer(properties, "work_z")),
    65	            data.Priority,
    66	            data.CreatedTick,
    67	            new JobRetryPolicy(data.MaximumRetries, data.RetryDelayTicks),
    68	            data.Dependencies.Select(EntityId.Parse));
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    private static SavePropertyData Property(string key, object value)
    72	    {
    73	        return new SavePropertyData
    74	        {
    75	            Key = key,
    76	            Value = Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
    77	                ?? string.Empty,
    78	        };
    79	    }
    80	
    81	    private static string Required(
    82	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> properties,
    83	        string key)
    84	    {
    85	        if (!properties.TryGetValue(key, out string? value)
    86	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
    87	        {
    88	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
    89	                $"Saved BuildingBox job is missing property '{key}'.");
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        return value;
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    private static int Integer(
    96	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> properties,
    97	        string key)
    98	    {
    99	        string value = Required(properties, key);
   100	        if (!int.TryParse(
   101	            value,
   102	            NumberStyles.Integer,
   103	            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
   104	            out int parsed))
   105	        {
   106	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   107	                $"Saved BuildingBox job property '{key}' is not an integer.");
   108	        }
   109	
   110	        return parsed;
   111	    }
   112	}
   113	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/BuildingBoxPackingJobSaveCodec.cs =====

    12	public sealed class BuildingBoxPackingJobSaveCodec : IJobDefinitionSaveCodec
    13	{
    14	    public string TypeId => "job.building_box_packing.v1";
    15	
    16	    public bool CanEncode(JobDefinition definition)
    17	    {
    18	        return definition is BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition;
    19	    }

    31	            RetryDelayTicks = packing.RetryPolicy.RetryDelayTicks,
    32	            Dependencies = packing.Dependencies
    33	                .Select(value => value.ToString())
    34	                .ToList(),

    36	            {
    37	                Property("building_id", packing.BuildingId.ToString()),
    38	                Property("output_stack_id", packing.OutputStackId.ToString()),
    39	                Property("work_x", packing.WorkPosition.X),
    40	                Property("work_y", packing.WorkPosition.Y),
    41	                Property("work_z", packing.WorkPosition.Z),
    42	            },
    43	        };
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    public JobDefinition Decode(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    47	    {
    48	        Dictionary<string, string> properties = data.Properties
    49	            .ToDictionary(value => value.Key, value => value.Value, StringComparer.Ordinal);
    50	        return new BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition(
    51	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    52	            EntityId.Parse(Required(properties, "building_id")),
    53	            EntityId.Parse(Required(properties, "output_stack_id")),
    54	            new CellId(
    55	                Integer(properties, "work_x"),
    56	                Integer(properties, "work_y"),
    57	                Integer(properties, "work_z")),
    58	            data.Priority,
    59	            data.CreatedTick,
    60	            new JobRetryPolicy(data.MaximumRetries, data.RetryDelayTicks),
    61	            data.Dependencies.Select(EntityId.Parse));
    62	    }
    63

    65	    {
    66	        return new SavePropertyData
    67	        {
    68	            Key = key,
    69	            Value = Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
    70	                ?? string.Empty,
    71	        };
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    private static string Required(
    75	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> properties,
    76	        string key)
    77	    {
    78	        if (!properties.TryGetValue(key, out string? value)
    79	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
    80	        {
    81	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
    82	                $"Saved BuildingBox packing job is missing property '{key}'.");
    83	        }
    84	
    85	        return value;
    86	    }
    87

    89	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> properties,
    90	        string key)
    91	    {

    93	        if (!int.TryParse(
    94	            value,
    95	            NumberStyles.Integer,
    96	            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
    97	            out int parsed))
    98	        {
    99	            throw new InvalidOperationException(

===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/BuildingSupplyJobSaveCodec.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Globalization;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class BuildingSupplyJobSaveCodec : IJobDefinitionSaveCodec
    14	{
    15	    public const string StableTypeId = "job.building_supply.v1";
    16	
    17	    public string TypeId => StableTypeId;
    18	
    19	    public bool CanEncode(JobDefinition definition) =>
    20	        definition is BuildingSupplyJobDefinition;
    21	
    22	    public JobDefinitionSaveData Encode(JobDefinition definition)
    23	    {
    24	        BuildingSupplyJobDefinition job = definition as BuildingSupplyJobDefinition
    25	            ?? throw new ArgumentException("Expected a building supply job.", nameof(definition));
    26	        List<SavePropertyData> properties = new List<SavePropertyData>
    27	        {
    28	            Property("building.id", job.BuildingId),
    29	            Property("work.x", job.WorkPosition.X),
    30	            Property("work.y", job.WorkPosition.Y),
    31	            Property("work.z", job.WorkPosition.Z),
    32	            Property("allocation.count", job.Allocations.Count),
    33	            Property("transit.count", job.TransitStackIds.Count),
    34	            Property("deposit.count", job.DepositStackIds.Count),
    35	        };
    36	        for (int index = 0; index < job.Allocations.Count; index++)
    37	        {
    38	            ItemReservationAllocation allocation = job.Allocations[index];
    39	            properties.Add(Property($"allocation.{index}.stack", allocation.StackId));
    40	            properties.Add(Property($"allocation.{index}.item", allocation.ItemId));
    41	            properties.Add(Property($"allocation.{index}.quantity", allocation.Quantity));
    42	        }
    43	
    44	        AddIds(properties, "transit", job.TransitStackIds);
    45	        AddIds(properties, "deposit", job.DepositStackIds);
    46	        return new JobDefinitionSaveData
    47	        {
    48	            TypeId = TypeId,
    49	            JobId = job.Id.ToString(),
    50	            Priority = job.Priority,
    51	            CreatedTick = job.CreatedTick,
    52	            MaximumRetries = job.RetryPolicy.MaximumRetries,
    53	            RetryDelayTicks = job.RetryPolicy.RetryDelayTicks,
    54	            Dependencies = job.Dependencies.Select(value => value.ToString()).ToList(),
    55	            Properties = properties,
    56	        };
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    public JobDefinition Decode(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    60	    {
    61	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values = data.Properties.ToDictionary(
    62	            value => value.Key,
    63	            value => value.Value,
    64	            StringComparer.Ordinal);
    65	        int allocationCount = ParseInt(values, "allocation.count");
    66	        ItemReservationAllocation[] allocations = Enumerable.Range(0, allocationCount)
    67	            .Select(index => new ItemReservationAllocation(
    68	                EntityId.Parse(Get(values, $"allocation.{index}.stack")),
    69	                new ItemId(Get(values, $"allocation.{index}.item")),
    70	                ParseInt(values, $"allocation.{index}.quantity")))
    71	            .ToArray();
    72	        return new BuildingSupplyJobDefinition(
    73	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    74	            EntityId.Parse(Get(values, "building.id")),
    75	            new CellId(
    76	                ParseInt(values, "work.x"),
    77	                ParseInt(values, "work.y"),
    78	                ParseInt(values, "work.z")),
    79	            allocations,
    80	            ParseIds(values, "transit"),
    81	            ParseIds(values, "deposit"),
    82	            data.Priority,
    83	            data.CreatedTick,
    84	            new JobRetryPolicy(data.MaximumRetries, data.RetryDelayTicks));
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    private static void AddIds(
    88	        ICollection<SavePropertyData> values,
    89	        string prefix,
    90	        IReadOnlyList<EntityId> ids)
    91	    {
    92	        for (int index = 0; index < ids.Count; index++)
    93	        {
    94	            values.Add(Property($"{prefix}.{index}", ids[index]));
    95	        }
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    private static EntityId[] ParseIds(
    99	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values,
   100	        string prefix)
   101	    {
   102	        int count = ParseInt(values, $"{prefix}.count");
   103	        return Enumerable.Range(0, count)
   104	            .Select(index => EntityId.Parse(Get(values, $"{prefix}.{index}")))
   105	            .ToArray();
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    private static SavePropertyData Property(string key, object value)
   109	    {
   110	        return new SavePropertyData
   111	        {
   112	            Key = key,
   113	            Value = Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? string.Empty,
   114	        };
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    private static string Get(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key)
   118	    {
   119	        if (!values.TryGetValue(key, out string? value) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
   120	        {
   121	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Missing building supply property '{key}'.");
   122	        }
   123	
   124	        return value;
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    private static int ParseInt(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key)
   128	    {
   129	        if (!int.TryParse(Get(values, key), NumberStyles.Integer,
   130	            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out int value) || value < 0)
   131	        {
   132	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Invalid building supply property '{key}'.");
   133	        }
   134	
   135	        return value;
   136	    }
   137	}
   138	
   139	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/BuildingWorkJobSaveCodec.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
     7	{
     8	
     9	public sealed class BuildingWorkJobSaveCodec : IJobDefinitionSaveCodec
    10	{
    11	    public const string StableTypeId = "job.building_work.v1";
    12	
    13	    public string TypeId => StableTypeId;
    14	
    15	    public bool CanEncode(JobDefinition definition) =>
    16	        definition is BuildingWorkJobDefinition;
    17	
    18	    public JobDefinitionSaveData Encode(JobDefinition definition)
    19	    {
    20	        BuildingWorkJobDefinition job = definition as BuildingWorkJobDefinition
    21	            ?? throw new ArgumentException(
    22	                "Expected a building work job.",
    23	                nameof(definition));
    24	        return JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Create(job, new[]
    25	        {
    26	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Property("building.id", job.BuildingId),
    27	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Property("work.kind", (int)job.Kind),
    28	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Property("work.x", job.WorkPosition.X),
    29	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Property("work.y", job.WorkPosition.Y),
    30	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Property("work.z", job.WorkPosition.Z),
    31	        });
    32	    }
    33	
    34	    public JobDefinition Decode(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    35	    {
    36	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values =
    37	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Read(data);
    38	        int kindValue = JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Integer(values, "work.kind");
    39	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingWorkKind), kindValue))
    40	        {
    41	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Saved building work kind is invalid.");
    42	        }
    43	
    44	        return new BuildingWorkJobDefinition(
    45	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    46	            EntityId.Parse(JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Required(values, "building.id")),
    47	            (BuildingWorkKind)kindValue,
    48	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Cell(values, "work"),
    49	            data.Priority,
    50	            data.CreatedTick,
    51	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.RetryPolicy(data),
    52	            JobDefinitionSaveCodecValues.Dependencies(data));
    53	    }
    54	}
    55	
    56	}

    73	
    74	    private static SavePropertyData Property(string key, object value)
    75	    {

    80	        };

    84	    {

    96	        {
    97	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Saved mushroom job property '{key}' is invalid.");
    98	        }

   100	        return value;

   103	    private static long ParseLong(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> properties, string key)
   104	    {
   105	        if (!long.TryParse(Get(properties, key), NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out long value))
   106	        {
   107	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Saved mushroom job property '{key}' is invalid.");
   108	        }
   109	
   110	        return value;
   111	    }
   112	}
   113	
   114	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/ProductionWorkJobSaveCodec.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Globalization;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class ProductionWorkJobSaveCodec : IJobDefinitionSaveCodec
    14	{
    15	    public const string StableTypeId = "job.production_work.v1";
    16	
    17	    public string TypeId => StableTypeId;
    18	
    19	    public bool CanEncode(JobDefinition definition) =>
    20	        definition is ProductionWorkJobDefinition;
    21	
    22	    public JobDefinitionSaveData Encode(JobDefinition definition)
    23	    {
    24	        ProductionWorkJobDefinition job = definition as ProductionWorkJobDefinition
    25	            ?? throw new ArgumentException("Expected a production job.", nameof(definition));
    26	        return new JobDefinitionSaveData
    27	        {
    28	            TypeId = TypeId,
    29	            JobId = job.Id.ToString(),
    30	            Priority = job.Priority,
    31	            CreatedTick = job.CreatedTick,
    32	            MaximumRetries = job.RetryPolicy.MaximumRetries,
    33	            RetryDelayTicks = job.RetryPolicy.RetryDelayTicks,
    34	            Dependencies = job.Dependencies.Select(value => value.ToString()).ToList(),
    35	            Properties = new List<SavePropertyData>
    36	            {
    37	                Property("order.id", job.OrderId),
    38	                Property("building.id", job.BuildingId),
    39	                Property("recipe.id", job.RecipeId),
    40	                Property("work.x", job.WorkPosition.X),
    41	                Property("work.y", job.WorkPosition.Y),
    42	                Property("work.z", job.WorkPosition.Z),
    43	            },
    44	        };
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    public JobDefinition Decode(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    48	    {
    49	        IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values = Values(data);
    50	        return new ProductionWorkJobDefinition(
    51	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    52	            EntityId.Parse(Get(values, "order.id")),
    53	            EntityId.Parse(Get(values, "building.id")),
    54	            new RecipeId(Get(values, "recipe.id")),
    55	            new CellId(
    56	                ParseInt(values, "work.x"),
    57	                ParseInt(values, "work.y"),
    58	                ParseInt(values, "work.z")),
    59	            data.Priority,
    60	            data.CreatedTick,
    61	            new JobRetryPolicy(data.MaximumRetries, data.RetryDelayTicks));
    62	    }
    63	
    64	    private static IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> Values(JobDefinitionSaveData data)
    65	    {
    66	        return data.Properties.ToDictionary(value => value.Key, value => value.Value);
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    private static SavePropertyData Property(string key, object value)
    70	    {
    71	        return new SavePropertyData
    72	        {
    73	            Key = key,
    74	            Value = Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? string.Empty,
    75	        };
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    private static string Get(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key)
    79	    {
    80	        if (!values.TryGetValue(key, out string? value) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
    81	        {
    82	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Missing production job property '{key}'.");
    83	        }
    84	
    85	        return value;
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    private static int ParseInt(IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string> values, string key)
    89	    {
    90	        if (!int.TryParse(Get(values, key), NumberStyles.Integer,
    91	            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out int value))
    92	        {
    93	            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Invalid production job property '{key}'.");
    94	        }
    95	
    96	        return value;
    97	    }
    98	}
    99	
   100	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/SaveGameBuilder.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    11	{

    13	public sealed partial class SaveGameBuilder
    14	{
    15	    private readonly JobDefinitionSaveRegistry _jobDefinitions;
    16	
    17	    public SaveGameBuilder(JobDefinitionSaveRegistry jobDefinitions)
    18	    {
    19	        _jobDefinitions = jobDefinitions
    20	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobDefinitions));
    21	    }
    22	
    23	    public SaveGameDocument Build(SaveGameContext context)
    24	    {
    25	        if (context is null)
    26	        {
    27	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(context));
    28	        }
    29	
    30	        Result<SaveGameDocument> result = Build(
    31	            context,
    32	            context.MiningOutputCommits);
    33	        if (result.IsFailure)
    34	        {
    35	            throw new InvalidOperationException(result.Error!.ToString());
    36	        }
    37	
    38	        return result.Value;
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    private SaveGameDocument BuildCore(SaveGameContext context)
    42	    {
    43	        ValidateMetadata(context.Metadata);
    44	        return new SaveGameDocument
    45	        {
    46	            FormatVersion = SaveFormat.CurrentVersion,
    47	            Metadata = CopyMetadata(context.Metadata),
    48	            World = BuildWorld(context.World.CreateSnapshot()),
    49	            Inventory = BuildInventory(context.Inventory.CreateSnapshot()),
    50	            Jobs = BuildJobs(context.Jobs),
    51	            Buildings = BuildBuildings(context.Buildings),
    52	            AgentSkills = BuildAgentSkills(context.Agents),
    53	            AgentPositions = BuildAgentPositions(context.Agents),
    54	            AgentRuntime = BuildAgentRuntime(context.Agents),
    55	            TerrainDeposits = BuildTerrainDeposits(context.TerrainDeposits),
    56	            PackableBuildingExecutions = PackableBuildingExecutionSaveDataAdapter.Encode(
    57	                context.PackableBuildingExecutions),
    58	            Mushrooms = BuildMushrooms(context.Mushrooms),
    59	            BuildingProduction = BuildingProductionSaveAdapter.Encode(
    60	                context.Production,
    61	                context.BuildingSupply,
    62	                context.Inventory),
    63	            Barrels = BuildBarrels(context.Barrels),
    64	            Combat = CombatSaveAdapter.Encode(context.Combat),
    65	        };
    66	    }

    68	    private static WorldSaveData BuildWorld(WorldSnapshot snapshot)
    69	    {
    70	        WorldSaveData data = new WorldSaveData
    71	        {
    72	            Width = snapshot.Size.Width,
    73	            Height = snapshot.Size.Height,
    74	            Depth = snapshot.Size.Depth,
    75	            ChunkSize = snapshot.ChunkSize,
    76	            Version = snapshot.Version,
    77	        };
    78	        foreach (ChunkSnapshot chunk in snapshot.Chunks.OrderBy(item => item.Id))
    79	        {
    80	            WorldChunkSaveData savedChunk = new WorldChunkSaveData
    81	            {
    82	                X = chunk.Id.X,
    83	                Y = chunk.Id.Y,
    84	                Z = chunk.Id.Z,
    85	                Version = chunk.ChunkVersion,
    86	            };
    87	            foreach (CellSnapshot cell in chunk.Cells.OrderBy(item => item.Id))
    88	            {
    89	                savedChunk.Cells.Add(new WorldCellSaveData
    90	                {
    91	                    X = cell.Id.X,
    92	                    Y = cell.Id.Y,
    93	                    Z = cell.Id.Z,
    94	                    MaterialId = cell.State.MaterialId.ToString(),
    95	                    Designation = (int)cell.State.Designation,
    96	                    IsExplored = cell.State.IsExplored,
    97	                    Damage = cell.State.Damage,
    98	                    Temperature = cell.State.Temperature,
    99	                    CompletedExcavationQuarters =
   100	                        (int)cell.State.CompletedExcavationQuarters,
   101	                    ExcavationCutPattern = (int)cell.State.ExcavationCutPattern,
   102	                    ExcavationSourceMaterialId =
   103	                        cell.State.ExcavationSourceMaterialId.ToString(),
   104	                });
   105	            }
   106	
   107	            data.Chunks.Add(savedChunk);
   108	        }
   109	
   110	        return data;
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    private static InventorySaveData BuildInventory(InventorySnapshot snapshot)
   114	    {
   115	        InventorySaveData data = new InventorySaveData
   116	        {
   117	            Version = snapshot.Version,
   118	        };
   119	        foreach (ItemStackSnapshot stack in snapshot.Stacks
   120	            .OrderBy(item => item.StackId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   121	        {
   122	            ItemStackSaveData saved = new ItemStackSaveData
   123	            {
   124	                StackId = stack.StackId.ToString(),
   125	                ItemId = stack.ItemId.ToString(),
   126	                Quantity = stack.Quantity,
   127	                Location = BuildLocation(stack.Location),
   128	            };
   129	            foreach (ItemQuantityReservationSnapshot reservation in stack.Reservations
   130	                .OrderBy(item => item.JobId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   131	            {
   132	                saved.Reservations.Add(new ItemReservationSaveData
   133	                {
   134	                    JobId = reservation.JobId.ToString(),
   135	                    Quantity = reservation.Quantity,
   136	                });
   137	            }
   138	
   139	            data.Stacks.Add(saved);
   140	        }
   141	
   142	        foreach (HeldItemReferenceSnapshot held in snapshot.HeldItems
   143	            .OrderBy(item => item.ResidentId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   144	        {
   145	            data.HeldItems.Add(new HeldItemReferenceSaveData
   146	            {
   147	                ResidentId = held.ResidentId.ToString(),
   148	                StackId = held.StackId.ToString(),
   149	                Quantity = held.Quantity,
   150	                Purpose = (int)held.Purpose,
   151	            });
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        foreach (ResidentInventorySlotClaimSnapshot claim in snapshot.ResidentSlotClaims
   155	            .OrderBy(item => item.JobId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
   156	            .ThenBy(item => item.Slot.Compartment)
   157	            .ThenBy(item => item.Slot.Index))
   158	        {
   159	            data.ResidentSlotClaims.Add(new ResidentSlotClaimSaveData
   160	            {
   161	                JobId = claim.JobId.ToString(),
   162	                ResidentId = claim.ResidentId.ToString(),
   163	                ItemId = claim.ItemId.ToString(),
   164	                Compartment = (int)claim.Slot.Compartment,
   165	                SlotIndex = claim.Slot.Index,
   166	                Quantity = claim.Quantity,
   167	            });
   168	        }
   169	
   170	        return data;
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    private JobsSaveData BuildJobs(JobSystem jobs)
   174	    {
   175	        JobsSaveData data = new JobsSaveData();
   176	        foreach (JobSnapshot job in jobs.GetAll()
   177	            .OrderBy(item => item.Id.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   178	        {
   179	            data.Jobs.Add(new JobSaveData
   180	            {
   181	                Definition = _jobDefinitions.Encode(job.Definition),
   182	                Status = (int)job.Status,
   183	                Stage = (int)job.Stage,
   184	                AssignedAgentId = job.AssignedAgentId?.ToString(),
   185	                RetryCount = job.RetryCount,
   186	                NextRetryTick = job.NextRetryTick,
   187	                Version = job.Version,
   188	                ReasonCode = job.Reason?.Code,
   189	                ReasonMessage = job.Reason?.Message,
   190	            });
   191	        }
   192	
   193	        foreach (ReservationSnapshot reservation in jobs.GetReservations()
   194	            .OrderBy(item => item.JobId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
   195	            .ThenBy(item => item.Key))
   196	        {
   197	            data.Reservations.Add(new JobReservationSaveData
   198	            {
   199	                JobId = reservation.JobId.ToString(),
   200	                AgentId = reservation.AgentId.ToString(),
   201	                Kind = (int)reservation.Key.Kind,
   202	                Value = reservation.Key.Value,
   203	                AcquiredTick = reservation.AcquiredTick,
   204	            });
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return data;
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    private static BuildingsSaveData BuildBuildings(BuildingsState buildings)
   211	    {
   212	        BuildingsSaveData data = new BuildingsSaveData();
   213	        foreach (BuildingSnapshot building in buildings.GetAll()
   214	            .OrderBy(item => item.Id.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   215	        {
   216	            BuildingSaveData saved = new BuildingSaveData
   217	            {
   218	                BuildingId = building.Id.ToString(),
   219	                DefinitionId = building.Definition.Id.ToString(),
   220	                OriginX = building.Origin.X,
   221	                OriginY = building.Origin.Y,
   222	                OriginZ = building.Origin.Z,
   223	                Orientation = (int)building.Orientation,
   224	                WorkPositionX = building.WorkPosition.X,
   225	                WorkPositionY = building.WorkPosition.Y,
   226	                WorkPositionZ = building.WorkPosition.Z,
   227	                Status = (int)building.Status,
   228	                CompletedWork = building.CompletedWork,
   229	                Durability = building.Durability,
   230	                Version = building.Version,
   231	                DiagnosticReason = building.DiagnosticReason,
   232	            };
   233	            if (building.BoxPlan is not null)
   234	            {
   235	                saved.BoxPlan = new BuildingBoxPlanSaveData
   236	                {
   237	                    SourceStackId = building.BoxPlan.SourceStackId.ToString(),
   238	                    JobId = building.BoxPlan.JobId.ToString(),
   239	                    CommitState = (int)building.BoxPlan.CommitState,
   240	                };
   241	            }
   242	
   243	            if (building.PackingPlan is not null)
   244	            {
   245	                saved.PackingPlan = new BuildingPackingPlanSaveData
   246	                {
   247	                    JobId = building.PackingPlan.JobId.ToString(),
   248	                    OutputStackId = building.PackingPlan.OutputStackId.ToString(),
   249	                    CompletedWork = building.PackingPlan.CompletedWork,
   250	                    CommitState = (int)building.PackingPlan.CommitState,
   251	                };
   252	            }
   253	
   254	            data.Buildings.Add(saved);
   255	        }
   256	
   257	        return data;
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    private static ItemLocationSaveData BuildLocation(ItemLocation location)
   261	    {
   262	        return new ItemLocationSaveData
   263	        {
   264	            Kind = (int)location.Kind,
   265	            OwnerId = location.HasOwner ? location.OwnerId.ToString() : null,
   266	            CellX = location.HasCell ? location.CellId.X : (int?)null,
   267	            CellY = location.HasCell ? location.CellId.Y : (int?)null,
   268	            CellZ = location.HasCell ? location.CellId.Z : (int?)null,
   269	            ResidentCompartment = location.HasResidentSlot
   270	                ? (int)location.ResidentCompartment
   271	                : (int?)null,
   272	            ResidentSlotIndex = location.HasResidentSlot
   273	                ? location.ResidentSlotIndex
   274	                : (int?)null,
   275	        };
   276	    }

   278	    private static AgentPositionsSaveData BuildAgentPositions(

   293	        }

   298	    private static TerrainDepositsSaveData BuildTerrainDeposits(
   299	        IEnumerable<TerrainDepositInstance> deposits)
   300	    {
   301	        TerrainDepositsSaveData data = new TerrainDepositsSaveData();
   302	        foreach (TerrainDepositInstance deposit in deposits
   303	            .OrderBy(value => value.Cell)
   304	            .ThenBy(value => value.InstanceId, StringComparer.Ordinal))
   305	        {
   306	            data.Deposits.Add(new TerrainDepositSaveData
   307	            {
   308	                InstanceId = deposit.InstanceId,
   309	                DefinitionId = deposit.Definition.Id,
   310	                X = deposit.Cell.X,
   311	                Y = deposit.Cell.Y,
   312	                Z = deposit.Cell.Z,
   313	                IsRevealed = deposit.IsRevealed,
   314	                RemainingYield = deposit.RemainingYield,
   315	                Version = deposit.Version,
   316	            });
   317	        }
   318	
   319	        return data;
   320	    }

   322	    private static SaveMetadataData CopyMetadata(SaveMetadataData metadata)
   323	    {
   324	        return new SaveMetadataData
   325	        {
   326	            SlotId = metadata.SlotId,
   327	            DisplayName = metadata.DisplayName,
   328	            SavedAtUtc = metadata.SavedAtUtc,
   329	            SimulationTick = metadata.SimulationTick,
   330	            WorldSeed = metadata.WorldSeed,
   331	            GeneratorVersion = metadata.GeneratorVersion,
   332	        };
   333	    }
   334	
   335	    private static void ValidateMetadata(SaveMetadataData metadata)
   336	    {
   337	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(metadata.SlotId)
   338	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(metadata.DisplayName)
   339	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(metadata.SavedAtUtc)
   340	            || metadata.SimulationTick < 0
   341	            || metadata.GeneratorVersion <= 0)
   342	        {
   343	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Save metadata is incomplete or invalid.");
   344	        }
   345	    }
   346	}
