===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/SaveGameLoader.Buildings.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed partial class SaveGameLoader
    14	{
    15	    private static Result<BuildingsState> BuildBuildingsState(
    16	        BuildingsSaveData data,
    17	        BuildingCatalog? catalog)
    18	    {
    19	        if (data is null || data.Buildings is null)
    20	        {
    21	            return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
    22	        }
    23	
    24	        if (data.Buildings.Count == 0)
    25	        {
    26	            return Result<BuildingsState>.Success(new BuildingsState());
    27	        }
    28	
    29	        if (catalog is null)
    30	        {
    31	            return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(SaveErrors.UnknownBuildingDefinition);
    32	        }
    33	
    34	        List<BuildingSnapshot> snapshots = new List<BuildingSnapshot>();
    35	        foreach (BuildingSaveData saved in data.Buildings
    36	            .OrderBy(item => item.BuildingId, StringComparer.Ordinal))
    37	        {
    38	            if (saved is null
    39	                || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingOrientation), saved.Orientation)
    40	                || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingStatus), saved.Status))
    41	            {
    42	                return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
    43	            }
    44	
    45	            BuildingDefinition definition;
    46	            try
    47	            {
    48	                definition = catalog.Get(new BuildingDefinitionId(saved.DefinitionId));
    49	            }
    50	            catch (KeyNotFoundException)
    51	            {
    52	                return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(SaveErrors.UnknownBuildingDefinition);
    53	            }
    54	
    55	            BuildingOrientation orientation = (BuildingOrientation)saved.Orientation;
    56	            CellId origin = new CellId(saved.OriginX, saved.OriginY, saved.OriginZ);
    57	            BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? boxPlan = ParseBoxPlan(saved.BoxPlan);
    58	            BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? packingPlan = ParsePackingPlan(saved.PackingPlan);
    59	            snapshots.Add(new BuildingSnapshot(
    60	                EntityId.Parse(saved.BuildingId),
    61	                definition,
    62	                origin,
    63	                orientation,
    64	                definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, orientation),
    65	                new CellId(saved.WorkPositionX, saved.WorkPositionY, saved.WorkPositionZ),
    66	                (BuildingStatus)saved.Status,
    67	                saved.CompletedWork,
    68	                saved.Durability,
    69	                saved.Version,
    70	                saved.DiagnosticReason,
    71	                boxPlan,
    72	                packingPlan));
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        return BuildingsState.RestoreWithPacking(snapshots);
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    private static BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? ParseBoxPlan(
    79	        BuildingBoxPlanSaveData? data)
    80	    {
    81	        if (data is null)
    82	        {
    83	            return null;
    84	        }
    85	
    86	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingBoxCommitState), data.CommitState))
    87	        {
    88	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Saved box commitment is invalid.");
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        return new BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot(
    92	            EntityId.Parse(data.SourceStackId),
    93	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
    94	            (BuildingBoxCommitState)data.CommitState);
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    private static BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? ParsePackingPlan(
    98	        BuildingPackingPlanSaveData? data)
    99	    {
   100	        if (data is null)
   101	        {
   102	            return null;
   103	        }
   104	
   105	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingPackingCommitState), data.CommitState))
   106	        {
   107	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Saved packing commitment is invalid.");
   108	        }
   109	
   110	        return new BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot(
   111	            EntityId.Parse(data.JobId),
   112	            EntityId.Parse(data.OutputStackId),
   113	            data.CompletedWork,
   114	            (BuildingPackingCommitState)data.CommitState);
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    private static Result ValidateBuildingReferences(
   118	        InventoryState inventory,
   119	        JobSystem jobs,
   120	        BuildingsState buildings)
   121	    {
   122	        foreach (BuildingSnapshot building in buildings.GetAll())
   123	        {
   124	            Result assembly = ValidateAssemblyReferences(inventory, jobs, building);
   125	            if (assembly.IsFailure)
   126	            {
   127	                return assembly;
   128	            }
   129	
   130	            Result packing = ValidatePackingReferences(inventory, jobs, building);
   131	            if (packing.IsFailure)
   132	            {
   133	                return packing;
   134	            }
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        return Result.Success();
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    private static Result ValidateAssemblyReferences(
   141	        InventoryState inventory,
   142	        JobSystem jobs,
   143	        BuildingSnapshot building)
   144	    {
   145	        if (building.BoxPlan is null)
   146	        {
   147	            return Result.Success();
   148	        }
   149	
   150	        BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot plan = building.BoxPlan;
   151	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(plan.JobId);
   152	        if (job?.Definition is not BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition definition
   153	            || definition.BuildingId != building.Id
   154	            || definition.SourceStackId != plan.SourceStackId)
   155	        {
   156	            return Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   157	        }
   158	
   159	        ItemStackSnapshot? stack = inventory.GetStack(plan.SourceStackId);
   160	        if (building.Status == BuildingStatus.Cancelled)
   161	        {
   162	            return job.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled
   163	                && IsAvailableSingleBox(stack, building.Definition.BoxPolicy!.BoxItemId)
   164	                && !HasReservation(stack!, plan.JobId)
   165	                ? Result.Success()
   166	                : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   167	        }
   168	
   169	        if (plan.CommitState == BuildingBoxCommitState.Reserved)
   170	        {
   171	            return !job.IsTerminal
   172	                && IsAvailableSingleBox(stack, building.Definition.BoxPolicy!.BoxItemId)
   173	                && HasReservation(stack!, plan.JobId)
   174	                ? Result.Success()
   175	                : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   176	        }
   177	
   178	        if (plan.CommitState == BuildingBoxCommitState.AtSite)
   179	        {
   180	            return !job.IsTerminal
   181	                && IsAvailableSingleBox(stack, building.Definition.BoxPolicy!.BoxItemId)
   182	                && stack!.Location == ItemLocation.InBuilding(building.Id)
   183	                && !HasReservation(stack, plan.JobId)
   184	                ? Result.Success()
   185	                : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   186	        }
   187	
   188	        return plan.CommitState == BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed
   189	            && job.Status == JobStatus.Completed
   190	            && stack is null
   191	            ? Result.Success()
   192	            : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    private static Result ValidatePackingReferences(
   196	        InventoryState inventory,
   197	        JobSystem jobs,
   198	        BuildingSnapshot building)
   199	    {
   200	        if (building.PackingPlan is null)
   201	        {
   202	            return Result.Success();
   203	        }
   204	
   205	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot plan = building.PackingPlan;
   206	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(plan.JobId);
   207	        if (job?.Definition is not BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition definition
   208	            || definition.BuildingId != building.Id
   209	            || definition.OutputStackId != plan.OutputStackId)
   210	        {
   211	            return Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   212	        }
   213	
   214	        ItemStackSnapshot? output = inventory.GetStack(plan.OutputStackId);
   215	        if (plan.CommitState == BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
   216	        {
   217	            return !job.IsTerminal && output is null
   218	                ? Result.Success()
   219	                : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   220	        }
   221	
   222	        if (plan.CommitState == BuildingPackingCommitState.Cancelled)
   223	        {
   224	            return job.Status == JobStatus.Cancelled && output is null
   225	                ? Result.Success()
   226	                : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   227	        }
   228	
   229	        return plan.CommitState == BuildingPackingCommitState.Completed
   230	            && job.Status == JobStatus.Completed
   231	            && IsAvailableSingleBox(output, building.Definition.BoxPolicy!.BoxItemId)
   232	            && output!.Location == ItemLocation.InWorld(building.Origin)
   233	            && output.Reservations.Count == 0
   234	            ? Result.Success()
   235	            : Result.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   236	    }
   237	
   238	    private static bool IsAvailableSingleBox(ItemStackSnapshot? stack, ItemId itemId)
   239	    {
   240	        return stack is not null
   241	            && stack.ItemId == itemId
   242	            && stack.Quantity == 1;
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    private static bool HasReservation(ItemStackSnapshot stack, EntityId jobId)
   246	    {
   247	        return stack.Reservations.Any(item => item.JobId == jobId && item.Quantity == 1);
   248	    }
   249	}
   250	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/SaveGameLoader.Mushrooms.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Ecology;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed partial class SaveGameLoader
    13	{
    14	    private static Result<MushroomState> BuildMushroomState(
    15	        MushroomSaveData data,
    16	        MushroomCatalog? catalog,
    17	        JobSystem jobs)
    18	    {
    19	        if (data is null || data.Sites is null)
    20	        {
    21	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Mushroom save data is missing.");
    22	        }
    23	
    24	        if (data.Sites.Count > 0 && catalog is null)
    25	        {
    26	            return Result<MushroomState>.Failure(SaveErrors.UnknownMushroomDefinition);
    27	        }
    28	
    29	        MushroomCatalog resolvedCatalog = catalog ?? new MushroomCatalog(
    30	            Array.Empty<MushroomDefinition>());
    31	        List<MushroomSiteSnapshot> snapshots = new List<MushroomSiteSnapshot>();
    32	        foreach (MushroomSiteSaveData site in data.Sites
    33	            .OrderBy(value => value.SiteId, StringComparer.Ordinal))
    34	        {
    35	            if (site is null
    36	                || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(MushroomStage), site.Stage)
    37	                || !resolvedCatalog.Contains(new MushroomDefinitionId(site.DefinitionId)))
    38	            {
    39	                return Result<MushroomState>.Failure(SaveErrors.UnknownMushroomDefinition);
    40	            }
    41	
    42	            EntityId? activeJob = ParseOptionalId(site.ActiveChopJobId);
    43	            EntityId? activeWorker = ParseOptionalId(site.ActiveWorkerId);
    44	            if (activeJob.HasValue)
    45	            {
    46	                JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(activeJob.Value);
    47	                if (job?.Definition is not MushroomChopJobDefinition definition
    48	                    || definition.SiteId != EntityId.Parse(site.SiteId)
    49	                    || job.IsTerminal
    50	                    || job.AssignedAgentId != activeWorker)
    51	                {
    52	                    return Result<MushroomState>.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
    53	                }
    54	            }
    55	
    56	            snapshots.Add(new MushroomSiteSnapshot(
    57	                EntityId.Parse(site.SiteId),
    58	                new MushroomDefinitionId(site.DefinitionId),
    59	                new CellId(site.X, site.Y, site.Z),
    60	                (MushroomStage)site.Stage,
    61	                site.StageStartedTick,
    62	                site.NextStageTick,
    63	                site.GrowthGeneration,
    64	                activeJob,
    65	                activeWorker,
    66	                site.RequiredSwings,
    67	                site.CompletedSwings,
    68	                site.GrowthPausedAtTick,
    69	                site.Version));
    70	        }
    71	
    72	        return MushroomState.Restore(resolvedCatalog, snapshots);
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    private static EntityId? ParseOptionalId(string? value) =>
    76	        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value) ? null : EntityId.Parse(value);
    77	}
    78	
    79	}
===== src/Dig.Application/Saving/SaveMigrations.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Application.Saving
     7	{
     8	
     9	public static class SaveErrors
    10	{
    11	    public static readonly DomainError InvalidDocument = new DomainError(
    12	        "save.document.invalid",
    13	        "The save document is malformed or inconsistent.");
    14	
    15	    public static readonly DomainError UnsupportedVersion = new DomainError(
    16	        "save.version.unsupported",
    17	        "The save format version cannot be loaded by this build.");
    18	
    19	    public static readonly DomainError UnknownJobType = new DomainError(
    20	        "save.job_type.unknown",
    21	        "The save references a job type without a registered codec.");
    22	
    23	    public static readonly DomainError UnknownBuildingDefinition = new DomainError(
    24	        "save.building_definition.unknown",
    25	        "The save references a building definition missing from the current catalog.");
    26	
    27	    public static readonly DomainError UnknownTerrainDepositDefinition = new DomainError(
    28	        "save.terrain_deposit_definition.unknown",
    29	        "The save references a terrain deposit definition missing from the current catalog.");
    30	
    31	    public static readonly DomainError UnknownMushroomDefinition = new DomainError(
    32	        "save.mushroom_definition.unknown",
    33	        "The save references a mushroom definition missing from the current catalog.");
    34	
    35	    public static readonly DomainError UnknownBarrelDefinition = new DomainError(
    36	        "save.barrel_definition.unknown",
    37	        "The save references a barrel definition missing from the current catalog.");
    38	}
    39	
    40	public sealed class SaveMigrationPipeline
    41	{
    42	    private readonly Dictionary<int, ISaveMigration> _bySourceVersion;
    43	
    44	    public SaveMigrationPipeline(IEnumerable<ISaveMigration> migrations)
    45	    {
    46	        if (migrations is null)
    47	        {
    48	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(migrations));
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        ISaveMigration[] values = migrations.ToArray();
    52	        if (values.Any(item => item is null
    53	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.Id)
    54	            || item.ToVersion != item.FromVersion + 1))
    55	        {
    56	            throw new ArgumentException(
    57	                "Save migrations must have ids and advance exactly one version.",
    58	                nameof(migrations));
    59	        }
    60	
    61	        if (values.Select(item => item.FromVersion).Distinct().Count() != values.Length)
    62	        {
    63	            throw new ArgumentException(
    64	                "Only one migration may start from each save version.",
    65	                nameof(migrations));
    66	        }
    67	
    68	        _bySourceVersion = values.ToDictionary(item => item.FromVersion);
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    public Result<SaveMigrationReport> Apply(SaveGameDocument document)
    72	    {
    73	        if (document is null)
    74	        {
    75	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(document));
    76	        }
    77	
    78	        if (document.FormatVersion < 0
    79	            || document.FormatVersion > SaveFormat.CurrentVersion)
    80	        {
    81	            return Result<SaveMigrationReport>.Failure(SaveErrors.UnsupportedVersion);
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        List<string> applied = new List<string>();
    85	        while (document.FormatVersion < SaveFormat.CurrentVersion)
    86	        {
    87	            if (!_bySourceVersion.TryGetValue(
    88	                document.FormatVersion,
    89	                out ISaveMigration? migration))
    90	            {
    91	                return Result<SaveMigrationReport>.Failure(SaveErrors.UnsupportedVersion);
    92	            }
    93	
    94	            int previousVersion = document.FormatVersion;
    95	            migration.Apply(document);
    96	            if (document.FormatVersion != migration.ToVersion
    97	                || previousVersion != migration.FromVersion)
    98	            {
    99	                return Result<SaveMigrationReport>.Failure(SaveErrors.InvalidDocument);
   100	            }
   101	
   102	            applied.Add(migration.Id);
   103	        }
   104	
   105	        return Result<SaveMigrationReport>.Success(new SaveMigrationReport(applied));
   106	    }
   107	}
   108	
   109	public sealed class LegacySaveVersionZeroMigration : ISaveMigration
   110	{
   111	    public string Id => "save.v0_to_v1.metadata";
   112	    public int FromVersion => 0;
   113	    public int ToVersion => 1;
   114	
   115	    public void Apply(SaveGameDocument document)
   116	    {
   117	        if (document is null)
   118	        {
   119	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(document));
   120	        }
   121	
   122	        if (document.FormatVersion != FromVersion)
   123	        {
   124	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Migration received the wrong source version.");
   125	        }
   126	
   127	        document.Metadata ??= new SaveMetadataData();
   128	        if (document.Metadata.GeneratorVersion <= 0)
   129	        {
   130	            document.Metadata.GeneratorVersion = 1;
   131	        }
   132	
   133	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(document.Metadata.DisplayName))
   134	        {
   135	            document.Metadata.DisplayName = document.Metadata.SlotId;
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        document.FormatVersion = ToVersion;
   139	    }
   140	}

   211	public sealed class SaveVersionFourAuthoritativeCoordinatesMigration : ISaveMigration
   212	{
   213	    public string Id => "save.v4_to_v5.authoritative_xyz";
   214	    public int FromVersion => 4;
   215	    public int ToVersion => 5;
   216	
   217	    public void Apply(SaveGameDocument document)
   218	    {
   219	        if (document is null)
   220	        {
   221	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(document));
   222	        }
   223	
   224	        if (document.FormatVersion != FromVersion)
   225	        {
   226	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   227	                "Migration received the wrong source version.");
   228	        }
   229	
   230	        document.World ??= new WorldSaveData();
   231	        document.World.Depth = Dig.Domain.World.WorldSize.RequiredDepth;
   232	        document.World.Chunks ??= new List<WorldChunkSaveData>();
   233	        foreach (WorldChunkSaveData chunk in document.World.Chunks)
   234	        {
   235	            if (chunk is null)
   236	            {
   237	                continue;
   238	            }
   239	
   240	            chunk.Z = 0;
   241	            chunk.Cells ??= new List<WorldCellSaveData>();
   242	            foreach (WorldCellSaveData cell in chunk.Cells)
   243	            {
   244	                if (cell is not null)
   245	                {
   246	                    cell.Z = 0;
   247	                }
   248	            }
   249	        }
   250	
   251	        document.Inventory ??= new InventorySaveData();
   252	        document.Inventory.Stacks ??= new List<ItemStackSaveData>();
   253	        foreach (ItemStackSaveData stack in document.Inventory.Stacks)
   254	        {
   255	            if (stack?.Location is not null && stack.Location.CellX.HasValue)
   256	            {
   257	                stack.Location.CellZ = 0;
   258	            }
   259	        }
   260	
   261	        document.Buildings ??= new BuildingsSaveData();
   262	        document.Buildings.Buildings ??= new List<BuildingSaveData>();
   263	        foreach (BuildingSaveData building in document.Buildings.Buildings)
   264	        {
   265	            if (building is not null)
   266	            {
   267	                building.OriginZ = 0;
   268	                building.WorkPositionZ = 0;
   269	            }
   270	        }
   271	
   272	        document.AgentPositions ??= new AgentPositionsSaveData();
   273	        document.TerrainDeposits ??= new TerrainDepositsSaveData();
   274	        document.FormatVersion = ToVersion;
   275	    }
   276	}

===== src/Dig.Application/WorldObjects/BarrelAttackStartUseCase.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     4	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     7	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Application.WorldObjects
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler
    13	    : ICommandHandler<StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand, Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>>
    14	{
    15	    private readonly IBarrelRepository _barrels;
    16	    private readonly IJobRepository _jobs;
    17	    private readonly IEventSink _events;
    18	
    19	    public StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler(
    20	        IBarrelRepository barrels,
    21	        IJobRepository jobs,
    22	        IEventSink events)
    23	    {
    24	        _barrels = barrels ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(barrels));
    25	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
    26	        _events = events ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(events));
    27	    }
    28	
    29	    public Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult> Handle(StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand command)
    30	    {
    31	        if (command is null)
    32	        {
    33	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(command));
    34	        }
    35	
    36	        BarrelSnapshot? barrel = _barrels.Get().Get(command.BarrelId);
    37	        if (barrel is null)
    38	        {
    39	            return Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>.Failure(BarrelErrors.NotFound);
    40	        }
    41	
    42	        if (!barrel.IsAttackable)
    43	        {
    44	            return Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>.Failure(BarrelErrors.NotAttackable);
    45	        }
    46	
    47	        JobSystem jobs = _jobs.Get();
    48	        ReservationSnapshot? workerReservation = jobs.GetReservations()
    49	            .FirstOrDefault(reservation =>
    50	                reservation.Key == ReservationKey.ForAgent(command.WorkerId));
    51	        if (workerReservation != null)
    52	        {
    53	            return Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>.Failure(JobErrors.AgentUnavailable);
    54	        }
    55	
    56	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition definition = new BarrelAttackJobDefinition(
    57	            command.JobId,
    58	            command.BarrelId,
    59	            barrel.Cell,
    60	            command.WorkPosition,
    61	            barrel.Version,
    62	            barrel.ContentsGeneration,
    63	            command.Priority,
    64	            command.Tick,
    65	            JobRetryPolicy.Default);
    66	        Result added = jobs.Add(definition);
    67	        if (added.IsFailure)
    68	        {
    69	            return Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>.Failure(added.Error!);
    70	        }
    71	
    72	        Result available = jobs.MakeAvailable(command.JobId, command.Tick);
    73	        if (available.IsFailure)
    74	        {
    75	            return RejectNewAttack(jobs, command.JobId, available.Error!, command.Tick);
    76	        }
    77	
    78	        Result canClaim = jobs.CanClaim(
    79	            command.JobId,
    80	            command.WorkerId,
    81	            toolStackId: null,
    82	            command.Tick);
    83	        if (canClaim.IsFailure)
    84	        {
    85	            return RejectNewAttack(jobs, command.JobId, canClaim.Error!, command.Tick);
    86	        }
    87	
    88	        Result claimed = jobs.Claim(command.JobId, command.WorkerId, command.Tick);
    89	        if (claimed.IsFailure)
    90	        {
    91	            return RejectNewAttack(jobs, command.JobId, claimed.Error!, command.Tick);
    92	        }
    93	
    94	        Result started = jobs.Start(command.JobId, command.Tick);
    95	        if (started.IsFailure)
    96	        {
    97	            return RejectNewAttack(jobs, command.JobId, started.Error!, command.Tick);
    98	        }
    99	
   100	        SaveAndPublish(jobs);
   101	        return Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>.Success(new BarrelAttackStartedResult(
   102	            command.JobId,
   103	            command.BarrelId,
   104	            command.WorkerId,
   105	            barrel.Version,
   106	            barrel.ContentsGeneration));
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    private Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult> RejectNewAttack(
   110	        JobSystem jobs,
   111	        EntityId jobId,
   112	        DomainError error,
   113	        long tick)
   114	    {
   115	        JobSnapshot? job = jobs.Get(jobId);
   116	        if (job != null && !job.IsTerminal)
   117	        {
   118	            Result cancelled = jobs.Cancel(
   119	                jobId,
   120	                new JobBlockReason("barrel_start_rejected", error.Message),
   121	                tick);
   122	            if (cancelled.IsFailure)
   123	            {
   124	                error = cancelled.Error!;
   125	            }
   126	        }
   127	
   128	        SaveAndPublish(jobs);
   129	        return Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>.Failure(error);
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    private void SaveAndPublish(JobSystem jobs)
   133	    {
   134	        _jobs.Save(jobs);
   135	        _events.Append(jobs.DequeueUncommittedEvents());
   136	    }
   137	}
   138	
   139	}
===== src/Dig.Application/WorldObjects/BarrelContracts.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.World;
     5	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Application.WorldObjects
     8	{
     9	
    10	public interface IBarrelRepository
    11	{
    12	    BarrelState Get();
    13	    void Save(BarrelState barrels);
    14	}
    15	
    16	public sealed class StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand
    17	    : ICommand<Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult>>
    18	{
    19	    public StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    20	        EntityId jobId,
    21	        EntityId barrelId,
    22	        EntityId workerId,
    23	        CellId workPosition,
    24	        int priority,
    25	        long tick)
    26	    {
    27	        if (jobId.IsEmpty || barrelId.IsEmpty || workerId.IsEmpty)
    28	        {
    29	            throw new ArgumentException("Job, barrel and worker ids are required.");
    30	        }
    31	
    32	        if (priority < 0 || priority > 1000 || tick < 0)
    33	        {
    34	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(priority));
    35	        }
    36	
    37	        JobId = jobId;
    38	        BarrelId = barrelId;
    39	        WorkerId = workerId;
    40	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
    41	        Priority = priority;
    42	        Tick = tick;
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    public EntityId JobId { get; }
    46	    public EntityId BarrelId { get; }
    47	    public EntityId WorkerId { get; }
    48	    public CellId WorkPosition { get; }
    49	    public int Priority { get; }

===== src/Dig.Domain/Buildings/BuildingDefinitions.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	
     8	namespace Dig.Domain.Buildings

    11	public readonly struct BuildingDefinitionId
    12	    : IEquatable<BuildingDefinitionId>, IComparable<BuildingDefinitionId>
    13	{
    14	    private readonly string? _value;
    15	
    16	    public BuildingDefinitionId(string value)
    17	    {
    18	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
    19	        {
    20	            throw new ArgumentException("Building definition id is required.", nameof(value));
    21	        }
    22	
    23	        _value = value.Trim();
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public bool IsEmpty => string.IsNullOrEmpty(_value);
    27	
    28	    public int CompareTo(BuildingDefinitionId other)
    29	    {
    30	        return string.Compare(_value, other._value, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    public bool Equals(BuildingDefinitionId other)
    34	    {
    35	        return string.Equals(_value, other._value, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    public override bool Equals(object? obj)
    39	    {
    40	        return obj is BuildingDefinitionId other && Equals(other);
    41	    }
    42	
    43	    public override int GetHashCode()
    44	    {
    45	        return StringComparer.Ordinal.GetHashCode(_value ?? string.Empty);
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    public override string ToString()
    49	    {
    50	        return _value ?? string.Empty;
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    public static bool operator ==(BuildingDefinitionId left, BuildingDefinitionId right)
    54	    {
    55	        return left.Equals(right);
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    public static bool operator !=(BuildingDefinitionId left, BuildingDefinitionId right)
    59	    {
    60	        return !left.Equals(right);
    61	    }
    62	}

    64	public enum BuildingOrientation
    65	{
    66	    North = 0,
    67	    East = 1,
    68	    South = 2,
    69	    West = 3,
    70	}
    71	
    72	public readonly struct CellOffset : IEquatable<CellOffset>, IComparable<CellOffset>
    73	{
    74	    public CellOffset(int x, int y)
    75	    {
    76	        X = x;
    77	        Y = y;
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    public int X { get; }
    81	
    82	    public int Y { get; }
    83	
    84	    public CellOffset Rotate(BuildingOrientation orientation)
    85	    {
    86	        return orientation switch
    87	        {
    88	            BuildingOrientation.North => this,
    89	            BuildingOrientation.East => new CellOffset(-Y, X),
    90	            BuildingOrientation.South => new CellOffset(-X, -Y),
    91	            BuildingOrientation.West => new CellOffset(Y, -X),
    92	            _ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(orientation)),
    93	        };
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    public CellId Apply(CellId origin)
    97	    {
    98	        return new CellId(checked(origin.X + X), checked(origin.Y + Y), origin.Z);
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    public int CompareTo(CellOffset other)
   102	    {
   103	        int yComparison = Y.CompareTo(other.Y);
   104	        return yComparison != 0 ? yComparison : X.CompareTo(other.X);
   105	    }
   106	
   107	    public bool Equals(CellOffset other)
   108	    {
   109	        return X == other.X && Y == other.Y;
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    public override bool Equals(object? obj)
   113	    {
   114	        return obj is CellOffset other && Equals(other);
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    public override int GetHashCode()
   118	    {
   119	        return HashCode.Combine(X, Y);
   120	    }
   121	}

   123	public readonly struct BuildingMaterialRequirement
   124	{
   125	    public BuildingMaterialRequirement(ItemId itemId, int quantity)
   126	    {
   127	        if (itemId.IsEmpty)
   128	        {
   129	            throw new ArgumentException("Material item id cannot be empty.", nameof(itemId));
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        if (quantity <= 0)
   133	        {
   134	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(quantity));
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        ItemId = itemId;
   138	        Quantity = quantity;
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    public ItemId ItemId { get; }
   142	
   143	    public int Quantity { get; }
   144	}
   145	
   146	public sealed class BuildingDefinition
   147	{
   148	    private readonly CellOffset[] _footprint;
   149	    private readonly CellOffset[] _workPositions;
   150	    private readonly BuildingMaterialRequirement[] _materials;
   151	
   152	    public BuildingDefinition(
   153	        BuildingDefinitionId id,
   154	        string name,
   155	        IEnumerable<CellOffset> footprint,
   156	        IEnumerable<CellOffset> workPositions,
   157	        IEnumerable<BuildingMaterialRequirement> materials,
   158	        int requiredWork,
   159	        int maximumDurability,
   160	        BuildingBoxPolicy? boxPolicy = null)
   161	    {
   162	        if (id.IsEmpty)
   163	        {
   164	            throw new ArgumentException("Building definition id cannot be empty.", nameof(id));
   165	        }
   166	
   167	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
   168	        {
   169	            throw new ArgumentException("Building name is required.", nameof(name));
   170	        }
   171	
   172	        _footprint = NormalizeOffsets(footprint, nameof(footprint));
   173	        _workPositions = NormalizeOffsets(workPositions, nameof(workPositions));
   174	        _materials = NormalizeMaterials(materials, requireMaterials: boxPolicy is null);
   175	        if (boxPolicy is not null && _materials.Length != 0)
   176	        {
   177	            throw new ArgumentException(
   178	                "A building cannot use box and legacy material construction together.",
   179	                nameof(materials));
   180	        }
   181	
   182	        if (requiredWork <= 0 || maximumDurability <= 0)
   183	        {
   184	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(requiredWork));
   185	        }
   186	
   187	        Id = id;
   188	        Name = name.Trim();
   189	        RequiredWork = requiredWork;
   190	        MaximumDurability = maximumDurability;
   191	        BoxPolicy = boxPolicy;
   192	    }
   193	
   194	    public BuildingDefinitionId Id { get; }
   195	
   196	    public string Name { get; }
   197	
   198	    public int RequiredWork { get; }
   199	
   200	    public int MaximumDurability { get; }
   201	
   202	    public BuildingBoxPolicy? BoxPolicy { get; }
   203	
   204	    public BuildingConstructionPolicyKind ConstructionPolicy => BoxPolicy is null
   205	        ? BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.LegacyMaterials
   206	        : BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.BuildingBox;
   207	
   208	    public IReadOnlyList<CellOffset> Footprint =>
   209	        new ReadOnlyCollection<CellOffset>(_footprint);
   210	
   211	    public IReadOnlyList<CellOffset> WorkPositions =>
   212	        new ReadOnlyCollection<CellOffset>(_workPositions);
   213	
   214	    public IReadOnlyList<BuildingMaterialRequirement> Materials =>
   215	        new ReadOnlyCollection<BuildingMaterialRequirement>(_materials);
   216	
   217	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> ResolveFootprint(
   218	        CellId origin,
   219	        BuildingOrientation orientation)
   220	    {
   221	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(_footprint
   222	            .Select(offset => offset.Rotate(orientation).Apply(origin))
   223	            .OrderBy(cell => cell)
   224	            .ToArray());
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> ResolveWorkPositions(
   228	        CellId origin,
   229	        BuildingOrientation orientation)
   230	    {
   231	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(_workPositions
   232	            .Select(offset => offset.Rotate(orientation).Apply(origin))
   233	            .OrderBy(cell => cell)
   234	            .ToArray());
   235	    }
   236	
   237	    private static CellOffset[] NormalizeOffsets(
   238	        IEnumerable<CellOffset> values,
   239	        string parameterName)
   240	    {
   241	        if (values is null)
   242	        {
   243	            throw new ArgumentNullException(parameterName);
   244	        }
   245	
   246	        CellOffset[] result = values.Distinct().OrderBy(value => value).ToArray();
   247	        if (result.Length == 0)
   248	        {
   249	            throw new ArgumentException("At least one cell offset is required.", parameterName);
   250	        }
   251	
   252	        return result;
   253	    }
   254	
   255	    private static BuildingMaterialRequirement[] NormalizeMaterials(
   256	        IEnumerable<BuildingMaterialRequirement> values,
   257	        bool requireMaterials)
   258	    {
   259	        if (values is null)
   260	        {
   261	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(values));
   262	        }
   263	
   264	        BuildingMaterialRequirement[] result = values
   265	            .GroupBy(value => value.ItemId)
   266	            .Select(group => new BuildingMaterialRequirement(
   267	                group.Key,
   268	                checked(group.Sum(value => value.Quantity))))
   269	            .OrderBy(value => value.ItemId)
   270	            .ToArray();
   271	        if (requireMaterials && result.Length == 0)
   272	        {
   273	            throw new ArgumentException("A legacy building needs at least one material.", nameof(values));
   274	        }
   275	
   276	        return result;
   277	    }
   278	}
   279	
   280	public sealed class BuildingCatalog
   281	{
   282	    private readonly Dictionary<BuildingDefinitionId, BuildingDefinition> _definitions;
   283	
   284	    public BuildingCatalog(IEnumerable<BuildingDefinition> definitions)
   285	    {
   286	        if (definitions is null)
   287	        {
   288	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definitions));
   289	        }
   290	
   291	        BuildingDefinition[] values = definitions.OrderBy(value => value.Id).ToArray();
   292	        if (values.Length == 0
   293	            || values.Select(value => value.Id).Distinct().Count() != values.Length)
   294	        {
   295	            throw new ArgumentException("Building definitions must be non-empty and unique.");
   296	        }
   297	
   298	        _definitions = values.ToDictionary(value => value.Id);
   299	    }
   300	
   301	    public BuildingDefinition Get(BuildingDefinitionId id)
   302	    {
   303	        return _definitions.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingDefinition? value)
   304	            ? value
   305	            : throw new KeyNotFoundException($"Unknown building definition '{id}'.");
   306	    }
   307	
   308	    public IReadOnlyList<BuildingDefinition> GetAll()
   309	    {
   310	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<BuildingDefinition>(_definitions.Values
   311	            .OrderBy(value => value.Id)
   312	            .ToArray());
   313	    }
   314	
   315	    public BuildingDefinition? FindByBoxItemId(ItemId boxItemId)
   316	    {
   317	        if (boxItemId.IsEmpty)
   318	        {
   319	            throw new ArgumentException("BuildingBox item id is required.", nameof(boxItemId));
   320	        }
   321	
   322	        return _definitions.Values
   323	            .Where(value => value.BoxPolicy?.BoxItemId == boxItemId)
   324	            .OrderBy(value => value.Id)
   325	            .FirstOrDefault();
   326	    }
   327	}
   328	}

     1	using System;

    14	        "buildings.already_exists",
    15	        "A building project with the same id already exists.");
    16	
    17	    public static readonly DomainError NotFound = new DomainError(
    18	        "buildings.not_found",
    19	        "The requested building project does not exist.");
    20	
    21	    public static readonly DomainError InvalidStatus = new DomainError(
    22	        "buildings.invalid_status",
    23	        "The building cannot perform that transition from its current status.");

    25	    public static readonly DomainError WrongConstructionPolicy = new DomainError(
    26	        "buildings.construction_policy.invalid",

    30	        "buildings.box_plan.not_found",

    34	        "buildings.placement.out_of_bounds",

    66	public sealed class BuildingPlacementResult
    67	{
    68	    private BuildingPlacementResult(
    69	        bool succeeded,
    70	        DomainError? error,
    71	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> footprint,
    72	        CellId workPosition)
    73	    {
    74	        Succeeded = succeeded;
    75	        Error = error;
    76	        Footprint = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(footprint.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray());
    77	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    public bool Succeeded { get; }
    81	
    82	    public DomainError? Error { get; }
    83	
    84	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> Footprint { get; }
    85	
    86	    public CellId WorkPosition { get; }
    87	
    88	    public static BuildingPlacementResult Success(
    89	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> footprint,
    90	        CellId workPosition)
    91	    {
    92	        return new BuildingPlacementResult(true, null, footprint, workPosition);
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    public static BuildingPlacementResult Failure(
    96	        DomainError error,
    97	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> footprint)
    98	    {
    99	        return new BuildingPlacementResult(false, error, footprint, default);
   100	    }
   101	}
   102	
   103	public sealed class BuildingPlacementValidator
   104	{
   105	    public BuildingPlacementResult Validate(
   106	        BuildingDefinition definition,
   107	        CellId origin,
   108	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   109	        WorldSnapshot world,
   110	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> occupiedCells,
   111	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> reachableCells,
   112	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId>? ecologyBlockedCells = null)
   113	    {
   114	        if (definition is null)
   115	        {
   116	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definition));
   117	        }
   118	
   119	        if (world is null)
   120	        {
   121	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(world));
   122	        }
   123	
   124	        if (occupiedCells is null || reachableCells is null)
   125	        {
   126	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(occupiedCells));
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        IReadOnlyList<CellId> footprint = definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, orientation);
   130	        if (footprint.Any(cell => !world.Size.Contains(cell)))
   131	        {
   132	            return BuildingPlacementResult.Failure(
   133	                BuildingErrors.PlacementOutOfBounds,
   134	                footprint);
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        Dictionary<CellId, CellSnapshot> cells = world.Chunks
   138	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
   139	            .ToDictionary(cell => cell.Id);
   140	        if (footprint.Any(cell => cells[cell].IsSolid))
   141	        {
   142	            return BuildingPlacementResult.Failure(
   143	                BuildingErrors.PlacementSolid,
   144	                footprint);
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        if (footprint.Any(cell => !cells[cell].State.IsExplored))
   148	        {
   149	            return BuildingPlacementResult.Failure(
   150	                BuildingErrors.PlacementUnexplored,
   151	                footprint);
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        HashSet<CellId> ecologyBlocked = new HashSet<CellId>(
   155	            ecologyBlockedCells ?? Array.Empty<CellId>());
   156	        if (footprint.Any(ecologyBlocked.Contains))
   157	        {
   158	            return BuildingPlacementResult.Failure(
   159	                BuildingErrors.PlacementEcologyBlocked,
   160	                footprint);
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        HashSet<CellId> occupied = new HashSet<CellId>(occupiedCells);
   164	        if (footprint.Any(occupied.Contains))
   165	        {
   166	            return BuildingPlacementResult.Failure(
   167	                BuildingErrors.PlacementOccupied,
   168	                footprint);
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        HashSet<CellId> reachable = new HashSet<CellId>(reachableCells);
   172	        CellId[] configured = definition
   173	            .ResolveWorkPositions(origin, orientation)
   174	            .ToArray();
   175	        bool legacyConfiguredPositionIsReachable = configured.Any(reachable.Contains);
   176	        CellId? workPosition = ResolveSideWorkPositions(footprint, origin)
   177	            .Where(world.Size.Contains)
   178	            .Where(cell => !occupied.Contains(cell))
   179	            .Where(cell => cells.TryGetValue(cell, out CellSnapshot snapshot)
   180	                && !snapshot.IsSolid
   181	                && snapshot.State.IsExplored)
   182	            .Where(cell => reachable.Contains(cell) || legacyConfiguredPositionIsReachable)
   183	            .OrderBy(cell => reachable.Contains(cell) ? 0 : 1)
   184	            .ThenBy(cell => Math.Abs(cell.X - origin.X))
   185	            .ThenBy(cell => cell)
   186	            .Cast<CellId?>()
   187	            .FirstOrDefault();
   188	        if (!workPosition.HasValue)
   189	        {
   190	            return BuildingPlacementResult.Failure(
   191	                BuildingErrors.NoReachableWorkPosition,
   192	                footprint);
   193	        }
   194	
   195	        return BuildingPlacementResult.Success(footprint, workPosition.Value);
   196	    }
   197	
   198	    private static IEnumerable<CellId> ResolveSideWorkPositions(
   199	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> footprint,
   200	        CellId origin)
   201	    {
   202	        int minimumX = footprint.Min(cell => cell.X);
   203	        int maximumX = footprint.Max(cell => cell.X);
   204	        yield return new CellId(minimumX - 1, origin.Y, origin.Z);
   205	        yield return new CellId(maximumX + 1, origin.Y, origin.Z);
   206	    }
   207	}
   208	
   209	}
===== src/Dig.Domain/Buildings/BuildingProjectState.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Domain.Buildings
     8	{
     9	
    10	internal sealed class BuildingProjectState
    11	{
    12	    public BuildingProjectState(
    13	        EntityId id,
    14	        BuildingDefinition definition,
    15	        CellId origin,
    16	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    17	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> footprint,
    18	        CellId workPosition,
    19	        BuildingStatus initialStatus = BuildingStatus.AwaitingMaterials)
    20	    {
    21	        if (initialStatus != BuildingStatus.AwaitingMaterials
    22	            && initialStatus != BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox)
    23	        {
    24	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(initialStatus));
    25	        }
    26	
    27	        Id = id;
    28	        Definition = definition;
    29	        Origin = origin;
    30	        Orientation = orientation;
    31	        Footprint = footprint.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray();
    32	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
    33	        Status = initialStatus;
    34	    }
    35	
    36	    public EntityId Id { get; }
    37	
    38	    public BuildingDefinition Definition { get; }
    39	
    40	    public CellId Origin { get; }
    41	
    42	    public BuildingOrientation Orientation { get; }
    43	
    44	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> Footprint { get; }
    45	
    46	    public CellId WorkPosition { get; }
    47	
    48	    public BuildingStatus Status { get; private set; }
    49	
    50	    public int CompletedWork { get; private set; }
    51	
    52	    public int Durability { get; private set; }
    53	
    54	    public long Version { get; private set; }
    55	
    56	    public string? DiagnosticReason { get; private set; }
    57	
    58	    internal static Result<BuildingProjectState> Restore(BuildingSnapshot snapshot)
    59	    {
    60	        if (snapshot is null)
    61	        {
    62	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(snapshot));
    63	        }
    64	
    65	        CellId[] expectedFootprint = snapshot.Definition
    66	            .ResolveFootprint(snapshot.Origin, snapshot.Orientation)
    67	            .OrderBy(cell => cell)
    68	            .ToArray();
    69	        CellId[] savedFootprint = snapshot.Footprint.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray();
    70	        bool validWorkPosition = snapshot.Definition
    71	            .ResolveWorkPositions(snapshot.Origin, snapshot.Orientation)
    72	            .Contains(snapshot.WorkPosition);
    73	        if (!expectedFootprint.SequenceEqual(savedFootprint)
    74	            || !validWorkPosition
    75	            || snapshot.CompletedWork < 0
    76	            || snapshot.CompletedWork > snapshot.Definition.RequiredWork
    77	            || snapshot.Durability < 0
    78	            || snapshot.Durability > snapshot.Definition.MaximumDurability
    79	            || snapshot.Version < 0)
    80	        {
    81	            return Invalid("Saved building geometry or numeric state is inconsistent.");
    82	        }
    83	
    84	        BuildingConstructionPolicyKind policy = snapshot.Definition.ConstructionPolicy;
    85	        if ((snapshot.Status == BuildingStatus.AwaitingMaterials
    86	                && policy != BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.LegacyMaterials)
    87	            || (snapshot.Status == BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox
    88	                && policy != BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.BuildingBox)
    89	            || (snapshot.Status == BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete
    90	                && snapshot.CompletedWork != snapshot.Definition.RequiredWork)
    91	            || (snapshot.Status == BuildingStatus.Completed
    92	                && snapshot.Durability != snapshot.Definition.MaximumDurability)
    93	            || (snapshot.Status == BuildingStatus.Damaged
    94	                && snapshot.Durability >= snapshot.Definition.MaximumDurability))
    95	        {
    96	            return Invalid("Saved building status does not match its policy or progress.");
    97	        }
    98	
    99	        BuildingStatus initial = policy == BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.BuildingBox
   100	            ? BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox
   101	            : BuildingStatus.AwaitingMaterials;
   102	        BuildingProjectState restored = new BuildingProjectState(
   103	            snapshot.Id,
   104	            snapshot.Definition,
   105	            snapshot.Origin,
   106	            snapshot.Orientation,
   107	            snapshot.Footprint,
   108	            snapshot.WorkPosition,
   109	            initial)
   110	        {
   111	            Status = snapshot.Status,
   112	            CompletedWork = snapshot.CompletedWork,
   113	            Durability = snapshot.Durability,
   114	            Version = snapshot.Version,
   115	            DiagnosticReason = snapshot.DiagnosticReason,
   116	        };
   117	        return Result<BuildingProjectState>.Success(restored);
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    public void MarkMaterialsReady()
   121	    {
   122	        Status = BuildingStatus.ReadyToBuild;
   123	        DiagnosticReason = null;
   124	        IncrementVersion();
   125	    }
   126	
   127	    public void StartConstruction()
   128	    {
   129	        Status = BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction;
   130	        DiagnosticReason = null;
   131	        IncrementVersion();
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    public void AddWork(int amount)
   135	    {
   136	        CompletedWork = Math.Min(
   137	            Definition.RequiredWork,
   138	            checked(CompletedWork + amount));
   139	        if (CompletedWork == Definition.RequiredWork)
   140	        {
   141	            Status = BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete;
   142	        }
   143	
   144	        IncrementVersion();
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    public void Block(string reason)
   148	    {
   149	        DiagnosticReason = reason;
   150	        IncrementVersion();
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    public void Complete()
   154	    {
   155	        Status = BuildingStatus.Completed;
   156	        Durability = Definition.MaximumDurability;
   157	        DiagnosticReason = null;
   158	        IncrementVersion();
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    public void StartPacking()
   162	    {
   163	        DiagnosticReason = null;
   164	        IncrementVersion();
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    public void RecordPackingWork()
   168	    {
   169	        IncrementVersion();
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    public void CancelPacking(string reason)
   173	    {
   174	        DiagnosticReason = reason;
   175	        IncrementVersion();
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    public void Cancel(string reason)
   179	    {
   180	        Status = BuildingStatus.Cancelled;
   181	        DiagnosticReason = reason;
   182	        IncrementVersion();
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    public void Remove(string reason)
   186	    {
   187	        Status = BuildingStatus.Removed;
   188	        DiagnosticReason = reason;
   189	        IncrementVersion();
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    public void Damage(int amount)
   193	    {
   194	        Durability = Math.Max(0, Durability - amount);
   195	        Status = BuildingStatus.Damaged;
   196	        IncrementVersion();
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    public void Repair(int amount)
   200	    {
   201	        Durability = Math.Min(Definition.MaximumDurability, checked(Durability + amount));
   202	        if (Durability == Definition.MaximumDurability)
   203	        {
   204	            Status = BuildingStatus.Completed;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        IncrementVersion();
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    public BuildingSnapshot CreateSnapshot()
   211	    {
   212	        return new BuildingSnapshot(
   213	            Id,
   214	            Definition,
   215	            Origin,
   216	            Orientation,
   217	            Footprint,
   218	            WorkPosition,
   219	            Status,
   220	            CompletedWork,
   221	            Durability,
   222	            Version,
   223	            DiagnosticReason);
   224	    }
   225	
   226	    private static Result<BuildingProjectState> Invalid(string message)
   227	    {
   228	        return Result<BuildingProjectState>.Failure(new DomainError(
   229	            "buildings.restore.invalid_project",
   230	            message));
   231	    }
   232	
   233	    private void IncrementVersion()
   234	    {
   235	        Version = checked(Version + 1);
   236	    }
   237	}
   238	}

    60	        int completedWork,
    61	        BuildingPackingCommitState commitState)

    88	public sealed class BuildingSnapshot
    89	{
    90	    public BuildingSnapshot(
    91	        EntityId id,
    92	        BuildingDefinition definition,
    93	        CellId origin,
    94	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    95	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> footprint,
    96	        CellId workPosition,
    97	        BuildingStatus status,
    98	        int completedWork,
    99	        int durability,
   100	        long version,
   101	        string? diagnosticReason,
   102	        BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? boxPlan = null,
   103	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? packingPlan = null)
   104	    {
   105	        Id = id;
   106	        Definition = definition ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definition));
   107	        Origin = origin;
   108	        Orientation = orientation;
   109	        Footprint = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(footprint.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray());
   110	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
   111	        Status = status;
   112	        CompletedWork = completedWork;
   113	        Durability = durability;
   114	        Version = version;
   115	        DiagnosticReason = diagnosticReason;
   116	        BoxPlan = boxPlan;
   117	        PackingPlan = packingPlan;
   118	    }

   131

   133

   135	
   136	    public int Durability { get; }
   137	
   138	    public long Version { get; }
   139	
   140	    public string? DiagnosticReason { get; }
   141	
   142	    public BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? BoxPlan { get; }
   143	
   144	    public BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? PackingPlan { get; }
   145	
   146	    public bool IsActive => Status != BuildingStatus.Cancelled
   147	        && Status != BuildingStatus.Removed;
   148	}
