===== src/Dig.Domain/Buildings/BuildingState.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	
     8	namespace Dig.Domain.Buildings
     9	{
    10	
    11	public sealed partial class BuildingsState : AggregateRoot
    12	{
    13	    private readonly Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingProjectState> _buildings =
    14	        new Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingProjectState>();
    15	
    16	    public Result Place(
    17	        EntityId id,
    18	        BuildingDefinition definition,
    19	        CellId origin,
    20	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    21	        BuildingPlacementResult placement,
    22	        long tick)
    23	    {
    24	        ValidateTick(tick);
    25	        if (id.IsEmpty)
    26	        {
    27	            throw new ArgumentException("Building id cannot be empty.", nameof(id));
    28	        }
    29	
    30	        if (definition is null || placement is null)
    31	        {
    32	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definition));
    33	        }
    34	
    35	        if (definition.ConstructionPolicy != BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.LegacyMaterials)
    36	        {
    37	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.WrongConstructionPolicy);
    38	        }
    39	
    40	        if (!placement.Succeeded)
    41	        {
    42	            return Result.Failure(placement.Error!);
    43	        }
    44	
    45	        if (_buildings.ContainsKey(id))
    46	        {
    47	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.AlreadyExists);
    48	        }
    49	
    50	        BuildingProjectState project = new BuildingProjectState(
    51	            id,
    52	            definition,
    53	            origin,
    54	            orientation,
    55	            placement.Footprint,
    56	            placement.WorkPosition);
    57	        _buildings.Add(id, project);
    58	        Raise(new BuildingPlaced(tick, id, definition.Id));
    59	        return Result.Success();
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    public Result MarkMaterialsReady(EntityId id)
    63	    {
    64	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
    65	        if (project is null)
    66	        {
    67	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
    68	        }
    69	
    70	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.AwaitingMaterials)
    71	        {
    72	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        project.MarkMaterialsReady();
    76	        return Result.Success();
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    public Result StartConstruction(EntityId id)
    80	    {
    81	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
    82	        if (project is null)
    83	        {
    84	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.ReadyToBuild)
    88	        {
    89	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        project.StartConstruction();
    93	        return Result.Success();
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    public Result AddConstructionWork(EntityId id, int amount, long tick)
    97	    {
    98	        ValidateTick(tick);
    99	        if (amount <= 0)
   100	        {
   101	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));
   102	        }
   103	
   104	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   105	        if (project is null)
   106	        {
   107	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   108	        }
   109	
   110	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction)
   111	        {
   112	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        project.AddWork(amount);
   116	        Raise(new BuildingConstructionProgressed(
   117	            tick,
   118	            id,
   119	            project.CompletedWork,
   120	            project.Definition.RequiredWork));
   121	        return Result.Success();
   122	    }
   123	
   124	    public Result Complete(EntityId id, long tick)
   125	    {
   126	        ValidateTick(tick);
   127	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   128	        if (project is null)
   129	        {
   130	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   131	        }
   132	
   133	        if (project.Definition.ConstructionPolicy != BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.LegacyMaterials)
   134	        {
   135	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.WrongConstructionPolicy);
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete)
   139	        {
   140	            return Result.Failure(
   141	                project.CompletedWork < project.Definition.RequiredWork
   142	                    ? BuildingErrors.WorkIncomplete
   143	                    : BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        project.Complete();
   147	        Raise(new BuildingCompleted(tick, id));
   148	        return Result.Success();
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    public Result Block(EntityId id, string reason)
   152	    {
   153	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reason))
   154	        {
   155	            throw new ArgumentException("Block reason is required.", nameof(reason));
   156	        }
   157	
   158	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   159	        if (project is null)
   160	        {
   161	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        project.Block(reason.Trim());
   165	        return Result.Success();
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    public Result Cancel(EntityId id, string reason, long tick)
   169	    {
   170	        return RemoveCore(id, reason, tick, cancel: true);
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    public Result Remove(EntityId id, string reason, long tick)
   174	    {
   175	        return RemoveCore(id, reason, tick, cancel: false);
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    public Result Damage(EntityId id, int amount, long tick)
   179	    {
   180	        ValidateTick(tick);
   181	        if (amount <= 0)
   182	        {
   183	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));
   184	        }
   185	
   186	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   187	        if (project is null)
   188	        {
   189	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   190	        }
   191	
   192	        if (HasActivePackingPlan(id)
   193	            || (project.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   194	                && project.Status != BuildingStatus.Damaged))
   195	        {
   196	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   197	        }
   198	
   199	        project.Damage(amount);
   200	        Raise(new BuildingDamaged(tick, id, project.Durability));
   201	        return Result.Success();
   202	    }
   203	
   204	    public Result Repair(EntityId id, int amount)
   205	    {
   206	        if (amount <= 0)
   207	        {
   208	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   212	        if (project is null)
   213	        {
   214	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   215	        }
   216	
   217	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.Damaged)
   218	        {
   219	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   220	        }
   221	
   222	        project.Repair(amount);
   223	        return Result.Success();
   224	    }
   225	
   226	    public BuildingSnapshot? Get(EntityId id)
   227	    {
   228	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   229	        return project is null ? null : CreateCombinedSnapshot(project);
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    public IReadOnlyList<BuildingSnapshot> GetAll()
   233	    {
   234	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<BuildingSnapshot>(_buildings.Values
   235	            .Select(CreateCombinedSnapshot)
   236	            .OrderBy(value => value.Id.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
   237	            .ToArray());
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> GetOccupiedCells()
   241	    {
   242	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(GetAll()
   243	            .Where(value => value.IsActive)
   244	            .SelectMany(value => value.Footprint)
   245	            .Distinct()
   246	            .OrderBy(value => value)
   247	            .ToArray());
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    private Result RemoveCore(EntityId id, string reason, long tick, bool cancel)
   251	    {
   252	        ValidateTick(tick);
   253	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reason))
   254	        {
   255	            throw new ArgumentException("Removal reason is required.", nameof(reason));
   256	        }
   257	
   258	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   259	        if (project is null)
   260	        {
   261	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   262	        }
   263	
   264	        bool canCancel = project.Status is BuildingStatus.AwaitingMaterials
   265	            or BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox
   266	            or BuildingStatus.ReadyToBuild
   267	            or BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction
   268	            or BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete;
   269	        bool canRemove = project.Status is BuildingStatus.Completed or BuildingStatus.Damaged;
   270	        if ((!cancel && HasActivePackingPlan(id))
   271	            || (cancel && !canCancel)
   272	            || (!cancel && !canRemove))
   273	        {
   274	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   275	        }
   276	
   277	        if (cancel)
   278	        {
   279	            project.Cancel(reason.Trim());
   280	        }
   281	        else
   282	        {
   283	            project.Remove(reason.Trim());
   284	        }
   285	
   286	        Raise(new BuildingRemoved(tick, id, reason.Trim()));
   287	        return Result.Success();
   288	    }
   289	
   290	    private BuildingProjectState? Find(EntityId id)
   291	    {
   292	        if (id.IsEmpty)
   293	        {
   294	            throw new ArgumentException("Building id cannot be empty.", nameof(id));
   295	        }
   296	
   297	        return _buildings.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingProjectState? value) ? value : null;
   298	    }
   299	
   300	    private static void ValidateTick(long tick)
   301	    {
   302	        if (tick < 0)
   303	        {
   304	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(tick));
   305	        }
   306	    }
   307	}
   308	}
===== src/Dig.Domain/Buildings/BuildingsState.Boxes.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Domain.Buildings
     8	{
     9	
    10	internal sealed class BuildingBoxPlanState
    11	{
    12	    public BuildingBoxPlanState(EntityId sourceStackId, EntityId jobId)
    13	    {
    14	        if (sourceStackId.IsEmpty || jobId.IsEmpty)
    15	        {
    16	            throw new ArgumentException("Box plan ids cannot be empty.");
    17	        }
    18	
    19	        SourceStackId = sourceStackId;
    20	        JobId = jobId;
    21	        CommitState = BuildingBoxCommitState.Reserved;
    22	    }
    23	
    24	    public EntityId SourceStackId { get; }
    25	
    26	    public EntityId JobId { get; }
    27	
    28	    public BuildingBoxCommitState CommitState { get; private set; }
    29	
    30	    internal static Result<BuildingBoxPlanState> Restore(
    31	        BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot snapshot,
    32	        BuildingStatus buildingStatus)
    33	    {
    34	        if (snapshot is null)
    35	        {
    36	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(snapshot));
    37	        }
    38	
    39	        bool statusMatches = snapshot.CommitState switch
    40	        {
    41	            BuildingBoxCommitState.Reserved => buildingStatus is
    42	                BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox or BuildingStatus.Cancelled,
    43	            BuildingBoxCommitState.AtSite => buildingStatus is
    44	                BuildingStatus.ReadyToBuild
    45	                or BuildingStatus.UnderConstruction
    46	                or BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete
    47	                or BuildingStatus.Cancelled,
    48	            BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed => buildingStatus is
    49	                BuildingStatus.Completed
    50	                or BuildingStatus.Damaged
    51	                or BuildingStatus.Removed,
    52	            _ => false,
    53	        };
    54	        if (!statusMatches)
    55	        {
    56	            return Result<BuildingBoxPlanState>.Failure(new DomainError(
    57	                "buildings.restore.invalid_box_commit",
    58	                "Saved box commitment does not match the building status."));
    59	        }
    60	
    61	        BuildingBoxPlanState restored = new BuildingBoxPlanState(
    62	            snapshot.SourceStackId,
    63	            snapshot.JobId)
    64	        {
    65	            CommitState = snapshot.CommitState,
    66	        };
    67	        return Result<BuildingBoxPlanState>.Success(restored);
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    public void MarkAtSite()
    71	    {
    72	        if (CommitState != BuildingBoxCommitState.Reserved)
    73	        {
    74	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Only a reserved box can be committed to site.");
    75	        }
    76	
    77	        CommitState = BuildingBoxCommitState.AtSite;
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    public void MarkConsumed()
    81	    {
    82	        if (CommitState != BuildingBoxCommitState.AtSite)
    83	        {
    84	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Only a site box can be consumed.");
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        CommitState = BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed;
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    public BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot CreateSnapshot()
    91	    {
    92	        return new BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot(SourceStackId, JobId, CommitState);
    93	    }
    94	}
    95	
    96	public sealed partial class BuildingsState
    97	{
    98	    private readonly Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingBoxPlanState> _boxPlans =
    99	        new Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingBoxPlanState>();
   100	
   101	    public static Result<BuildingsState> Restore(
   102	        IEnumerable<BuildingSnapshot> snapshots)
   103	    {
   104	        if (snapshots is null)
   105	        {
   106	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(snapshots));
   107	        }
   108	
   109	        BuildingsState buildings = new BuildingsState();
   110	        foreach (BuildingSnapshot snapshot in snapshots
   111	            .OrderBy(item => item.Id.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal))
   112	        {
   113	            if (buildings._buildings.ContainsKey(snapshot.Id))
   114	            {
   115	                return InvalidRestore("Saved buildings contain duplicate ids.");
   116	            }
   117	
   118	            bool boxPolicy = snapshot.Definition.ConstructionPolicy
   119	                == BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.BuildingBox;
   120	            if (boxPolicy != (snapshot.BoxPlan is not null))
   121	            {
   122	                return InvalidRestore(
   123	                    "Saved building box plan does not match its construction policy.");
   124	            }
   125	
   126	            Result<BuildingProjectState> project = BuildingProjectState.Restore(snapshot);
   127	            if (project.IsFailure)
   128	            {
   129	                return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(project.Error!);
   130	            }
   131	
   132	            buildings._buildings.Add(snapshot.Id, project.Value);
   133	            if (snapshot.BoxPlan is not null)
   134	            {
   135	                Result<BuildingBoxPlanState> box = BuildingBoxPlanState.Restore(
   136	                    snapshot.BoxPlan,
   137	                    snapshot.Status);
   138	                if (box.IsFailure)
   139	                {
   140	                    return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(box.Error!);
   141	                }
   142	
   143	                buildings._boxPlans.Add(snapshot.Id, box.Value);
   144	            }
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        return Result<BuildingsState>.Success(buildings);
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    public Result PlaceBoxPlan(
   151	        EntityId id,
   152	        EntityId sourceStackId,
   153	        EntityId jobId,
   154	        BuildingDefinition definition,
   155	        CellId origin,
   156	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   157	        BuildingPlacementResult placement,
   158	        long tick)
   159	    {
   160	        ValidateTick(tick);
   161	        if (id.IsEmpty || sourceStackId.IsEmpty || jobId.IsEmpty)
   162	        {
   163	            throw new ArgumentException("Building, source stack and job ids are required.");
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        if (definition is null || placement is null)
   167	        {
   168	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definition));
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        if (definition.ConstructionPolicy != BuildingConstructionPolicyKind.BuildingBox)
   172	        {
   173	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.WrongConstructionPolicy);
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        if (!placement.Succeeded)
   177	        {
   178	            return Result.Failure(placement.Error!);
   179	        }
   180	
   181	        if (_buildings.ContainsKey(id) || _boxPlans.ContainsKey(id))
   182	        {
   183	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.AlreadyExists);
   184	        }
   185	
   186	        BuildingProjectState project = new BuildingProjectState(
   187	            id,
   188	            definition,
   189	            origin,
   190	            orientation,
   191	            placement.Footprint,
   192	            placement.WorkPosition,
   193	            BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox);
   194	        _buildings.Add(id, project);
   195	        _boxPlans.Add(id, new BuildingBoxPlanState(sourceStackId, jobId));
   196	        Raise(new BuildingPlaced(tick, id, definition.Id));
   197	        Raise(new BuildingBoxPlanCreated(
   198	            tick,
   199	            id,
   200	            sourceStackId,
   201	            jobId,
   202	            definition.Id));
   203	        return Result.Success();
   204	    }
   205	
   206	    public Result MarkBoxAtSite(EntityId id, long tick)
   207	    {
   208	        ValidateTick(tick);
   209	        if (!TryGetBoxProject(id, out BuildingProjectState project, out BuildingBoxPlanState box))
   210	        {
   211	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.BoxPlanNotFound);
   212	        }
   213	
   214	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.AwaitingBox
   215	            || box.CommitState != BuildingBoxCommitState.Reserved)
   216	        {
   217	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   218	        }
   219	
   220	        BuildingBoxCommitState previous = box.CommitState;
   221	        box.MarkAtSite();
   222	        project.MarkMaterialsReady();
   223	        Raise(new BuildingBoxCommitChanged(tick, id, previous, box.CommitState));
   224	        return Result.Success();
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    public Result CompleteBoxConstruction(EntityId id, long tick)
   228	    {
   229	        ValidateTick(tick);
   230	        if (!TryGetBoxProject(id, out BuildingProjectState project, out BuildingBoxPlanState box))
   231	        {
   232	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.BoxPlanNotFound);
   233	        }
   234	
   235	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.ReadyToComplete
   236	            || box.CommitState != BuildingBoxCommitState.AtSite)
   237	        {
   238	            return Result.Failure(
   239	                project.CompletedWork < project.Definition.RequiredWork
   240	                    ? BuildingErrors.WorkIncomplete
   241	                    : BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   242	        }
   243	
   244	        BuildingBoxCommitState previous = box.CommitState;
   245	        box.MarkConsumed();
   246	        project.Complete();
   247	        Raise(new BuildingBoxCommitChanged(tick, id, previous, box.CommitState));
   248	        Raise(new BuildingCompleted(tick, id));
   249	        return Result.Success();
   250	    }
   251	
   252	    public BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? GetBoxPlan(EntityId id)
   253	    {
   254	        if (id.IsEmpty)
   255	        {
   256	            throw new ArgumentException("Building id cannot be empty.", nameof(id));
   257	        }
   258	
   259	        return _boxPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingBoxPlanState? value)
   260	            ? value.CreateSnapshot()
   261	            : null;
   262	    }
   263	
   264	    private BuildingSnapshot CreateSnapshot(BuildingProjectState project)
   265	    {
   266	        BuildingSnapshot snapshot = project.CreateSnapshot();
   267	        BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? box = _boxPlans.TryGetValue(
   268	            project.Id,
   269	            out BuildingBoxPlanState? state)
   270	            ? state.CreateSnapshot()
   271	            : null;
   272	        return new BuildingSnapshot(
   273	            snapshot.Id,
   274	            snapshot.Definition,
   275	            snapshot.Origin,
   276	            snapshot.Orientation,
   277	            snapshot.Footprint,
   278	            snapshot.WorkPosition,
   279	            snapshot.Status,
   280	            snapshot.CompletedWork,
   281	            snapshot.Durability,
   282	            snapshot.Version,
   283	            snapshot.DiagnosticReason,
   284	            box);
   285	    }
   286	
   287	    private bool TryGetBoxProject(
   288	        EntityId id,
   289	        out BuildingProjectState project,
   290	        out BuildingBoxPlanState box)
   291	    {
   292	        if (id.IsEmpty)
   293	        {
   294	            throw new ArgumentException("Building id cannot be empty.", nameof(id));
   295	        }
   296	
   297	        if (_buildings.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingProjectState? foundProject)
   298	            && _boxPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingBoxPlanState? foundBox))
   299	        {
   300	            project = foundProject;
   301	            box = foundBox;
   302	            return true;
   303	        }
   304	
   305	        project = null!;
   306	        box = null!;
   307	        return false;
   308	    }
   309	
   310	    private static Result<BuildingsState> InvalidRestore(string message)
   311	    {
   312	        return Result<BuildingsState>.Failure(new DomainError(
   313	            "buildings.restore.invalid_state",
   314	            message));
   315	    }
   316	}

===== src/Dig.Domain/Buildings/BuildingsState.Packing.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     3	
     4	namespace Dig.Domain.Buildings
     5	{
     6	
     7	internal sealed class BuildingPackingPlanState
     8	{
     9	    public BuildingPackingPlanState(EntityId jobId, EntityId outputStackId)
    10	    {
    11	        if (jobId.IsEmpty || outputStackId.IsEmpty)
    12	        {
    13	            throw new ArgumentException("Packing job and output stack ids are required.");
    14	        }
    15	
    16	        JobId = jobId;
    17	        OutputStackId = outputStackId;
    18	        CommitState = BuildingPackingCommitState.Active;
    19	    }
    20	
    21	    public EntityId JobId { get; }
    22	
    23	    public EntityId OutputStackId { get; }
    24	
    25	    public int CompletedWork { get; private set; }
    26	
    27	    public BuildingPackingCommitState CommitState { get; private set; }
    28	
    29	    internal static Result<BuildingPackingPlanState> Restore(
    30	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot snapshot,
    31	        BuildingSnapshot building)
    32	    {
    33	        if (snapshot is null || building is null)
    34	        {
    35	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(snapshot));
    36	        }
    37	
    38	        BuildingBoxPolicy? policy = building.Definition.BoxPolicy;
    39	        if (policy is null
    40	            || !policy.PackingEnabled
    41	            || snapshot.CompletedWork > policy.PackingWork)
    42	        {
    43	            return Invalid("Saved packing plan does not match its building policy.");
    44	        }
    45	
    46	        bool stateMatches = snapshot.CommitState switch
    47	        {
    48	            BuildingPackingCommitState.Active => building.Status == BuildingStatus.Completed,
    49	            BuildingPackingCommitState.Completed => building.Status == BuildingStatus.Removed
    50	                && snapshot.CompletedWork == policy.PackingWork,
    51	            BuildingPackingCommitState.Cancelled => building.Status == BuildingStatus.Completed,
    52	            _ => false,
    53	        };
    54	        if (!stateMatches)
    55	        {
    56	            return Invalid("Saved packing commitment does not match building status.");
    57	        }
    58	
    59	        BuildingPackingPlanState restored = new BuildingPackingPlanState(
    60	            snapshot.JobId,
    61	            snapshot.OutputStackId)
    62	        {
    63	            CompletedWork = snapshot.CompletedWork,
    64	            CommitState = snapshot.CommitState,
    65	        };
    66	        return Result<BuildingPackingPlanState>.Success(restored);
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    public void AddWork(int amount, int requiredWork)
    70	    {
    71	        if (CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
    72	        {
    73	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Only active packing can receive work.");
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        CompletedWork = Math.Min(requiredWork, checked(CompletedWork + amount));
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    public void Complete(int requiredWork)
    80	    {
    81	        if (CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active
    82	            || CompletedWork != requiredWork)
    83	        {
    84	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Packing work is not ready to complete.");
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        CommitState = BuildingPackingCommitState.Completed;
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    public void Cancel()
    91	    {
    92	        if (CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
    93	        {
    94	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Only active packing can be cancelled.");
    95	        }
    96	
    97	        CommitState = BuildingPackingCommitState.Cancelled;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    public BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot CreateSnapshot()
   101	    {
   102	        return new BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot(
   103	            JobId,
   104	            OutputStackId,
   105	            CompletedWork,
   106	            CommitState);
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    private static Result<BuildingPackingPlanState> Invalid(string message)
   110	    {
   111	        return Result<BuildingPackingPlanState>.Failure(new DomainError(
   112	            "buildings.restore.invalid_packing_plan",
   113	            message));
   114	    }
   115	}
   116	
   117	public sealed partial class BuildingsState
   118	{
   119	    private readonly System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingPackingPlanState>
   120	        _packingPlans = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<EntityId, BuildingPackingPlanState>();
   121	
   122	    public Result StartBoxPacking(
   123	        EntityId id,
   124	        EntityId jobId,
   125	        EntityId outputStackId,
   126	        long tick)
   127	    {
   128	        ValidateTick(tick);
   129	        if (jobId.IsEmpty || outputStackId.IsEmpty)
   130	        {
   131	            throw new ArgumentException("Packing job and output stack ids are required.");
   132	        }
   133	
   134	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   135	        if (project is null)
   136	        {
   137	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        BuildingBoxPolicy? policy = project.Definition.BoxPolicy;
   141	        if (policy is null || !policy.PackingEnabled)
   142	        {
   143	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.WrongConstructionPolicy);
   144	        }
   145	
   146	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed || HasActivePackingPlan(id))
   147	        {
   148	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        project.StartPacking();
   152	        _packingPlans[id] = new BuildingPackingPlanState(jobId, outputStackId);
   153	        Raise(new BuildingPackingStarted(tick, id, jobId, outputStackId));
   154	        return Result.Success();
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    public Result AddBoxPackingWork(EntityId id, int amount, long tick)
   158	    {
   159	        ValidateTick(tick);
   160	        if (amount <= 0)
   161	        {
   162	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));
   163	        }
   164	
   165	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   166	        if (project is null)
   167	        {
   168	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   172	            || !_packingPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingPackingPlanState? packing)
   173	            || packing.CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
   174	        {
   175	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   176	        }
   177	
   178	        int requiredWork = project.Definition.BoxPolicy!.PackingWork;
   179	        packing.AddWork(amount, requiredWork);
   180	        project.RecordPackingWork();
   181	        Raise(new BuildingPackingProgressed(
   182	            tick,
   183	            id,
   184	            packing.CompletedWork,
   185	            requiredWork));
   186	        return Result.Success();
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    public Result CompleteBoxPacking(EntityId id, long tick)
   190	    {
   191	        ValidateTick(tick);
   192	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   193	        if (project is null)
   194	        {
   195	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   196	        }
   197	
   198	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   199	            || !_packingPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingPackingPlanState? packing)
   200	            || packing.CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
   201	        {
   202	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   203	        }
   204	
   205	        int requiredWork = project.Definition.BoxPolicy!.PackingWork;
   206	        if (packing.CompletedWork != requiredWork)
   207	        {
   208	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.WorkIncomplete);
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        packing.Complete(requiredWork);
   212	        project.Remove("packed");
   213	        Raise(new BuildingPacked(tick, id, packing.OutputStackId));
   214	        Raise(new BuildingRemoved(tick, id, "packed"));
   215	        return Result.Success();
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    public Result CancelBoxPacking(EntityId id, string reason, long tick)
   219	    {
   220	        ValidateTick(tick);
   221	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reason))
   222	        {
   223	            throw new ArgumentException("Packing cancellation reason is required.", nameof(reason));
   224	        }
   225	
   226	        BuildingProjectState? project = Find(id);
   227	        if (project is null)
   228	        {
   229	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.NotFound);
   230	        }
   231	
   232	        if (project.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   233	            || !_packingPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingPackingPlanState? packing)
   234	            || packing.CommitState != BuildingPackingCommitState.Active)
   235	        {
   236	            return Result.Failure(BuildingErrors.InvalidStatus);
   237	        }
   238	
   239	        packing.Cancel();
   240	        project.CancelPacking(reason.Trim());
   241	        Raise(new BuildingPackingCancelled(tick, id, reason.Trim()));
   242	        return Result.Success();
   243	    }
   244	
   245	    public BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? GetPackingPlan(EntityId id)
   246	    {
   247	        if (id.IsEmpty)
   248	        {
   249	            throw new ArgumentException("Building id cannot be empty.", nameof(id));
   250	        }
   251	
   252	        return _packingPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingPackingPlanState? value)
   253	            ? value.CreateSnapshot()
   254	            : null;
   255	    }
   256	
   257	    private BuildingSnapshot CreateCombinedSnapshot(BuildingProjectState project)
   258	    {
   259	        BuildingSnapshot snapshot = project.CreateSnapshot();
   260	        BuildingBoxPlanSnapshot? box = GetBoxPlan(project.Id);
   261	        BuildingPackingPlanSnapshot? packing = _packingPlans.TryGetValue(
   262	            project.Id,
   263	            out BuildingPackingPlanState? state)
   264	            ? state.CreateSnapshot()
   265	            : null;
   266	        return new BuildingSnapshot(
   267	            snapshot.Id,
   268	            snapshot.Definition,
   269	            snapshot.Origin,
   270	            snapshot.Orientation,
   271	            snapshot.Footprint,
   272	            snapshot.WorkPosition,
   273	            snapshot.Status,
   274	            snapshot.CompletedWork,
   275	            snapshot.Durability,
   276	            snapshot.Version,
   277	            snapshot.DiagnosticReason,
   278	            box,
   279	            packing);
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    private bool HasActivePackingPlan(EntityId id)
   283	    {
   284	        return _packingPlans.TryGetValue(id, out BuildingPackingPlanState? packing)
   285	            && packing.CommitState == BuildingPackingCommitState.Active;
   286	    }
   287	}
   288	}
===== src/Dig.Domain/Jobs/BarrelAttackJobDefinition.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;

     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Domain.Jobs
     8	{
     9	
    10	public sealed class BarrelAttackJobDefinition : JobDefinition
    11	{
    12	    private static readonly JobStageKind[] WorkStages =
    13	    {
    14	        JobStageKind.TravelToTarget,
    15	        JobStageKind.PerformWork,
    16	        JobStageKind.Finalize,
    17	    };
    18	
    19	    public BarrelAttackJobDefinition(
    20	        EntityId id,
    21	        EntityId barrelId,
    22	        CellId targetCell,
    23	        CellId workPosition,
    24	        long barrelVersion,
    25	        long contentsGeneration,
    26	        int priority,
    27	        long createdTick,
    28	        JobRetryPolicy retryPolicy,
    29	        IEnumerable<EntityId>? dependencies = null)
    30	        : base(id, priority, createdTick, retryPolicy, WorkStages, dependencies)
    31	    {
    32	        if (barrelId.IsEmpty)
    33	        {
    34	            throw new ArgumentException("Barrel id cannot be empty.", nameof(barrelId));
    35	        }
    36	
    37	        if (barrelVersion < 0 || contentsGeneration < 0)
    38	        {
    39	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(barrelVersion));
    40	        }
    41	
    42	        BarrelId = barrelId;
    43	        TargetCell = targetCell;
    44	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
    45	        BarrelVersion = barrelVersion;
    46	        ContentsGeneration = contentsGeneration;
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    public EntityId BarrelId { get; }
    50	    public CellId TargetCell { get; }
    51	    public CellId WorkPosition { get; }
    52	    public long BarrelVersion { get; }
    53	    public long ContentsGeneration { get; }
    54	
    55	    public override string Description => $"AttackBarrel:{BarrelId}@{TargetCell}";
    56	
    57	    public override IReadOnlyList<ReservationKey> CreateReservationKeys()
    58	    {
    59	        // The barrel target intentionally has no exclusive reservation: multiple residents
    60	        // may attack it concurrently and the first authoritative destruction commit wins.
    61	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<ReservationKey>(new[]
    62	        {
    63	            ReservationKey.ForPosition(WorkPosition),
    64	        });
    65	    }
    66	}
    67

===== src/Dig.Domain/Jobs/BuildingSupplyJobDefinition.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Domain.Jobs
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class BuildingSupplyJobDefinition : JobDefinition
    13	{
    14	    private static readonly JobStageKind[] SupplyStages =
    15	    {
    16	        JobStageKind.TravelToTarget,
    17	        JobStageKind.AcquireItem,
    18	        JobStageKind.TravelToDestination,
    19	        JobStageKind.DepositItem,
    20	    };
    21	
    22	    private readonly ItemReservationAllocation[] _allocations;
    23	    private readonly EntityId[] _transitStackIds;
    24	    private readonly EntityId[] _depositStackIds;
    25	
    26	    public BuildingSupplyJobDefinition(
    27	        EntityId id,
    28	        EntityId buildingId,
    29	        CellId workPosition,
    30	        IEnumerable<ItemReservationAllocation> allocations,
    31	        IEnumerable<EntityId> transitStackIds,
    32	        IEnumerable<EntityId> depositStackIds,
    33	        int priority,
    34	        long createdTick,
    35	        JobRetryPolicy retryPolicy)
    36	        : base(id, priority, createdTick, retryPolicy, SupplyStages, dependencies: null)
    37	    {
    38	        if (buildingId.IsEmpty || allocations is null)
    39	        {
    40	            throw new ArgumentException("Building and supply allocations are required.");
    41	        }
    42	
    43	        _allocations = allocations
    44	            .OrderBy(value => value.ItemId)
    45	            .ThenBy(value => value.StackId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
    46	            .ToArray();
    47	        if (_allocations.Length == 0
    48	            || _allocations.Select(value => value.StackId).Distinct().Count()
    49	                != _allocations.Length)
    50	        {
    51	            throw new ArgumentException(
    52	                "Building supply allocations must be non-empty and source-unique.",
    53	                nameof(allocations));
    54	        }
    55	
    56	        _transitStackIds = NormalizeIds(
    57	            transitStackIds,
    58	            nameof(transitStackIds),
    59	            allowEmpty: true);
    60	        _depositStackIds = NormalizeIds(
    61	            depositStackIds,
    62	            nameof(depositStackIds),
    63	            allowEmpty: false);
    64	        if (_depositStackIds.Length != _allocations.Select(value => value.ItemId)
    65	            .Distinct().Count())
    66	        {
    67	            throw new ArgumentException(
    68	                "One deposit stack id is required per supplied item type.",
    69	                nameof(depositStackIds));
    70	        }
    71	
    72	        BuildingId = buildingId;
    73	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    public EntityId BuildingId { get; }
    77	    public CellId WorkPosition { get; }
    78	
    79	    public IReadOnlyList<ItemReservationAllocation> Allocations =>
    80	        new ReadOnlyCollection<ItemReservationAllocation>(_allocations);
    81	
    82	    public IReadOnlyList<EntityId> TransitStackIds =>
    83	        new ReadOnlyCollection<EntityId>(_transitStackIds);
    84	
    85	    public IReadOnlyList<EntityId> DepositStackIds =>
    86	        new ReadOnlyCollection<EntityId>(_depositStackIds);
    87	
    88	
    89	    private static EntityId[] NormalizeIds(
    90	        IEnumerable<EntityId> ids,
    91	        string parameterName,
    92	        bool allowEmpty)
    93	    {
    94	        if (ids is null)
    95	        {
    96	            throw new ArgumentNullException(parameterName);
    97	        }
    98	
    99	        EntityId[] values = ids.ToArray();
   100	        if ((!allowEmpty && values.Length == 0)
   101	            || values.Any(value => value.IsEmpty)
   102	            || values.Distinct().Count() != values.Length)
   103	        {
   104	            throw new ArgumentException(
   105	                "Stack ids must be non-empty and unique.",
   106	                parameterName);
   107	        }
   108	
   109	        return values;
   110	    }
   111	
   112	    public override string Description =>
   113	        $"SupplyBuilding:{BuildingId}:{_allocations.Sum(value => value.Quantity)}";
   114	
   115	    public override IReadOnlyList<ReservationKey> CreateReservationKeys()
   116	    {
   117	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<ReservationKey>(new[]
   118	        {
   119	            ReservationKey.ForDestination(BuildingId),
   120	            ReservationKey.ForPosition(WorkPosition),
   121	        });
   122	    }
   123	}
   124	
   125	}

    29	    public BuildingWorkJobDefinition(

    35	        long createdTick,

    68	        {
    69	            ReservationKey.ForPosition(WorkPosition),
    70	        });
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    private static SkillGrantProfile CreateSkillProfile(BuildingWorkKind kind)
    74	    {
    75	        return kind == BuildingWorkKind.Construction
    76	            ? DefaultSkillProgressionContent.Catalog.GetProfile(

    78	            : DefaultSkillProgressionContent.Catalog.GetProfile(
    79	                DefaultSkillGrantProfileIds.Logistics);

===== src/Dig.Domain/Navigation/NavigationOpenSet.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Domain.World;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Domain.Navigation
     6	{
     7	
     8	internal readonly struct NavigationSearchCost
     9	    : IComparable<NavigationSearchCost>, IEquatable<NavigationSearchCost>
    10	{
    11	    public NavigationSearchCost(int shaftGapCount, int movementCost)
    12	    {
    13	        if (shaftGapCount < 0)
    14	        {
    15	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(shaftGapCount));
    16	        }
    17	
    18	        if (movementCost < 0)
    19	        {
    20	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(movementCost));
    21	        }
    22	
    23	        ShaftGapCount = shaftGapCount;
    24	        MovementCost = movementCost;
    25	    }
    26	
    27	    public int ShaftGapCount { get; }
    28	
    29	    public int MovementCost { get; }
    30	
    31	    public NavigationSearchCost Advance(NavigationTransition transition)
    32	    {
    33	        return new NavigationSearchCost(
    34	            checked(ShaftGapCount
    35	                + (transition.TraversalKind == TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse
    36	                    ? 1
    37	                    : 0)),
    38	            checked(MovementCost + transition.Cost));
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    public int CompareTo(NavigationSearchCost other)
    42	    {
    43	        int gap = ShaftGapCount.CompareTo(other.ShaftGapCount);
    44	        return gap != 0 ? gap : MovementCost.CompareTo(other.MovementCost);
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    public bool Equals(NavigationSearchCost other)
    48	    {
    49	        return ShaftGapCount == other.ShaftGapCount
    50	            && MovementCost == other.MovementCost;
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    public override bool Equals(object? obj)
    54	    {
    55	        return obj is NavigationSearchCost other && Equals(other);
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    public override int GetHashCode()
    59	    {
    60	        return HashCode.Combine(ShaftGapCount, MovementCost);
    61	    }
    62	}
    63	
    64	internal readonly struct NavigationOpenNode
    65	{
    66	    public NavigationOpenNode(
    67	        CellId cell,
    68	        NavigationSearchCost cost,
    69	        int heuristic)
    70	    {
    71	        if (heuristic < 0)
    72	        {
    73	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(heuristic));
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        Cell = cell;
    77	        Cost = cost;
    78	        Heuristic = heuristic;
    79	    }
    80	
    81	    public CellId Cell { get; }
    82	
    83	    public NavigationSearchCost Cost { get; }
    84	
    85	    public int Heuristic { get; }
    86	}
    87	
    88	internal sealed class NavigationOpenSet
    89	{
    90	    private readonly List<NavigationOpenNode> _heap = new List<NavigationOpenNode>();
    91	
    92	    public int Count => _heap.Count;
    93	
    94	    public void Push(NavigationOpenNode node)
    95	    {
    96	        _heap.Add(node);
    97	        int index = _heap.Count - 1;
    98	        while (index > 0)
    99	        {
   100	            int parent = (index - 1) / 2;
   101	            if (Compare(_heap[parent], _heap[index]) <= 0)
   102	            {
   103	                break;
   104	            }
   105	
   106	            Swap(parent, index);
   107	            index = parent;
   108	        }
   109	    }
   110	
   111	    public NavigationOpenNode Pop()
   112	    {
   113	        if (_heap.Count == 0)
   114	        {
   115	            throw new InvalidOperationException("The navigation open set is empty.");
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        NavigationOpenNode result = _heap[0];
   119	        int lastIndex = _heap.Count - 1;
   120	        _heap[0] = _heap[lastIndex];
   121	        _heap.RemoveAt(lastIndex);
   122	
   123	        int index = 0;
   124	        while (true)
   125	        {
   126	            int left = (index * 2) + 1;
   127	            int right = left + 1;
   128	            int smallest = index;
   129	
   130	            if (left < _heap.Count
   131	                && Compare(_heap[left], _heap[smallest]) < 0)
   132	            {
   133	                smallest = left;
   134	            }
   135	
   136	            if (right < _heap.Count
   137	                && Compare(_heap[right], _heap[smallest]) < 0)
   138	            {
   139	                smallest = right;
   140	            }
   141	
   142	            if (smallest == index)
   143	            {
   144	                break;
   145	            }
   146	
   147	            Swap(index, smallest);
   148	            index = smallest;
   149	        }
   150	
   151	        return result;
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    private static int Compare(
   155	        NavigationOpenNode left,
   156	        NavigationOpenNode right)
   157	    {
   158	        int cost = left.Cost.CompareTo(right.Cost);
   159	        if (cost != 0)
   160	        {
   161	            return cost;
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        int heuristic = left.Heuristic.CompareTo(right.Heuristic);
   165	        if (heuristic != 0)
   166	        {
   167	            return heuristic;
   168	        }
   169	
   170	        return left.Cell.CompareTo(right.Cell);
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    private void Swap(int left, int right)
   174	    {
   175	        NavigationOpenNode value = _heap[left];
   176	        _heap[left] = _heap[right];
   177	        _heap[right] = value;
   178	    }
   179	}
   180	}
===== src/Dig.Domain/Navigation/NavigationSnapshot.Transitions.cs =====
     1	using System.Collections.Generic;
     2	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     3	using System.Linq;
     4	using Dig.Domain.World;
     5	
     6	namespace Dig.Domain.Navigation
     7	{
     8	
     9	public sealed partial class NavigationSnapshot
    10	{
    11	    private void AddNormalTransitions(
    12	        CellId from,
    13	        Dictionary<CellId, NavigationTransition> transitions)
    14	    {
    15	        AddIfWalkable(
    16	            transitions,
    17	            from,
    18	            new CellId(from.X - 1, from.Y, from.Z),
    19	            Profile.OrthogonalCost);
    20	        AddIfWalkable(
    21	            transitions,
    22	            from,
    23	            new CellId(from.X + 1, from.Y, from.Z),
    24	            Profile.OrthogonalCost);
    25	        AddIfWalkable(
    26	            transitions,
    27	            from,
    28	            new CellId(from.X, from.Y, from.Z - 1),
    29	            Profile.OrthogonalCost);
    30	        AddIfWalkable(
    31	            transitions,
    32	            from,
    33	            new CellId(from.X, from.Y, from.Z + 1),
    34	            Profile.OrthogonalCost);
    35	
    36	        if (Profile.Mode == TraversalMode.Free)
    37	        {
    38	            AddIfWalkable(
    39	                transitions,
    40	                from,
    41	                new CellId(from.X, from.Y - 1, from.Z),
    42	                Profile.OrthogonalCost);
    43	            AddIfWalkable(
    44	                transitions,
    45	                from,
    46	                new CellId(from.X, from.Y + 1, from.Z),
    47	                Profile.OrthogonalCost);
    48	            return;
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        foreach (int direction in new[] { -1, 1 })
    52	        {
    53	            for (int step = 1; step <= Profile.MaxStepUp; step++)
    54	            {
    55	                AddIfWalkable(
    56	                    transitions,
    57	                    from,
    58	                    new CellId(from.X + direction, from.Y + step, from.Z),
    59	                    checked(Profile.StepCost * step));
    60	            }
    61	
    62	            for (int step = 1; step <= Profile.MaxStepDown; step++)
    63	            {
    64	                AddIfWalkable(
    65	                    transitions,
    66	                    from,
    67	                    new CellId(from.X + direction, from.Y - step, from.Z),
    68	                    checked(Profile.StepCost * step));
    69	            }
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    private void AddIfWalkable(
    74	        Dictionary<CellId, NavigationTransition> transitions,
    75	        CellId from,
    76	        CellId target,
    77	        int cost)
    78	    {
    79	        if (IsWalkable(target))
    80	        {
    81	            AddLowestCost(
    82	                transitions,
    83	                new NavigationTransition(
    84	                    target,
    85	                    cost,
    86	                    traversalKind: ClassifyNormalTraversal(from, target)));
    87	        }
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    private TunnelTraversalKind ClassifyNormalTraversal(
    91	        CellId from,
    92	        CellId to)
    93	    {
    94	        int deltaX = System.Math.Abs(to.X - from.X);
    95	        int deltaY = System.Math.Abs(to.Y - from.Y);
    96	        int deltaZ = System.Math.Abs(to.Z - from.Z);
    97	        if (deltaZ != 0 && deltaX == 0 && deltaY == 0)
    98	        {
    99	            return TunnelTraversalKind.DepthTraverse;
   100	        }
   101	
   102	        bool shaftGap = IsShaftGapCell(from) || IsShaftGapCell(to);
   103	        if (deltaY != 0 && deltaX == 0 && deltaZ == 0 && shaftGap)
   104	        {
   105	            return TunnelTraversalKind.VerticalClimb;
   106	        }
   107	
   108	        if (deltaX != 0 && deltaY == 0 && deltaZ == 0 && shaftGap)
   109	        {
   110	            return TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse;
   111	        }
   112	
   113	        return TunnelTraversalKind.SupportedWalk;
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    private static void AddLowestCost(
   117	        Dictionary<CellId, NavigationTransition> transitions,
   118	        NavigationTransition candidate)
   119	    {
   120	        if (!transitions.TryGetValue(
   121	            candidate.Target,
   122	            out NavigationTransition existing)
   123	            || candidate.Cost < existing.Cost)
   124	        {
   125	            transitions[candidate.Target] = candidate;
   126	        }
   127	    }
   128	
   129	    private static Dictionary<CellId, IReadOnlyList<NavigationTransition>>
   130	        BuildLinkTransitions(
   131	            IReadOnlyList<TraversalLink> links,
   132	            TraversalProfile profile)
   133	    {
   134	        Dictionary<CellId, List<NavigationTransition>> mutable =
   135	            new Dictionary<CellId, List<NavigationTransition>>();
   136	        foreach (TraversalLink link in links)
   137	        {
   138	            if (!profile.Allows(link.Kind))
   139	            {
   140	                continue;
   141	            }
   142	
   143	            AddLink(mutable, link.From, link.To, link);
   144	            if (link.Bidirectional)
   145	            {
   146	                AddLink(mutable, link.To, link.From, link);
   147	            }
   148	        }
   149	
   150	        return mutable.ToDictionary(
   151	            pair => pair.Key,
   152	            pair => (IReadOnlyList<NavigationTransition>)new ReadOnlyCollection<NavigationTransition>(
   153	                pair.Value
   154	                    .OrderBy(transition => transition.Target)
   155	                    .ThenBy(transition => transition.Cost)
   156	                    .ToArray()));
   157	    }
   158	
   159	    private static void AddLink(
   160	        Dictionary<CellId, List<NavigationTransition>> transitions,
   161	        CellId from,
   162	        CellId to,
   163	        TraversalLink link)
   164	    {
   165	        if (!transitions.TryGetValue(from, out List<NavigationTransition>? list))
   166	        {
   167	            list = new List<NavigationTransition>();
   168	            transitions.Add(from, list);
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        list.Add(new NavigationTransition(to, link.Cost, link.Kind));
   172	    }
   173	
   174	}
   175	
   176	}
===== src/Dig.Domain/Navigation/TunnelNavigationVolume.Pathfinding.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Domain.World;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Domain.Navigation
     6	{
     7	public sealed partial class TunnelNavigationVolume
     8	{
     9	    public TunnelPathResult FindPath(CellId start, CellId goal)
    10	    {
    11	        if (!Contains(start))
    12	        {
    13	            return TunnelPathResult.Failure(
    14	                TunnelPathFailureReason.InvalidStart,
    15	                "The resident start cell is outside the tunnel volume.");
    16	        }
    17	
    18	        if (!Contains(goal))
    19	        {
    20	            return TunnelPathResult.Failure(
    21	                TunnelPathFailureReason.InvalidGoal,
    22	                "The requested destination is outside the tunnel volume.");
    23	        }
    24	
    25	        if (!IsOpen(start))
    26	        {
    27	            return TunnelPathResult.Failure(
    28	                TunnelPathFailureReason.BlockedStart,
    29	                "The resident is not standing in an open tunnel cell.");
    30	        }
    31	
    32	        if (!IsOpen(goal))
    33	        {
    34	            return TunnelPathResult.Failure(
    35	                TunnelPathFailureReason.BlockedGoal,
    36	                "The requested destination is not an open tunnel cell.");
    37	        }
    38	
    39	        if (start == goal)
    40	        {
    41	            return TunnelPathResult.Success(
    42	                new TunnelPath(
    43	                    new[] { start },
    44	                    Array.Empty<TunnelTraversalKind>()));
    45	        }
    46	
    47	        List<CellId> frontier = new List<CellId> { start };
    48	        Dictionary<CellId, PathCost> costs = new Dictionary<CellId, PathCost>
    49	        {
    50	            [start] = new PathCost(0, 0),
    51	        };
    52	        Dictionary<CellId, CellId> previous =
    53	            new Dictionary<CellId, CellId>();
    54	        while (frontier.Count > 0)
    55	        {
    56	            int currentIndex = FindLowestCostIndex(frontier, costs);
    57	            CellId current = frontier[currentIndex];
    58	            frontier.RemoveAt(currentIndex);
    59	            if (current == goal)
    60	            {
    61	                return TunnelPathResult.Success(Reconstruct(previous, start, goal));
    62	            }
    63	
    64	            foreach (CellId neighbor in GetNeighbors(current))
    65	            {
    66	                TunnelTraversalKind kind = ClassifyTraversal(current, neighbor);
    67	                PathCost candidate = costs[current].Advance(kind);
    68	                if (costs.TryGetValue(neighbor, out PathCost known)
    69	                    && known.CompareTo(candidate) <= 0)
    70	                {
    71	                    continue;
    72	                }
    73	
    74	                costs[neighbor] = candidate;
    75	                previous[neighbor] = current;
    76	                if (!frontier.Contains(neighbor))
    77	                {
    78	                    frontier.Add(neighbor);
    79	                }
    80	            }
    81	        }
    82	
    83	        return TunnelPathResult.Failure(
    84	            TunnelPathFailureReason.Unreachable,
    85	            "No route connects the resident to the requested tunnel cell.");
    86	    }
    87	
    88	    private IEnumerable<CellId> GetNeighbors(CellId cell)
    89	    {
    90	        CellId[] candidates =
    91	        {
    92	            new CellId(cell.X - 1, cell.Y, cell.Z),
    93	            new CellId(cell.X + 1, cell.Y, cell.Z),
    94	            new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y, cell.Z - 1),
    95	            new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y, cell.Z + 1),
    96	            new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y - 1, cell.Z),
    97	            new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y + 1, cell.Z),
    98	        };
    99	        for (int index = 0; index < candidates.Length; index++)
   100	        {
   101	            if (CanTraverseStep(cell, candidates[index]))
   102	            {
   103	                yield return candidates[index];
   104	            }
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	
   108	    private TunnelPath Reconstruct(
   109	        IReadOnlyDictionary<CellId, CellId> previous,
   110	        CellId start,
   111	        CellId goal)
   112	    {
   113	        List<CellId> reverse = new List<CellId> { goal };
   114	        CellId current = goal;
   115	        while (current != start)
   116	        {
   117	            current = previous[current];
   118	            reverse.Add(current);
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        reverse.Reverse();
   122	        List<TunnelTraversalKind> traversalKinds =
   123	            new List<TunnelTraversalKind>(reverse.Count - 1);
   124	        for (int index = 0; index < reverse.Count - 1; index++)
   125	        {
   126	            traversalKinds.Add(ClassifyTraversal(reverse[index], reverse[index + 1]));
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        return new TunnelPath(reverse, traversalKinds);
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    private static int FindLowestCostIndex(
   133	        IReadOnlyList<CellId> frontier,
   134	        IReadOnlyDictionary<CellId, PathCost> costs)
   135	    {
   136	        int bestIndex = 0;
   137	        for (int index = 1; index < frontier.Count; index++)
   138	        {
   139	            int comparison = costs[frontier[index]].CompareTo(costs[frontier[bestIndex]]);
   140	            if (comparison < 0
   141	                || (comparison == 0 && frontier[index].CompareTo(frontier[bestIndex]) < 0))
   142	            {
   143	                bestIndex = index;
   144	            }
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        return bestIndex;
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    private readonly struct PathCost : IComparable<PathCost>
   151	    {
   152	        internal PathCost(int shaftGapCount, int stepCount)
   153	        {
   154	            ShaftGapCount = shaftGapCount;
   155	            StepCount = stepCount;
   156	        }
   157	
   158	        internal int ShaftGapCount { get; }
   159	
   160	        internal int StepCount { get; }
   161	
   162	        internal PathCost Advance(TunnelTraversalKind kind)
   163	        {
   164	            return new PathCost(
   165	                ShaftGapCount + (kind == TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse ? 1 : 0),
   166	                checked(StepCount + 1));
   167	        }
   168	
   169	        public int CompareTo(PathCost other)
   170	        {
   171	            int gap = ShaftGapCount.CompareTo(other.ShaftGapCount);
   172	            return gap != 0 ? gap : StepCount.CompareTo(other.StepCount);
   173	        }
   174	    }
   175	}
   176	}
===== src/Dig.Domain/Navigation/TunnelNavigationVolume.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Domain.Navigation
     8	{
     9	
    10	public enum TunnelPathFailureReason
    11	{
    12	    None = 0,
    13	    InvalidStart = 1,
    14	    InvalidGoal = 2,
    15	    BlockedStart = 3,
    16	    BlockedGoal = 4,
    17	    Unreachable = 5,
    18	}
    19	
    20	public enum TunnelTraversalKind
    21	{
    22	    Invalid = 0,
    23	    SupportedWalk = 1,
    24	    VerticalClimb = 2,
    25	    ShaftGapTraverse = 3,
    26	    DepthTraverse = 4,
    27	}
    28	
    29	public sealed class TunnelPath
    30	{
    31	    public TunnelPath(IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> cells)
    32	        : this(
    33	            cells,
    34	            Enumerable.Repeat(
    35	                TunnelTraversalKind.SupportedWalk,
    36	                Math.Max(0, (cells ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(cells))).Count - 1))
    37	                .ToArray())
    38	    {
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    public TunnelPath(
    42	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> cells,
    43	        IReadOnlyCollection<TunnelTraversalKind> traversalKinds)
    44	    {
    45	        if (cells is null)
    46	        {
    47	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(cells));
    48	        }
    49	
    50	        if (traversalKinds is null)
    51	        {
    52	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(traversalKinds));
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        if (cells.Count == 0)
    56	        {
    57	            throw new ArgumentException(
    58	                "A tunnel path requires at least one cell.",
    59	                nameof(cells));
    60	        }
    61	
    62	        if (traversalKinds.Count != cells.Count - 1
    63	            || traversalKinds.Any(value => value == TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid
    64	                || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TunnelTraversalKind), value)))
    65	        {
    66	            throw new ArgumentException(
    67	                "Tunnel traversal kinds must describe every path edge.",
    68	                nameof(traversalKinds));
    69	        }
    70	
    71	        Cells = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(cells.ToArray());
    72	        TraversalKinds = new ReadOnlyCollection<TunnelTraversalKind>(
    73	            traversalKinds.ToArray());
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> Cells { get; }
    77	
    78	    public IReadOnlyList<TunnelTraversalKind> TraversalKinds { get; }
    79	
    80	    public TunnelTraversalKind GetTraversalKind(int fromCellIndex)
    81	    {
    82	        if (fromCellIndex < 0 || fromCellIndex >= TraversalKinds.Count)
    83	        {
    84	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(fromCellIndex));
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        return TraversalKinds[fromCellIndex];
    88	    }
    89	}
    90	
    91	public sealed class TunnelPathResult
    92	{
    93	    private TunnelPathResult(
    94	        TunnelPath? path,
    95	        TunnelPathFailureReason failureReason,
    96	        string detail)
    97	    {
    98	        Path = path;
    99	        FailureReason = failureReason;
   100	        Detail = detail;
   101	    }
   102	
   103	    public bool Succeeded => Path != null;
   104	
   105	    public TunnelPath? Path { get; }
   106	
   107	    public TunnelPathFailureReason FailureReason { get; }
   108	
   109	    public string Detail { get; }
   110	
   111	    public static TunnelPathResult Success(TunnelPath path)
   112	    {
   113	        return new TunnelPathResult(
   114	            path ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(path)),
   115	            TunnelPathFailureReason.None,
   116	            "A deterministic tunnel route was found.");
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    public static TunnelPathResult Failure(
   120	        TunnelPathFailureReason reason,
   121	        string detail)
   122	    {
   123	        if (reason == TunnelPathFailureReason.None)
   124	        {
   125	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(reason));
   126	        }
   127	
   128	        return new TunnelPathResult(null, reason, detail);
   129	    }
   130	}
   131	
   132	public sealed partial class TunnelNavigationVolume
   133	{
   134	    private readonly HashSet<CellId> _openCells;
   135	    private readonly HashSet<CellId> _verticalCells;
   136	    private readonly HashSet<CellId> _supportedCells;
   137	    private readonly HashSet<CellId> _shaftGapCells;
   138	
   139	    public TunnelNavigationVolume(
   140	        int width,
   141	        int height,
   142	        int depth,
   143	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> openCells,
   144	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> verticalCells,
   145	        TunnelDemoLayout? demoLayout = null)
   146	        : this(
   147	            width,
   148	            height,
   149	            depth,
   150	            openCells,
   151	            verticalCells,
   152	            openCells,
   153	            demoLayout)
   154	    {
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    public TunnelNavigationVolume(
   158	        int width,
   159	        int height,
   160	        int depth,
   161	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> openCells,
   162	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> verticalCells,
   163	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> supportedCells,
   164	        TunnelDemoLayout? demoLayout = null)
   165	    {
   166	        if (width <= 0)
   167	        {
   168	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(width));
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        if (height <= 0)
   172	        {
   173	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(height));
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        if (depth <= 0)
   177	        {
   178	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(depth));
   179	        }
   180	
   181	        if (openCells is null)
   182	        {
   183	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(openCells));
   184	        }
   185	
   186	        if (verticalCells is null)
   187	        {
   188	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(verticalCells));
   189	        }
   190	
   191	        if (supportedCells is null)
   192	        {
   193	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(supportedCells));
   194	        }
   195	
   196	        Width = width;
   197	        Height = height;
   198	        Depth = depth;
   199	        DemoLayout = demoLayout;
   200	        _openCells = new HashSet<CellId>(openCells);
   201	        _verticalCells = new HashSet<CellId>(verticalCells);
   202	        _supportedCells = new HashSet<CellId>(supportedCells);
   203	        ValidateCells(_openCells, nameof(openCells));
   204	        ValidateCells(_verticalCells, nameof(verticalCells));
   205	        ValidateCells(_supportedCells, nameof(supportedCells));
   206	        if (!_verticalCells.IsSubsetOf(_openCells))
   207	        {
   208	            throw new ArgumentException(
   209	                "Vertical tunnel cells must also be open.",
   210	                nameof(verticalCells));
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        if (!_supportedCells.IsSubsetOf(_openCells))
   214	        {
   215	            throw new ArgumentException(
   216	                "Supported tunnel cells must also be open.",
   217	                nameof(supportedCells));
   218	        }
   219	
   220	        _shaftGapCells = new HashSet<CellId>(
   221	            _openCells.Where(cell => !_supportedCells.Contains(cell)
   222	                && IsVerticalTopologyCell(cell)));
   223	
   224	        Cells = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(
   225	            _openCells.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray());
   226	        VerticalCells = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(
   227	            _verticalCells.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray());
   228	        SupportedCells = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(
   229	            _supportedCells.OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray());
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    public int Width { get; }
   233	
   234	    public int Height { get; }
   235	
   236	    public int Depth { get; }
   237	
   238	    public TunnelDemoLayout? DemoLayout { get; }
   239	
   240	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> Cells { get; }
   241	
   242	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> VerticalCells { get; }
   243	
   244	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> SupportedCells { get; }
   245	
   246	    public bool Contains(CellId cell)
   247	    {
   248	        return cell.X >= 0
   249	            && cell.Y >= 0
   250	            && cell.Z >= 0
   251	            && cell.X < Width
   252	            && cell.Y < Height
   253	            && cell.Z < Depth;
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    public bool IsOpen(CellId cell)
   257	    {
   258	        return _openCells.Contains(cell);
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    public bool IsVerticalTunnel(CellId cell)
   262	    {
   263	        return _verticalCells.Contains(cell);
   264	    }
   265	
   266	    public bool HasFullActorSupport(CellId cell)
   267	    {
   268	        return _supportedCells.Contains(cell);
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    public bool IsShaftGapCell(CellId cell)
   272	    {
   273	        return _shaftGapCells.Contains(cell);
   274	    }
   275	
   276	    public TunnelTraversalKind ClassifyTraversal(CellId from, CellId to)
   277	    {
   278	        if (!IsOpen(from) || !IsOpen(to))
   279	        {
   280	            return TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid;
   281	        }
   282	
   283	        int deltaX = Math.Abs(to.X - from.X);
   284	        int deltaY = Math.Abs(to.Y - from.Y);
   285	        int deltaZ = Math.Abs(to.Z - from.Z);
   286	        if (deltaX + deltaY + deltaZ != 1)
   287	        {
   288	            return TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid;
   289	        }
   290	
   291	        if (deltaY != 0)
   292	        {
   293	            return deltaX == 0
   294	                && deltaZ == 0
   295	                && (IsVerticalTunnel(from) || IsVerticalTunnel(to))
   296	                    ? TunnelTraversalKind.VerticalClimb
   297	                    : TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid;
   298	        }
   299	
   300	        if (deltaZ != 0)
   301	        {
   302	            return TunnelTraversalKind.DepthTraverse;
   303	        }
   304	
   305	        bool crossesShaftGap = IsShaftGapCell(from) || IsShaftGapCell(to);
   306	        return crossesShaftGap
   307	            ? TunnelTraversalKind.ShaftGapTraverse
   308	            : TunnelTraversalKind.SupportedWalk;
   309	    }
   310	
   311	    private bool IsVerticalTopologyCell(CellId cell)
   312	    {
   313	        if (_verticalCells.Contains(cell))
   314	        {
   315	            return true;
   316	        }
   317	
   318	        CellId above = new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y - 1, cell.Z);
   319	        CellId below = new CellId(cell.X, cell.Y + 1, cell.Z);
   320	        return (Contains(above) && _verticalCells.Contains(above))
   321	            || (Contains(below) && _verticalCells.Contains(below));
   322	    }
   323	
   324	    public bool CanTraverseStep(CellId from, CellId to)
   325	    {
   326	        return ClassifyTraversal(from, to) != TunnelTraversalKind.Invalid;
   327	    }
   328	
   329	    private void ValidateCells(IEnumerable<CellId> cells, string parameterName)
   330	    {
   331	        foreach (CellId cell in cells)
   332	        {
   333	            if (!Contains(cell))
   334	            {
   335	                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(parameterName);
   336	            }
   337	        }
   338	    }
   339	
   340	
   341	}
   342	
   343	}

    65	
    66	public sealed class ProductionOrderSnapshot
    67	{
    68	    public ProductionOrderSnapshot(
    69	        EntityId id,
    70	        RecipeDefinition recipe,
    71	        EntityId buildingId,
    72	        long sequence,
    73	        ProductionOrderStatus status,
    74	        int completedWork,
    75	        long version,
    76	        IReadOnlyCollection<ItemReservationAllocation> inputAllocations,
    77	        string? reason,
    78	        IReadOnlyCollection<ProductionMaterialStepSnapshot>? materialSteps = null)
    79	    {
    80	        Id = id;
    81	        Recipe = recipe ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(recipe));
    82	        BuildingId = buildingId;
    83	        Sequence = sequence;
    84	        Status = status;
    85	        CompletedWork = completedWork;
    86	        Version = version;
    87	        InputAllocations = new ReadOnlyCollection<ItemReservationAllocation>(
    88	            inputAllocations.OrderBy(value => value.StackId.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
    89	                .ToArray());
    90	        Reason = reason;
    91	        MaterialSteps = new ReadOnlyCollection<ProductionMaterialStepSnapshot>(
    92	            (materialSteps ?? Array.Empty<ProductionMaterialStepSnapshot>())
    93	                .OrderBy(value => value.Index)
    94	                .ToArray());
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    public EntityId Id { get; }
    98	
    99	    public RecipeDefinition Recipe { get; }
   100	
   101	    public EntityId BuildingId { get; }
   102	
   103	    public long Sequence { get; }
   104	
   105	    public ProductionOrderStatus Status { get; }
   106	
   107	    public int CompletedWork { get; }
   108	
   109	    public long Version { get; }
   110	
   111	    public IReadOnlyList<ItemReservationAllocation> InputAllocations { get; }
   112	
   113	    public string? Reason { get; }
   114	
   115	    public IReadOnlyList<ProductionMaterialStepSnapshot> MaterialSteps { get; }
   116	
   117	    public int CurrentMaterialStepIndex => MaterialSteps.Count == 0
   118	        ? -1
   119	        : MaterialSteps.Count(value => value.Consumed);
   120	
   121	    public bool IsTerminal => Status is ProductionOrderStatus.Completed
   122	        or ProductionOrderStatus.Cancelled
   123	        or ProductionOrderStatus.Failed;
   124	}
   125	
   126	public sealed class ProductionOrderStatusChanged : IDomainEvent
   127	{
   128	    public ProductionOrderStatusChanged(
   129	        long tick,
   130	        EntityId orderId,
   131	        EntityId buildingId,
   132	        ProductionOrderStatus previous,
   133	        ProductionOrderStatus current,
   134	        string? reason)
   135	    {
   136	        if (tick < 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(tick));
   137	        if (orderId.IsEmpty || buildingId.IsEmpty)
   138	            throw new ArgumentException("Order and building ids are required.");
   139	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(ProductionOrderStatus), previous)
   140	            || !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(ProductionOrderStatus), current))
   141	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(current));
   142	        Tick = tick;
   143	        OrderId = orderId;
   144	        BuildingId = buildingId;
   145	        Previous = previous;
   146	        Current = current;
   147	        Reason = reason;
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    public long Tick { get; }
   151	
   152	    public EntityId OrderId { get; }
   153	
   154	    public EntityId BuildingId { get; }
   155	
   156	    public ProductionOrderStatus Previous { get; }
   157	
   158	    public ProductionOrderStatus Current { get; }
   159	
   160	    public string? Reason { get; }
   161	}
   162	
   163	public sealed class ProductionWorkApplied : IDomainEvent
   164	{
   165	    public ProductionWorkApplied(
   166	        long tick,
   167	        EntityId orderId,
   168	        EntityId buildingId,
   169	        int effectiveWork,
   170	        int completedWork,
   171	        int requiredWork)
   172	    {
   173	        if (tick < 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(tick));
   174	        if (orderId.IsEmpty || buildingId.IsEmpty)
   175	            throw new ArgumentException("Order and building ids are required.");
   176	        if (effectiveWork <= 0 || completedWork < 0
   177	            || requiredWork <= 0 || completedWork > requiredWork)
   178	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(effectiveWork));
   179	        Tick = tick;
   180	        OrderId = orderId;
   181	        BuildingId = buildingId;
   182	        EffectiveWork = effectiveWork;
   183	        CompletedWork = completedWork;
   184	        RequiredWork = requiredWork;
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    public long Tick { get; }
   188	
   189	    public EntityId OrderId { get; }
   190	
   191	    public EntityId BuildingId { get; }
   192	
   193	    public int EffectiveWork { get; }
   194	
   195	    public int CompletedWork { get; }
   196	
   197	    public int RequiredWork { get; }
   198	}
