===== src/Dig.Headless/HeadlessBuildingScenario.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using Dig.Application.Buildings;
     3	using Dig.Application.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Application.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.World;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     9	using Dig.Domain.Core;
    10	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
    11	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
    12	using Dig.Domain.Runtime;
    13	using Dig.Domain.World;
    14	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
    15	
    16	namespace Dig.Headless
    17	{
    18	
    19	internal static class HeadlessBuildingScenario
    20	{
    21	    public static BuildingSnapshot Run(
    22	        SimulationState state,
    23	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal,
    24	        InMemoryWorldRepository worldRepository,
    25	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventoryRepository,
    26	        InMemoryJobRepository jobRepository,
    27	        InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates,
    28	        EntityId residentId,
    29	        AgentSnapshot resident,
    30	        IAgentSkillGrantService skillGrants,
    31	        EntityId sourceStackId,
    32	        ItemId materialId,
    33	        CellId origin,
    34	        long startTick)
    35	    {
    36	        EntityId buildingId = Require(state.Entities.RegisterNew());
    37	        CellId workPosition = new CellId(origin.X - 1, origin.Y, origin.Z);
    38	        Require(new ExcavateCellCommandHandler(worldRepository, journal).Handle(
    39	            new ExcavateCellCommand(
    40	                workPosition,
    41	                new MaterialId("air"),
    42	                startTick)));
    43	        BuildingDefinition definition = new BuildingDefinition(
    44	            new BuildingDefinitionId("headless.workbench"),
    45	            "Headless Workbench",
    46	            new[] { new CellOffset(0, 0) },
    47	            new[] { new CellOffset(-1, 0), new CellOffset(1, 0) },
    48	            new[] { new BuildingMaterialRequirement(materialId, 1) },
    49	            requiredWork: 5,
    50	            maximumDurability: 100);
    51	        BuildingCatalog catalog = new BuildingCatalog(new[] { definition });
    52	        InMemoryBuildingsRepository buildingsRepository =
    53	            new InMemoryBuildingsRepository();
    54	        PlaceBuildingHandler place = new PlaceBuildingHandler(
    55	            catalog,
    56	            worldRepository,
    57	            buildingsRepository,
    58	            new BuildingPlacementValidator(),
    59	            journal);
    60	        Require(place.Handle(new PlaceBuildingCommand(
    61	            buildingId,
    62	            definition.Id,
    63	            origin,
    64	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    65	            new[] { workPosition },
    66	            startTick)));
    67	
    68	        EntityId deliveryJobId = Require(state.Entities.RegisterNew());
    69	        CreateBuildingDeliveryHandler createDelivery = new CreateBuildingDeliveryHandler(
    70	            buildingsRepository,
    71	            inventoryRepository,
    72	            jobRepository,
    73	            journal);
    74	        Require(createDelivery.Handle(new CreateBuildingDeliveryCommand(
    75	            deliveryJobId,
    76	            buildingId,
    77	            sourceStackId,
    78	            quantity: 1,
    79	            priority: 650,
    80	            tick: startTick + 1)));
    81	        Assign(
    82	            jobRepository,
    83	            candidates,
    84	            journal,
    85	            deliveryJobId,
    86	            residentId,
    87	            resident,
    88	            startTick + 2);
    89	        AdvanceJobHandler advance = new AdvanceJobHandler(jobRepository, journal);
    90	        Require(advance.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(deliveryJobId, startTick + 3)));
    91	        Require(advance.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(deliveryJobId, startTick + 4)));
    92	        Require(advance.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(deliveryJobId, startTick + 5)));
    93	        CompleteBuildingDeliveryHandler completeDelivery =
    94	            new CompleteBuildingDeliveryHandler(
    95	                inventoryRepository,
    96	                jobRepository,
    97	                journal);
    98	        Require(completeDelivery.Handle(new CompleteBuildingDeliveryCommand(
    99	            deliveryJobId,
   100	            splitStackId: default,
   101	            tick: startTick + 6)));
   102	
   103	        RefreshBuildingMaterialsHandler refresh = new RefreshBuildingMaterialsHandler(
   104	            buildingsRepository,
   105	            inventoryRepository,
   106	            journal);
   107	        Require(refresh.Handle(new RefreshBuildingMaterialsCommand(buildingId)));
   108	        EntityId constructionJobId = Require(state.Entities.RegisterNew());
   109	        CreateConstructionJobHandler createConstruction = new CreateConstructionJobHandler(
   110	            buildingsRepository,
   111	            jobRepository,
   112	            journal);
   113	        Require(createConstruction.Handle(new CreateConstructionJobCommand(
   114	            constructionJobId,
   115	            buildingId,
   116	            new[] { workPosition },
   117	            priority: 700,
   118	            tick: startTick + 7)));
   119	        Assign(
   120	            jobRepository,
   121	            candidates,
   122	            journal,
   123	            constructionJobId,
   124	            residentId,
   125	            resident,
   126	            startTick + 8);
   127	        Require(advance.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(constructionJobId, startTick + 9)));
   128	        Require(advance.Handle(new AdvanceJobCommand(constructionJobId, startTick + 10)));
   129	        AddConstructionWorkHandler addWork = new AddConstructionWorkHandler(
   130	            buildingsRepository,
   131	            jobRepository,
   132	            journal);
   133	        Require(addWork.Handle(new AddConstructionWorkCommand(
   134	            constructionJobId,
   135	            buildingId,
   136	            workAmount: definition.RequiredWork,
   137	            tick: startTick + 11)));
   138	        CompleteConstructionHandler completeConstruction = new CompleteConstructionHandler(
   139	            buildingsRepository,
   140	            inventoryRepository,
   141	            jobRepository,
   142	            journal,
   143	            skillGrants);
   144	        Require(completeConstruction.Handle(new CompleteConstructionCommand(
   145	            constructionJobId,
   146	            buildingId,
   147	            tick: startTick + 12)));
   148	
   149	        BuildingSnapshot completed = buildingsRepository.Get().Get(buildingId)
   150	            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Headless building disappeared.");
   151	        if (completed.Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   152	            || inventoryRepository.Get().GetTotal(materialId) != 0
   153	            || jobRepository.Get().GetReservations().Count != 0)
   154	        {
   155	            throw new InvalidOperationException(
   156	                "Headless construction did not consume materials and complete cleanly.");
   157	        }
   158	
   159	        return completed;
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    private static void Assign(
   163	        InMemoryJobRepository jobs,
   164	        InMemoryJobCandidateProvider candidates,
   165	        InMemoryExecutionJournal journal,
   166	        EntityId jobId,
   167	        EntityId residentId,
   168	        AgentSnapshot resident,
   169	        long tick)
   170	    {
   171	        candidates.SetCandidates(jobId, new[]
   172	        {
   173	            new JobCandidate(
   174	                residentId,
   175	                resident.GetSkillLevel(new AgentSkillId("general.work")),
   176	                distanceCost: 1,
   177	                isAvailable: true),
   178	        });
   179	        JobAssignmentReport report = new AssignAvailableJobsHandler(
   180	            jobs,
   181	            candidates,
   182	            journal).Handle(new AssignAvailableJobsCommand(tick));
   183	        if (report.Assignments.Count != 1 || report.Failures.Count != 0)
   184	        {
   185	            throw new InvalidOperationException("Headless building job was not assigned.");
   186	        }
   187	    }
   188	
   189	    private static T Require<T>(Result<T> result)
   190	    {
   191	        if (result.IsFailure)
   192	        {
   193	            throw new InvalidOperationException(result.Error!.ToString());
   194	        }
   195	
   196	        return result.Value;
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    private static void Require(Result result)
   200	    {
   201	        if (result.IsFailure)
   202	        {
   203	            throw new InvalidOperationException(result.Error!.ToString());
   204	        }
   205	    }
   206	}
   207	}

     8	{
     9	    private static readonly Type[] ExpectedJobDefinitionTypes =
    10	    {
    11	        typeof(Dig.Domain.Jobs.BarrelAttackJobDefinition),
    12	        typeof(Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition),
    13	        typeof(Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition),
    14	        typeof(Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingBoxPickupJobDefinition),

    24	        typeof(Dig.Domain.Jobs.StrategicExecutionJobDefinition),
    25	        typeof(Dig.Domain.Jobs.WorldItemPickupJobDefinition),

    28	    public static SaveGameService Create(string saveDirectory)
    29	    {
    30	        JobDefinitionSaveRegistry jobs = CreateJobDefinitionRegistry();
    31	        return new SaveGameService(
    32	            new SaveGameBuilder(jobs),
    33	            new SaveGameLoader(CreateMigrationPipeline(), jobs),
    34	            new FileSaveSlotStore(
    35	                saveDirectory,
    36	                new DataContractJsonSaveCodec()));
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    public static JobDefinitionSaveRegistry CreateJobDefinitionRegistry()
    40	    {
    41	        JobDefinitionSaveRegistry registry = new JobDefinitionSaveRegistry(
    42	            new JobDefinitionSaveRegistration[]
    43	            {
    44	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.BarrelAttackJobDefinition>(
    45	                    new BarrelAttackJobSaveCodec()),
    46	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingBoxAssemblyJobDefinition>(
    47	                    new BuildingBoxAssemblyJobSaveCodec()),
    48	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingBoxPackingJobDefinition>(
    49	                    new BuildingBoxPackingJobSaveCodec()),
    50	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingBoxPickupJobDefinition>(
    51	                    new BuildingBoxPickupJobSaveCodec()),
    52	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingSupplyJobDefinition>(
    53	                    new BuildingSupplyJobSaveCodec()),
    54	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.BuildingWorkJobDefinition>(
    55	                    new BuildingWorkJobSaveCodec()),
    56	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.DigJobDefinition>(
    57	                    new DigJobDefinitionSaveCodec()),
    58	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.HaulJobDefinition>(
    59	                    new HaulJobDefinitionSaveCodec()),
    60	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.HealingJobDefinition>(
    61	                    new HealingJobSaveCodec()),
    62	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.MushroomChopJobDefinition>(
    63	                    new MushroomChopJobSaveCodec()),
    64	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.ProductionWorkJobDefinition>(
    65	                    new ProductionWorkJobSaveCodec()),
    66	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.ResidentInventoryPlacementJobDefinition>(
    67	                    new ResidentInventoryPlacementJobSaveCodec()),
    68	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.SpatialDigJobDefinition>(
    69	                    new SpatialDigJobSaveCodec()),
    70	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.StrategicExecutionJobDefinition>(
    71	                    new StrategicExecutionJobSaveCodec()),
    72	                Registration<Dig.Domain.Jobs.WorldItemPickupJobDefinition>(
    73	                    new WorldItemPickupJobSaveCodec()),
    74	            });
    75	        registry.ValidateCoverage(ExpectedJobDefinitionTypes);
    76	        return registry;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    public static SaveMigrationPipeline CreateMigrationPipeline()
    80	    {
    81	        return new SaveMigrationPipeline(new ISaveMigration[]
    82	        {
    83	            new LegacySaveVersionZeroMigration(),
    84	            new SaveVersionOneBuildingsMigration(),
    85	            new SaveVersionTwoPackingMigration(),
    86	            new SaveVersionThreeAgentSkillsMigration(),
    87	            new SaveVersionFourAuthoritativeCoordinatesMigration(),
    88	            new SaveVersionFiveMushroomsMigration(),
    89	            new SaveVersionSixBuildingProductionMigration(),
    90	            new SaveVersionSevenWorldExcavationProgressMigration(),
    91	            new SaveVersionEightAgentRuntimeMigration(),
    92	            new SaveVersionNineCombatSpatialMigration(),
    93	        });
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    private static JobDefinitionSaveRegistration Registration<TDefinition>(
    97	        IJobDefinitionSaveCodec codec)
    98	        where TDefinition : Dig.Domain.Jobs.JobDefinition
    99	    {
   100	        return new JobDefinitionSaveRegistration(typeof(TDefinition), codec);
   101	    }

===== src/Dig.Presentation.Abstractions/Agents/ResidentActivityPresenter.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Strategy;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Presentation.Agents
    10	{
    11	
    12	internal static class ResidentActivityPresenter
    13	{
    14	    public static ResidentActivityDescriptor Present(
    15	        AgentSnapshot agent,
    16	        JobSnapshot? job)
    17	    {
    18	        if (agent is null)
    19	        {
    20	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(agent));
    21	        }
    22	
    23	        if (job is not null)
    24	        {
    25	            return PresentJob(agent.Id, job);
    26	        }
    27	
    28	        if (agent.ActiveAction.HasValue)
    29	        {
    30	            return PresentAction(agent.Id, agent.ActiveAction.Value);
    31	        }
    32	
    33	        ResidentActivityKind kind = agent.ScheduledActivity switch
    34	        {
    35	            ScheduleActivity.Work => ResidentActivityKind.Idle,
    36	            ScheduleActivity.Rest => ResidentActivityKind.Rest,
    37	            ScheduleActivity.Sleep => ResidentActivityKind.Sleep,
    38	            ScheduleActivity.Free => ResidentActivityKind.FreeTime,
    39	            _ => ResidentActivityKind.Idle,
    40	        };
    41	        return Descriptor(kind, agent.Id);
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    private static ResidentActivityDescriptor PresentAction(
    45	        EntityId actorId,
    46	        AgentActionSnapshot action)
    47	    {
    48	        ResidentActivityKind kind = action.IntentKind switch
    49	        {
    50	            AgentIntentKind.Flee => ResidentActivityKind.Flee,
    51	            AgentIntentKind.Eat => ResidentActivityKind.Eat,
    52	            AgentIntentKind.Sleep => ResidentActivityKind.Sleep,
    53	            AgentIntentKind.PlayerOrder => ResidentActivityKind.Move,
    54	            AgentIntentKind.Work => ResidentActivityKind.Work,
    55	            AgentIntentKind.Rest => ResidentActivityKind.Rest,
    56	            AgentIntentKind.Idle => ResidentActivityKind.Idle,
    57	            _ => ResidentActivityKind.Idle,
    58	        };
    59	        string? subjectId = action.Target?.EntityId.ToString();
    60	        return new ResidentActivityDescriptor(
    61	            kind,
    62	            actorId.ToString(),
    63	            LocalizationKey(kind),
    64	            subjectId,
    65	            sourceIntent: action.IntentKind,
    66	            sourceOrderId: action.PlayerOrderId,
    67	            progressCurrent: action.ElapsedTicks,
    68	            progressMaximum: action.RequiredTicks,
    69	            localizationArguments: Arguments(actorId, subjectId, destination: null));
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    private static ResidentActivityDescriptor PresentJob(
    73	        EntityId actorId,
    74	        JobSnapshot job)
    75	    {
    76	        ResidentActivityKind kind = ClassifyJob(job.Definition, job.Stage);
    77	        string? subjectId = SubjectId(job.Definition);
    78	        CellId? destination = Destination(job.Definition);
    79	        int progressMaximum = job.Definition.Stages.Count;
    80	        int progressCurrent = ResolveStageIndex(job);
    81	        if (job.Status == JobStatus.Blocked)
    82	        {
    83	            kind = ResidentActivityKind.Blocked;
    84	        }
    85	
    86	        string localizationKey = job.Definition is BarrelAttackJobDefinition
    87	            && kind != ResidentActivityKind.Blocked
    88	                ? "Атакует бочку"
    89	                : LocalizationKey(kind);
    90	        return new ResidentActivityDescriptor(
    91	            kind,
    92	            actorId.ToString(),
    93	            localizationKey,
    94	            subjectId,
    95	            destination,
    96	            job.Id.ToString(),
    97	            sourceJobStage: job.Stage,
    98	            progressCurrent: progressCurrent,
    99	            progressMaximum: progressMaximum,
   100	            blockReasonCode: job.Reason?.Code,
   101	            localizationArguments: Arguments(actorId, subjectId, destination));
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    private static ResidentActivityKind ClassifyJob(
   105	        JobDefinition definition,
   106	        JobStageKind stage)
   107	    {
   108	        if (definition is BarrelAttackJobDefinition)
   109	        {
   110	            return ResidentActivityKind.Attack;
   111	        }
   112	
   113	        if (definition is MushroomChopJobDefinition)
   114	        {
   115	            return ResidentActivityKind.GatherMushroom;
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        if (definition is DigJobDefinition
   119	            || definition is SpatialDigJobDefinition
   120	            || definition.PreferredToolKind == JobToolKind.Mining)
   121	        {
   122	            return ResidentActivityKind.Dig;
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        if (definition is HaulJobDefinition)
   126	        {
   127	            return stage == JobStageKind.AcquireItem
   128	                ? ResidentActivityKind.Pickup
   129	                : ResidentActivityKind.Logistics;
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        if (definition is ProductionWorkJobDefinition)
   133	        {
   134	            return ResidentActivityKind.Craft;
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        if (definition is HealingJobDefinition)
   138	        {
   139	            return ResidentActivityKind.Service;
   140	        }
   141	
   142	        if (definition is StrategicExecutionJobDefinition strategic)
   143	        {
   144	            return strategic.Goal switch
   145	            {
   146	                StrategicGoalKind.Attack => ResidentActivityKind.Attack,
   147	                StrategicGoalKind.Retreat => ResidentActivityKind.Flee,
   148	                _ => ResidentActivityKind.Work,
   149	            };
   150	        }
   151	
   152	        return ResidentActivityKind.Work;
   153	    }
   154	
   155	    private static string? SubjectId(JobDefinition definition)
   156	    {
   157	        return definition switch
   158	        {
   159	            HaulJobDefinition haul => haul.SourceStackId.ToString(),
   160	            ProductionWorkJobDefinition production => production.BuildingId.ToString(),
   161	            HealingJobDefinition healing => healing.PatientId.ToString(),
   162	            StrategicExecutionJobDefinition strategic when strategic.TargetFactionId.HasValue =>
   163	                strategic.TargetFactionId.Value.ToString(),
   164	            MushroomChopJobDefinition mushroom => mushroom.SiteId.ToString(),
   165	            BarrelAttackJobDefinition barrel => barrel.BarrelId.ToString(),
   166	            _ => null,
   167	        };
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    private static CellId? Destination(JobDefinition definition)
   171	    {
   172	        return definition switch
   173	        {
   174	            DigJobDefinition dig => dig.Target.CellId,
   175	            SpatialDigJobDefinition spatial => spatial.Target.TargetCell,
   176	            HaulJobDefinition haul when haul.Destination.HasCell => haul.Destination.CellId,
   177	            ProductionWorkJobDefinition production => production.WorkPosition,
   178	            HealingJobDefinition healing => healing.WorkPosition,
   179	            StrategicExecutionJobDefinition strategic => strategic.TargetCell,
   180	            MushroomChopJobDefinition mushroom => mushroom.TargetCell,
   181	            BarrelAttackJobDefinition barrel => barrel.TargetCell,
   182	            _ => null,
   183	        };
   184	    }
   185	
   186	    private static int ResolveStageIndex(JobSnapshot job)
   187	    {
   188	        if (job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress || job.Stage == JobStageKind.None)
   189	        {
   190	            return 0;
   191	        }
   192	
   193	        for (int index = 0; index < job.Definition.Stages.Count; index++)
   194	        {
   195	            if (job.Definition.Stages[index] == job.Stage)
   196	            {
   197	                return index;
   198	            }
   199	        }
   200	
   201	        return 0;
   202	    }
   203	
   204	    private static ResidentActivityDescriptor Descriptor(
   205	        ResidentActivityKind kind,
   206	        EntityId actorId)
   207	    {
   208	        return new ResidentActivityDescriptor(
   209	            kind,
   210	            actorId.ToString(),
   211	            LocalizationKey(kind),
   212	            localizationArguments: Arguments(actorId, subjectId: null, destination: null));
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    private static string LocalizationKey(ResidentActivityKind kind)
   216	    {
   217	        return kind == ResidentActivityKind.GatherMushroom
   218	            ? "Добывает гриб"
   219	            : "resident.activity." + kind.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
   220	    }
   221	
   222	    private static IReadOnlyList<ResidentLocalizationArgument> Arguments(
   223	        EntityId actorId,
   224	        string? subjectId,
   225	        CellId? destination)
   226	    {
   227	        List<ResidentLocalizationArgument> arguments = new List<ResidentLocalizationArgument>
   228	        {
   229	            new ResidentLocalizationArgument("actor", actorId.ToString()),
   230	        };
   231	        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(subjectId))
   232	        {
   233	            arguments.Add(new ResidentLocalizationArgument("subject", subjectId));
   234	        }
   235	
   236	        if (destination.HasValue)
   237	        {
   238	            arguments.Add(new ResidentLocalizationArgument(
   239	                "destination",
   240	                destination.Value.ToString()));
   241	        }
   242	
   243	        return arguments;
   244	    }
   245	}
   246	}
===== src/Dig.Presentation.Abstractions/Buildings/BuildingBoxPlacementModels.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     8	using Dig.Domain.World;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Presentation.Buildings
    11	{
    12	
    13	public enum BuildingBoxGhostStyle
    14	{
    15	    Valid = 0,
    16	    Invalid = 1,
    17	}
    18	
    19	public enum BuildingBoxPlacementKind
    20	{
    21	    RelocateBox = 0,
    22	    AssembleBuilding = 1,
    23	}
    24	
    25	public sealed class BuildingBoxGhostViewModel
    26	{
    27	    public BuildingBoxGhostViewModel(
    28	        EntityId? sourceStackId,
    29	        BuildingDefinitionId definitionId,
    30	        CellId origin,
    31	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    32	        IEnumerable<CellId> footprint,
    33	        CellId? workPosition,
    34	        bool isValid,
    35	        string? reasonCode,
    36	        BuildingBoxPlacementKind placementKind = BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding,
    37	        bool isVisible = true,
    38	        ItemId? sourceItemId = null)
    39	    {
    40	        if (sourceStackId.HasValue && sourceStackId.Value.IsEmpty)
    41	        {
    42	            throw new ArgumentException("Source stack id cannot be empty.", nameof(sourceStackId));
    43	        }
    44	
    45	        if (definitionId.IsEmpty)
    46	        {
    47	            throw new ArgumentException("Building definition id cannot be empty.", nameof(definitionId));
    48	        }
    49	
    50	        if (footprint is null)
    51	        {
    52	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(footprint));
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingBoxPlacementKind), placementKind))
    56	        {
    57	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(placementKind));
    58	        }
    59	
    60	        CellId[] ordered = footprint.Distinct().OrderBy(cell => cell).ToArray();
    61	        if (ordered.Length == 0)
    62	        {
    63	            throw new ArgumentException("Ghost footprint cannot be empty.", nameof(footprint));
    64	        }
    65	
    66	        if (!isValid && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reasonCode))
    67	        {
    68	            throw new ArgumentException(
    69	                "Invalid ghost preview requires a reason code.",
    70	                nameof(reasonCode));
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        SourceStackId = sourceStackId;
    74	        SourceItemId = sourceItemId;
    75	        DefinitionId = definitionId;
    76	        Origin = origin;
    77	        Orientation = orientation;
    78	        Footprint = new ReadOnlyCollection<CellId>(ordered);
    79	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
    80	        IsValid = isValid;
    81	        ReasonCode = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reasonCode) ? null : reasonCode.Trim();
    82	        PlacementKind = placementKind;
    83	        IsVisible = isVisible
    84	            && !string.Equals(
    85	                ReasonCode,
    86	                PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing.Code,
    87	                StringComparison.Ordinal);
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    public EntityId? SourceStackId { get; }
    91	
    92	    public ItemId? SourceItemId { get; }
    93	
    94	    public BuildingDefinitionId DefinitionId { get; }
    95	
    96	    public CellId Origin { get; }
    97	
    98	    public BuildingOrientation Orientation { get; }
    99	
   100	    public IReadOnlyList<CellId> Footprint { get; }
   101	
   102	    public CellId? WorkPosition { get; }
   103	
   104	    public bool IsValid { get; }
   105	
   106	    public string? ReasonCode { get; }
   107	
   108	    public BuildingBoxPlacementKind PlacementKind { get; }
   109	
   110	    public bool IsVisible { get; }
   111	
   112	    public BuildingBoxGhostStyle Style => IsValid
   113	        ? BuildingBoxGhostStyle.Valid
   114	        : BuildingBoxGhostStyle.Invalid;
   115	}
   116	
   117	public readonly struct BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft
   118	{
   119	    public BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft(
   120	        EntityId sourceStackId,
   121	        BuildingDefinitionId definitionId,
   122	        CellId origin,
   123	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   124	        CellId workPosition,
   125	        BuildingBoxPlacementKind placementKind = BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding)
   126	    {
   127	        if (sourceStackId.IsEmpty)
   128	        {
   129	            throw new ArgumentException("Source stack id cannot be empty.", nameof(sourceStackId));
   130	        }
   131	
   132	        if (definitionId.IsEmpty)
   133	        {
   134	            throw new ArgumentException("Building definition id cannot be empty.", nameof(definitionId));
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingBoxPlacementKind), placementKind))
   138	        {
   139	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(placementKind));
   140	        }
   141	
   142	        SourceStackId = sourceStackId;
   143	        DefinitionId = definitionId;
   144	        Origin = origin;
   145	        Orientation = orientation;
   146	        WorkPosition = workPosition;
   147	        PlacementKind = placementKind;
   148	    }
   149	
   150	    public EntityId SourceStackId { get; }
   151	
   152	    public BuildingDefinitionId DefinitionId { get; }
   153	
   154	    public CellId Origin { get; }
   155	
   156	    public BuildingOrientation Orientation { get; }
   157	
   158	    public CellId WorkPosition { get; }
   159	
   160	    public BuildingBoxPlacementKind PlacementKind { get; }
   161	}
   162	
   163	public readonly struct BuildingBoxPlacementModeState
   164	{
   165	    public BuildingBoxPlacementModeState(
   166	        EntityId sourceStackId,
   167	        BuildingDefinitionId definitionId,
   168	        BuildingOrientation orientation = BuildingOrientation.North)
   169	    {
   170	        if (sourceStackId.IsEmpty)
   171	        {
   172	            throw new ArgumentException("Source stack id cannot be empty.", nameof(sourceStackId));
   173	        }
   174	
   175	        if (definitionId.IsEmpty)
   176	        {
   177	            throw new ArgumentException("Building definition id cannot be empty.", nameof(definitionId));
   178	        }
   179	
   180	        SourceStackId = sourceStackId;
   181	        DefinitionId = definitionId;
   182	        Orientation = orientation;
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    public EntityId SourceStackId { get; }
   186	
   187	    public BuildingDefinitionId DefinitionId { get; }
   188	
   189	    public BuildingOrientation Orientation { get; }
   190	
   191	    public BuildingBoxPlacementModeState RotateClockwise()
   192	    {
   193	        BuildingOrientation next = (BuildingOrientation)(((int)Orientation + 1) % 4);
   194	        return new BuildingBoxPlacementModeState(SourceStackId, DefinitionId, next);
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    public BuildingBoxPlacementModeState RotateCounterClockwise()
   198	    {
   199	        BuildingOrientation next = (BuildingOrientation)(((int)Orientation + 3) % 4);
   200	        return new BuildingBoxPlacementModeState(SourceStackId, DefinitionId, next);
   201	    }
   202	}
   203

===== src/Dig.Presentation.Abstractions/Buildings/BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter.Relocation.cs =====
     1	using System.Collections.Generic;
     2	using System.Linq;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	
     7	namespace Dig.Presentation.Buildings
     8	{
     9	public sealed partial class BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter
    10	{
    11	    private static BuildingBoxGhostViewModel PreviewRelocation(
    12	        ItemStackSnapshot sourceStack,
    13	        BuildingDefinition definition,
    14	        CellId origin,
    15	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    16	        WorldSnapshot world,
    17	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> occupiedCells,
    18	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> reachableCells)
    19	    {
    20	        CellId[] footprint = { origin };
    21	        if (!world.Size.Contains(origin))
    22	        {
    23	            return Invalid(
    24	                sourceStack.StackId,
    25	                definition,
    26	                origin,
    27	                orientation,
    28	                footprint,
    29	                BuildingErrors.PlacementOutOfBounds.Code,
    30	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox,
    31	                isVisible: false);
    32	        }
    33	
    34	        CellSnapshot cell = world.Chunks
    35	            .SelectMany(chunk => chunk.Cells)
    36	            .First(value => value.Id == origin);
    37	        if (cell.IsSolid)
    38	        {
    39	            return Invalid(
    40	                sourceStack.StackId,
    41	                definition,
    42	                origin,
    43	                orientation,
    44	                footprint,
    45	                BuildingErrors.PlacementSolid.Code,
    46	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox);
    47	        }
    48	
    49	        if (!cell.State.IsExplored)
    50	        {
    51	            return Invalid(
    52	                sourceStack.StackId,
    53	                definition,
    54	                origin,
    55	                orientation,
    56	                footprint,
    57	                BuildingErrors.PlacementUnexplored.Code,
    58	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox,
    59	                isVisible: false);
    60	        }
    61	
    62	        if (!BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector.HasSupportingPlane(origin, world))
    63	        {
    64	            return Invalid(
    65	                sourceStack.StackId,
    66	                definition,
    67	                origin,
    68	                orientation,
    69	                footprint,
    70	                PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing.Code,
    71	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox,
    72	                isVisible: false);
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        if (occupiedCells.Contains(origin))
    76	        {
    77	            return Invalid(
    78	                sourceStack.StackId,
    79	                definition,
    80	                origin,
    81	                orientation,
    82	                footprint,
    83	                BuildingErrors.PlacementOccupied.Code,
    84	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox);
    85	        }
    86	
    87	        if (!reachableCells.Contains(origin))
    88	        {
    89	            return Invalid(
    90	                sourceStack.StackId,
    91	                definition,
    92	                origin,
    93	                orientation,
    94	                footprint,
    95	                BuildingErrors.NoReachableWorkPosition.Code,
    96	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox);
    97	        }
    98	
    99	        return new BuildingBoxGhostViewModel(
   100	            sourceStack.StackId,
   101	            definition.Id,
   102	            origin,
   103	            orientation,
   104	            footprint,
   105	            origin,
   106	            isValid: true,
   107	            reasonCode: null,
   108	            BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox,
   109	            sourceItemId: sourceStack.ItemId);
   110	    }
   111	}
   112	}
===== src/Dig.Presentation.Abstractions/Buildings/BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Content;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     7	using Dig.Domain.World;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Presentation.Buildings
    10	{
    11	public static class BuildingBoxPreviewReasons
    12	{
    13	    public const string MissingSource = "building_box.preview.source_missing";
    14	    public const string DefinitionNotEnabled = "building_box.preview.definition_not_enabled";
    15	    public const string ItemMismatch = "building_box.preview.item_mismatch";
    16	    public const string BoxNotSingle = "building_box.preview.box_not_single";
    17	    public const string BoxUnavailable = "building_box.preview.box_unavailable";
    18	}
    19	
    20	public sealed partial class BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter
    21	{
    22	    private static readonly DomainError InvalidPreview = new DomainError(
    23	        "building_box.preview.invalid",
    24	        "The BuildingBox placement preview is not valid for confirmation.");
    25	
    26	    private readonly BuildingPlacementValidator _validator;
    27	    private readonly PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyValidator _physicalValidator;
    28	    private readonly BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector _surfaceFacts;
    29	    private readonly PackableBuildingContentCatalog _packableCatalog;
    30	
    31	    public BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter(BuildingPlacementValidator validator)
    32	        : this(
    33	            validator,
    34	            new PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyValidator(),
    35	            CampfireBuildingBoxContent.Catalog)
    36	    {
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    public BuildingBoxPlacementPresenter(
    40	        BuildingPlacementValidator validator,
    41	        PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyValidator physicalValidator,
    42	        PackableBuildingContentCatalog packableCatalog)
    43	    {
    44	        _validator = validator ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(validator));
    45	        _physicalValidator = physicalValidator
    46	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(physicalValidator));
    47	        _surfaceFacts = new BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector(_physicalValidator);
    48	        _packableCatalog = packableCatalog
    49	            ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(packableCatalog));
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    public BuildingBoxGhostViewModel Preview(
    53	        ItemStackSnapshot? sourceStack,
    54	        ItemDefinition? sourceItem,
    55	        BuildingDefinition definition,
    56	        CellId origin,
    57	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    58	        WorldSnapshot world,
    59	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> occupiedCells,
    60	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> reachableCells,
    61	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId>? ecologyBlockedCells = null)
    62	    {
    63	        if (definition is null)
    64	        {
    65	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definition));
    66	        }
    67	
    68	        if (world is null)
    69	        {
    70	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(world));
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        if (occupiedCells is null || reachableCells is null)
    74	        {
    75	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(occupiedCells));
    76	        }
    77	
    78	        BuildingBoxPlacementKind kind = origin.Z == 0
    79	            ? BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox
    80	            : BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding;
    81	        IReadOnlyList<CellId> initialFootprint = kind == BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox
    82	            ? new[] { origin }
    83	            : definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, orientation);
    84	        string? sourceReason = ValidateSource(sourceStack, sourceItem, definition);
    85	        if (sourceReason is not null)
    86	        {
    87	            return Invalid(
    88	                sourceStack?.StackId,
    89	                definition,
    90	                origin,
    91	                orientation,
    92	                initialFootprint,
    93	                sourceReason,
    94	                kind);
    95	        }
    96	
    97	        return kind == BuildingBoxPlacementKind.RelocateBox
    98	            ? PreviewRelocation(
    99	                sourceStack!,
   100	                definition,
   101	                origin,
   102	                orientation,
   103	                world,
   104	                occupiedCells,
   105	                reachableCells)
   106	            : PreviewAssembly(
   107	                sourceStack!,
   108	                definition,
   109	                origin,
   110	                orientation,
   111	                world,
   112	                occupiedCells,
   113	                reachableCells,
   114	                ecologyBlockedCells);
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    public Result<BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft> CreateConfirmationDraft(
   118	        BuildingBoxGhostViewModel preview)
   119	    {
   120	        if (preview is null)
   121	        {
   122	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(preview));
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        if (!preview.IsVisible
   126	            || !preview.IsValid
   127	            || !preview.SourceStackId.HasValue
   128	            || !preview.WorkPosition.HasValue)
   129	        {
   130	            return Result<BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft>.Failure(InvalidPreview);
   131	        }
   132	
   133	        return Result<BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft>.Success(
   134	            new BuildingBoxPlacementConfirmationDraft(
   135	                preview.SourceStackId.Value,
   136	                preview.DefinitionId,
   137	                preview.Origin,
   138	                preview.Orientation,
   139	                preview.WorkPosition.Value,
   140	                preview.PlacementKind));
   141	    }
   142	
   143	    private BuildingBoxGhostViewModel PreviewAssembly(
   144	        ItemStackSnapshot sourceStack,
   145	        BuildingDefinition definition,
   146	        CellId origin,
   147	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   148	        WorldSnapshot world,
   149	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> occupiedCells,
   150	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId> reachableCells,
   151	        IReadOnlyCollection<CellId>? ecologyBlockedCells)
   152	    {
   153	        BuildingPlacementResult placement = _validator.Validate(
   154	            definition,
   155	            origin,
   156	            orientation,
   157	            world,
   158	            occupiedCells,
   159	            reachableCells,
   160	            ecologyBlockedCells);
   161	        if (!placement.Succeeded)
   162	        {
   163	            return Invalid(
   164	                sourceStack.StackId,
   165	                definition,
   166	                origin,
   167	                orientation,
   168	                placement.Footprint,
   169	                placement.Error!.Code,
   170	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding);
   171	        }
   172	
   173	        if (!BuildingPlacementSurfaceFactProjector.HasSupportingPlane(
   174	            placement.Footprint,
   175	            world))
   176	        {
   177	            return Invalid(
   178	                sourceStack.StackId,
   179	                definition,
   180	                origin,
   181	                orientation,
   182	                placement.Footprint,
   183	                PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing.Code,
   184	                BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding,
   185	                isVisible: false);
   186	        }
   187	
   188	        IReadOnlyList<CellId> previewFootprint = placement.Footprint;
   189	        if (_packableCatalog.TryGet(
   190	            definition.Id,
   191	            out PackableBuildingContentDefinition? content))
   192	        {
   193	            PackableBuildingSurfacePolicy policy = content!.Placement.ToSurfacePolicy();
   194	            IReadOnlyList<BuildingPlacementSurfaceCell> surfaceCells =
   195	                _surfaceFacts.Project(policy, origin, world);
   196	            PackableBuildingPlacementPolicyResult physical = _physicalValidator.Validate(
   197	                policy,
   198	                origin,
   199	                surfaceCells,
   200	                occupiedCells);
   201	            if (!physical.Succeeded)
   202	            {
   203	                return Invalid(
   204	                    sourceStack.StackId,
   205	                    definition,
   206	                    origin,
   207	                    orientation,
   208	                    physical.Footprint.CoveredCells,
   209	                    physical.Error!.Code,
   210	                    BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding,
   211	                    isVisible: physical.Error != PackableBuildingPlacementErrors.SurfaceMissing);
   212	            }
   213	
   214	            previewFootprint = physical.Footprint.CoveredCells;
   215	        }
   216	
   217	        return new BuildingBoxGhostViewModel(
   218	            sourceStack.StackId,
   219	            definition.Id,
   220	            origin,
   221	            orientation,
   222	            previewFootprint,
   223	            placement.WorkPosition,
   224	            isValid: true,
   225	            reasonCode: null,
   226	            BuildingBoxPlacementKind.AssembleBuilding,
   227	            sourceItemId: sourceStack.ItemId);
   228	    }
   229	
   230	    private static string? ValidateSource(
   231	        ItemStackSnapshot? sourceStack,
   232	        ItemDefinition? sourceItem,
   233	        BuildingDefinition definition)
   234	    {
   235	        if (definition.BoxPolicy is null)
   236	        {
   237	            return BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.DefinitionNotEnabled;
   238	        }
   239	
   240	        if (sourceStack is null || sourceItem is null)
   241	        {
   242	            return BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.MissingSource;
   243	        }
   244	
   245	        if (sourceStack.ItemId != definition.BoxPolicy.BoxItemId
   246	            || sourceItem.Id != sourceStack.ItemId)
   247	        {
   248	            return BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.ItemMismatch;
   249	        }
   250	
   251	        if (sourceItem.MaximumStackSize != 1 || sourceStack.Quantity != 1)
   252	        {
   253	            return BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.BoxNotSingle;
   254	        }
   255	
   256	        return sourceStack.AvailableQuantity == 1
   257	            ? null
   258	            : BuildingBoxPreviewReasons.BoxUnavailable;
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    private static BuildingBoxGhostViewModel Invalid(
   262	        EntityId? sourceStackId,
   263	        BuildingDefinition definition,
   264	        CellId origin,
   265	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   266	        IEnumerable<CellId> footprint,
   267	        string reasonCode,
   268	        BuildingBoxPlacementKind kind,
   269	        bool isVisible = true)
   270	    {
   271	        return new BuildingBoxGhostViewModel(
   272	            sourceStackId,
   273	            definition.Id,
   274	            origin,
   275	            orientation,
   276	            footprint,
   277	            workPosition: null,
   278	            isValid: false,
   279	            reasonCode,
   280	            kind,
   281	            isVisible,
   282	            definition.BoxPolicy?.BoxItemId);
   283	    }
   284	}
   285	
   286	}

===== src/Dig.Presentation.Abstractions/Buildings/BuildingWorldModels.cs =====
     1	using System;

    12	{

    30	public sealed class BuildingWorldViewModel
    31	{
    32	    public BuildingWorldViewModel(
    33	        string id,
    34	        string definitionId,
    35	        string name,
    36	        int originX,
    37	        int originY,
    38	        BuildingStatus status,
    39	        long version,
    40	        IReadOnlyCollection<BuildingFootprintCellViewModel> footprint,
    41	        BuildingFunctionsViewModel functions)
    42	        : this(
    43	            id,
    44	            definitionId,
    45	            name,
    46	            originX,
    47	            originY,
    48	            CellId.MinimumDepth,
    49	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    50	            originX,
    51	            originY,
    52	            CellId.MinimumDepth,
    53	            status,
    54	            status == BuildingStatus.Completed || status == BuildingStatus.Damaged ? 1 : 0,
    55	            1,
    56	            version,
    57	            footprint,
    58	            functions)
    59	    {
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    public BuildingWorldViewModel(
    63	        string id,
    64	        string definitionId,
    65	        string name,
    66	        int originX,
    67	        int originY,
    68	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
    69	        int workPositionX,
    70	        int workPositionY,
    71	        BuildingStatus status,
    72	        int completedWork,
    73	        int requiredWork,
    74	        long version,
    75	        IReadOnlyCollection<BuildingFootprintCellViewModel> footprint,
    76	        BuildingFunctionsViewModel functions)
    77	        : this(
    78	            id,
    79	            definitionId,
    80	            name,
    81	            originX,
    82	            originY,
    83	            CellId.MinimumDepth,
    84	            orientation,
    85	            workPositionX,
    86	            workPositionY,
    87	            CellId.MinimumDepth,
    88	            status,
    89	            completedWork,
    90	            requiredWork,
    91	            version,
    92	            footprint,
    93	            functions)
    94	    {
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    public BuildingWorldViewModel(
    98	        string id,
    99	        string definitionId,
   100	        string name,
   101	        int originX,
   102	        int originY,
   103	        int originZ,
   104	        BuildingOrientation orientation,
   105	        int workPositionX,
   106	        int workPositionY,
   107	        int workPositionZ,
   108	        BuildingStatus status,
   109	        int completedWork,
   110	        int requiredWork,
   111	        long version,
   112	        IReadOnlyCollection<BuildingFootprintCellViewModel> footprint,
   113	        BuildingFunctionsViewModel functions,
   114	        string? sourceBuildingBoxStackId = null,
   115	        string? buildingBoxJobId = null,
   116	        BuildingBoxCommitState? buildingBoxCommitState = null)
   117	    {
   118	        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(id)
   119	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(definitionId)
   120	            || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
   121	        {
   122	            throw new ArgumentException("Building view identifiers and name are required.");
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(BuildingOrientation), orientation))
   126	        {
   127	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(orientation));
   128	        }
   129	
   130	        if (originZ < CellId.MinimumDepth || originZ > CellId.MaximumDepth)
   131	        {
   132	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(originZ));
   133	        }
   134	
   135	        if (workPositionZ < CellId.MinimumDepth || workPositionZ > CellId.MaximumDepth)
   136	        {
   137	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(workPositionZ));
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        if (completedWork < 0
   141	            || requiredWork <= 0
   142	            || completedWork > requiredWork)
   143	        {
   144	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(completedWork));
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        if (version < 0)
   148	        {
   149	            throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(version));
   150	        }
   151	
   152	        if (footprint is null || footprint.Count == 0)
   153	        {
   154	            throw new ArgumentException("Building footprint is required.", nameof(footprint));
   155	        }
   156	
   157	        bool hasBoxIdentity = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceBuildingBoxStackId)
   158	            && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(buildingBoxJobId)
   159	            && buildingBoxCommitState.HasValue;
   160	        bool hasPartialBoxIdentity = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceBuildingBoxStackId)
   161	            || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(buildingBoxJobId)
   162	            || buildingBoxCommitState.HasValue;
   163	        if (hasPartialBoxIdentity && !hasBoxIdentity)
   164	        {
   165	            throw new ArgumentException(
   166	                "BuildingBox transformation identity must be complete.");
   167	        }
   168	
   169	        Id = id.Trim();
   170	        DefinitionId = definitionId.Trim();
   171	        Name = name.Trim();
   172	        OriginX = originX;
   173	        OriginY = originY;
   174	        OriginZ = originZ;
   175	        Orientation = orientation;
   176	        WorkPositionX = workPositionX;
   177	        WorkPositionY = workPositionY;
   178	        WorkPositionZ = workPositionZ;
   179	        Status = status;
   180	        CompletedWork = completedWork;
   181	        RequiredWork = requiredWork;
   182	        Version = version;
   183	        Footprint = new ReadOnlyCollection<BuildingFootprintCellViewModel>(
   184	            footprint.OrderBy(cell => cell.Z)
   185	                .ThenBy(cell => cell.Y)
   186	                .ThenBy(cell => cell.X)
   187	                .ToArray());
   188	        Functions = functions ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(functions));
   189	        SourceBuildingBoxStackId = hasBoxIdentity
   190	            ? sourceBuildingBoxStackId!.Trim()
   191	            : null;
   192	        BuildingBoxJobId = hasBoxIdentity
   193	            ? buildingBoxJobId!.Trim()
   194	            : null;
   195	        BuildingBoxCommitState = buildingBoxCommitState;
   196	    }
   197	
   198	    public string Id { get; }
   199	    public string DefinitionId { get; }
   200	    public string Name { get; }
   201	    public int OriginX { get; }
   202	    public int OriginY { get; }
   203	    public int OriginZ { get; }
   204	    public BuildingOrientation Orientation { get; }
   205	    public int WorkPositionX { get; }
   206	    public int WorkPositionY { get; }
   207	    public int WorkPositionZ { get; }
   208	    public BuildingStatus Status { get; }
   209	    public int CompletedWork { get; }
   210	    public int RequiredWork { get; }
   211	    public long Version { get; }
   212	    public IReadOnlyList<BuildingFootprintCellViewModel> Footprint { get; }
   213	    public BuildingFunctionsViewModel Functions { get; }
   214	    public string? SourceBuildingBoxStackId { get; }
   215	    public string? BuildingBoxJobId { get; }
   216	    public BuildingBoxCommitState? BuildingBoxCommitState { get; }
   217	    public bool HasBuildingBoxLifecycle =>
   218	        SourceBuildingBoxStackId != null
   219	        && BuildingBoxJobId != null
   220	        && BuildingBoxCommitState.HasValue;
   221	    public bool IsPendingBuildingBoxLifecycle =>
   222	        HasBuildingBoxLifecycle
   223	        && BuildingBoxCommitState != Dig.Domain.Buildings.BuildingBoxCommitState.Consumed
   224	        && Status != BuildingStatus.Completed
   225	        && Status != BuildingStatus.Damaged;
   226	    public bool IsSelectable => Status == BuildingStatus.Completed;
   227	    public double AssemblyProgress => Math.Min(1d, (double)CompletedWork / RequiredWork);
   228	    public BuildingVisualState VisualState => BuildingVisualStateResolver.Resolve(
   229	        Status,
   230	        Functions.IsPacking);
   231	}
   232	
   233	public sealed class BuildingWorldPresenter
   234	{
   235	    private readonly BuildingFunctionsPresenter _functions;
   236	
   237	    public BuildingWorldPresenter(BuildingFunctionsPresenter functions)
   238	    {
   239	        _functions = functions ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(functions));
   240	    }
   241	
   242	    public IReadOnlyList<BuildingWorldViewModel> Load(
   243	        IReadOnlyCollection<BuildingSnapshot> snapshots)
   244	    {
   245	        if (snapshots is null)
   246	        {
   247	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(snapshots));
   248	        }
   249	
   250	        BuildingWorldViewModel[] models = snapshots
   251	            .Where(snapshot => snapshot.IsActive)
   252	            .OrderBy(snapshot => snapshot.Id.ToString(), StringComparer.Ordinal)
   253	            .Select(Present)
   254	            .ToArray();
   255	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<BuildingWorldViewModel>(models);
   256	    }
   257	
   258	    private BuildingWorldViewModel Present(BuildingSnapshot snapshot)
   259	    {
   260	        BuildingFootprintCellViewModel[] footprint = snapshot.Footprint
   261	            .Select(cell => new BuildingFootprintCellViewModel(cell.X, cell.Y, cell.Z))
   262	            .ToArray();
   263	        return new BuildingWorldViewModel(
   264	            snapshot.Id.ToString(),
   265	            snapshot.Definition.Id.ToString(),
   266	            snapshot.Definition.Name,
   267	            snapshot.Origin.X,
   268	            snapshot.Origin.Y,
   269	            snapshot.Origin.Z,
   270	            snapshot.Orientation,
   271	            snapshot.WorkPosition.X,
   272	            snapshot.WorkPosition.Y,
   273	            snapshot.WorkPosition.Z,
   274	            snapshot.Status,
   275	            snapshot.CompletedWork,
   276	            snapshot.Definition.RequiredWork,
   277	            snapshot.Version,
   278	            footprint,
   279	            _functions.Present(snapshot),
   280	            snapshot.BoxPlan?.SourceStackId.ToString(),
   281	            snapshot.BoxPlan?.JobId.ToString(),
   282	            snapshot.BoxPlan?.CommitState);
   283	    }
   284	}

===== src/Dig.Presentation.Abstractions/Jobs/JobOverlayPresenter.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.Collections.Generic;
     3	using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
     4	using System.Linq;
     5	using Dig.Application.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Application.Messaging;
     7	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     8	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     9	
    10	namespace Dig.Presentation.Jobs
    11	{
    12	
    13	public sealed class JobOverlayPresenter
    14	{
    15	    private const string PrepareSuggestedToolLabel = "Equip suggested tool";
    16	    private const string BypassSuggestedToolLabel = "Proceed without suggested tool";
    17	    private readonly IQueryHandler<GetJobsQuery, IReadOnlyList<JobSnapshot>> _jobs;
    18	    private readonly IQueryHandler<GetJobReservationsQuery, IReadOnlyList<ReservationSnapshot>> _reservations;
    19	
    20	    public JobOverlayPresenter(
    21	        IQueryHandler<GetJobsQuery, IReadOnlyList<JobSnapshot>> jobs,
    22	        IQueryHandler<GetJobReservationsQuery, IReadOnlyList<ReservationSnapshot>> reservations)
    23	    {
    24	        _jobs = jobs ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(jobs));
    25	        _reservations = reservations ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(reservations));
    26	    }
    27	
    28	    public IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> Load(
    29	        JobAssignmentReport? latestAssignmentReport = null)
    30	    {
    31	        return LoadCore(latestAssignmentReport, assignmentReports: null);
    32	    }
    33	
    34	    public IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> LoadIndexed(
    35	        IReadOnlyDictionary<EntityId, JobAssignmentReport> assignmentReports)
    36	    {
    37	        if (assignmentReports is null)
    38	        {
    39	            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(assignmentReports));
    40	        }
    41	
    42	        return LoadCore(latestAssignmentReport: null, assignmentReports);
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    private IReadOnlyList<JobOverlayViewModel> LoadCore(
    46	        JobAssignmentReport? latestAssignmentReport,
    47	        IReadOnlyDictionary<EntityId, JobAssignmentReport>? assignmentReports)
    48	    {
    49	        IReadOnlyList<JobSnapshot> jobs = _jobs.Handle(new GetJobsQuery());
    50	        IReadOnlyList<ReservationSnapshot> reservations = _reservations.Handle(
    51	            new GetJobReservationsQuery());
    52	        JobOverlayViewModel[] models = new JobOverlayViewModel[jobs.Count];
    53	        for (int index = 0; index < jobs.Count; index++)
    54	        {
    55	            JobSnapshot job = jobs[index];
    56	            JobAssignmentReport? report = ResolveReport(
    57	                job.Id,
    58	                latestAssignmentReport,
    59	                assignmentReports);
    60	            models[index] = Map(
    61	                job,
    62	                reservations,
    63	                MapAssignment(job.Id, report),
    64	                MapActions(job, reservations, report),
    65	                JobExecutionReadinessProjection.Map(job, report));
    66	        }
    67	
    68	        return new ReadOnlyCollection<JobOverlayViewModel>(models);
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    private static JobOverlayViewModel Map(
    72	        JobSnapshot job,
    73	        IReadOnlyList<ReservationSnapshot> reservations,
    74	        JobAssignmentDiagnosticViewModel? assignmentDiagnostic,
    75	        IReadOnlyList<JobActionViewModel> actions,
    76	        JobExecutionReadinessViewModel executionReadiness)
    77	    {
    78	        JobReservationViewModel[] values = reservations
    79	            .Where(item => item.JobId == job.Id)
    80	            .Select(item => new JobReservationViewModel(
    81	                item.Key.Kind.ToString(),
    82	                item.Key.Value,
    83	                item.AgentId.ToString(),
    84	                item.AcquiredTick))
    85	            .OrderBy(item => item.Kind, StringComparer.Ordinal)
    86	            .ThenBy(item => item.Value, StringComparer.Ordinal)
    87	            .ToArray();
    88	        int? targetX = null;
    89	        int? targetY = null;
    90	        int? targetZ = null;
    91	        if (job.Definition is DigJobDefinition digging)
    92	        {
    93	            targetX = digging.Target.CellId.X;
    94	            targetY = digging.Target.CellId.Y;
    95	            targetZ = 0;
    96	        }
    97	        else if (job.Definition is SpatialDigJobDefinition spatial)
    98	        {
    99	            targetX = spatial.Target.TargetCell.X;
   100	            targetY = spatial.Target.TargetCell.Y;
   101	            targetZ = spatial.Target.TargetCell.Z;
   102	        }
   103	        else if (job.Definition is MushroomChopJobDefinition mushroom)
   104	        {
   105	            targetX = mushroom.TargetCell.X;
   106	            targetY = mushroom.TargetCell.Y;
   107	            targetZ = mushroom.TargetCell.Z;
   108	        }
   109	        else if (job.Definition is BarrelAttackJobDefinition barrel)
   110	        {
   111	            targetX = barrel.TargetCell.X;
   112	            targetY = barrel.TargetCell.Y;
   113	            targetZ = barrel.TargetCell.Z;
   114	        }
   115	
   116	        return new JobOverlayViewModel(
   117	            job.Id.ToString(),
   118	            job.Definition.Description,
   119	            job.Status.ToString(),
   120	            job.Stage.ToString(),
   121	            job.Definition.Priority,
   122	            job.AssignedAgentId?.ToString(),
   123	            targetX,
   124	            targetY,
   125	            job.RetryCount,
   126	            job.NextRetryTick,
   127	            job.Reason?.ToString(),
   128	            new ReadOnlyCollection<JobReservationViewModel>(values),
   129	            job.Definition.PreferredToolKind,
   130	            assignmentDiagnostic,
   131	            actions,
   132	            executionReadiness,
   133	            targetZ,
   134	            isMushroomChop: job.Definition is MushroomChopJobDefinition,
   135	            isBarrelAttack: job.Definition is BarrelAttackJobDefinition);
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    private static IReadOnlyList<JobActionViewModel> MapActions(
   139	        JobSnapshot job,
   140	        IReadOnlyList<ReservationSnapshot> reservations,
   141	        JobAssignmentReport? report)
   142	    {
   143	        JobAssignment? suggestion = report?.Assignments.SingleOrDefault(
   144	            item => item.JobId == job.Id);
   145	        if (suggestion is null
   146	            || suggestion.ToolPreparation != JobToolPreparationOutcome.Suggested
   147	            || !suggestion.ToolStackId.HasValue)
   148	        {
   149	            return Array.Empty<JobActionViewModel>();
   150	        }
   151	
   152	        DomainError? prepareDisabledReason = ResolvePrepareDisabledReason(
   153	            job,
   154	            suggestion,
   155	            reservations);
   156	        DomainError? bypassDisabledReason = ResolveBypassDisabledReason(job);
   157	        return new[]
   158	        {
   159	            new JobActionViewModel(
   160	                JobActionKind.PrepareSuggestedTool,
   161	                PrepareSuggestedToolLabel,
   162	                isEnabled: prepareDisabledReason is null,
   163	                disabledReasonCode: prepareDisabledReason?.Code,
   164	                disabledReasonMessage: prepareDisabledReason?.Message),
   165	            new JobActionViewModel(
   166	                JobActionKind.BypassSuggestedTool,
   167	                BypassSuggestedToolLabel,
   168	                isEnabled: bypassDisabledReason is null,
   169	                disabledReasonCode: bypassDisabledReason?.Code,
   170	                disabledReasonMessage: bypassDisabledReason?.Message),
   171	        };
   172	    }
   173	
   174	    private static DomainError? ResolvePrepareDisabledReason(
   175	        JobSnapshot job,
   176	        JobAssignment suggestion,
   177	        IReadOnlyList<ReservationSnapshot> reservations)
   178	    {
   179	        DomainError? statusReason = ResolveBypassDisabledReason(job);
   180	        if (statusReason != null)
   181	        {
   182	            return statusReason;
   183	        }
   184	
   185	        if (suggestion.AgentId != job.AssignedAgentId!.Value)
   186	        {
   187	            return PrepareSuggestedJobToolErrors.SuggestionStale;
   188	        }
   189	
   190	        EntityId toolStackId = suggestion.ToolStackId!.Value;
   191	        ReservationSnapshot? reservation = reservations.SingleOrDefault(
   192	            value => value.JobId == job.Id
   193	                && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForTool(toolStackId));
   194	        return reservation is null || reservation.AgentId != suggestion.AgentId
   195	            ? PrepareSuggestedJobToolErrors.ToolReservationMissing
   196	            : null;
   197	    }
   198	
   199	    private static DomainError? ResolveBypassDisabledReason(JobSnapshot job)
   200	    {
   201	        return (job.Status != JobStatus.Claimed && job.Status != JobStatus.InProgress)
   202	            || !job.AssignedAgentId.HasValue
   203	            ? JobErrors.InvalidStatus
   204	            : null;
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    private static JobAssignmentDiagnosticViewModel? MapAssignment(
   208	        EntityId jobId,
   209	        JobAssignmentReport? report)
   210	    {
   211	        if (report is null)
   212	        {
   213	            return null;
   214	        }
   215	
   216	        JobAssignment? assignment = report.Assignments
   217	            .FirstOrDefault(item => item.JobId == jobId);
   218	        if (assignment != null)
   219	        {
   220	            return new JobAssignmentDiagnosticViewModel(
   221	                report.Tick,
   222	                assignment.Score,
   223	                assignment.ToolPreparation,
   224	                assignment.ToolStackId?.ToString(),
   225	                failureCode: null,
   226	                failureMessage: null);
   227	        }
   228	
   229	        JobAssignmentFailure? failure = report.Failures
   230	            .FirstOrDefault(item => item.JobId == jobId);
   231	        return failure is null
   232	            ? null
   233	            : new JobAssignmentDiagnosticViewModel(
   234	                report.Tick,
   235	                score: null,
   236	                toolPreparation: null,
   237	                toolStackId: null,
   238	                failureCode: failure.Error.Code,
   239	                failureMessage: failure.Error.Message);
   240	    }
   241	
   242	    private static JobAssignmentReport? ResolveReport(
   243	        EntityId jobId,
   244	        JobAssignmentReport? latestAssignmentReport,
   245	        IReadOnlyDictionary<EntityId, JobAssignmentReport>? assignmentReports)
   246	    {
   247	        if (assignmentReports != null
   248	            && assignmentReports.TryGetValue(jobId, out JobAssignmentReport? indexedReport))
   249	        {
   250	            return indexedReport;
   251	        }
   252	
   253	        return latestAssignmentReport;
   254	    }
   255	}
   256	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/AuthoritativeXyzSaveTests.cs =====
     1	using System;

     3	using Dig.Application.Saving;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Agents;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Buildings;
     6	using Dig.Domain.Core;

    10	using Xunit;
    11	
    12	namespace Dig.Tests
    13	{
    14	
    15	public sealed class AuthoritativeXyzSaveTests
    16	{
    17	    private static readonly MaterialId Air = new MaterialId("xyz-save.air");
    18	    private static readonly ItemId Plank = new ItemId("xyz-save.plank");
    19	    private static readonly EntityId AgentId = EntityId.Parse(
    20	        "a1000000000000000000000000000001");
    21	    private static readonly EntityId BuildingId = EntityId.Parse(
    22	        "b1000000000000000000000000000001");
    23	
    24	    [Fact]
    25	    public void Agent_position_round_trip_preserves_exact_depth()
    26	    {
    27	        AgentState agent = new AgentState(
    28	            AgentId,
    29	            "Deep Dwarf",
    30	            AgentTestFactory.CreateNeeds(8_000, 8_000, 8_000, 10_000),
    31	            AgentTestFactory.CreateWorkSchedule(),
    32	            skills: null,
    33	            traits: null,
    34	            initialPosition: new CellId(3, 4, 2));
    35	        SaveGameDocument document = CreateBuilder().Build(CreateContext(
    36	            new BuildingsState(),
    37	            new[] { agent }));
    38	
    39	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = CreateLoader().Load(
    40	            document,
    41	            CreateMaterials(),
    42	            CreateItems());
    43	
    44	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess);
    45	        Assert.Equal(new CellId(3, 4, 2), loaded.Value.AgentPositions[AgentId]);
    46	        AgentPositionSaveData saved = Assert.Single(document.AgentPositions.Agents);
    47	        Assert.Equal(2, saved.Z);
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    [Fact]
    51	    public void Building_origin_footprint_and_work_position_round_trip_on_depth_two()
    52	    {
    53	        BuildingDefinition definition = CreateBuildingDefinition();
    54	        CellId origin = new CellId(4, 4, 2);
    55	        CellId workPosition = new CellId(4, 3, 2);
    56	        BuildingsState buildings = new BuildingsState();
    57	        Assert.True(buildings.Place(
    58	            BuildingId,
    59	            definition,
    60	            origin,
    61	            BuildingOrientation.North,
    62	            BuildingPlacementResult.Success(
    63	                definition.ResolveFootprint(origin, BuildingOrientation.North),
    64	                workPosition),
    65	            tick: 1).IsSuccess);
    66	        SaveGameDocument document = CreateBuilder().Build(CreateContext(
    67	            buildings,
    68	            Array.Empty<AgentState>()));
    69	
    70	        Result<LoadedGameState> loaded = CreateLoader().Load(
    71	            document,
    72	            CreateMaterials(),
    73	            CreateItems(),
    74	            new BuildingCatalog(new[] { definition }));
    75	
    76	        Assert.True(loaded.IsSuccess);
    77	        BuildingSnapshot restored = Assert.Single(loaded.Value.Buildings.GetAll());
    78	        Assert.Equal(origin, restored.Origin);
    79	        Assert.Equal(workPosition, restored.WorkPosition);
    80	        Assert.All(restored.Footprint, cell => Assert.Equal(2, cell.Z));
    81	        BuildingSaveData saved = Assert.Single(document.Buildings.Buildings);
    82	        Assert.Equal(2, saved.OriginZ);
    83	        Assert.Equal(2, saved.WorkPositionZ);
    84	    }
    85

    87	        BuildingsState buildings,
    88	        AgentState[] agents)

   100	                DisplayName = "XYZ save",
   101	                SavedAtUtc = "2026-07-22T10:00:00Z",
   102	                SimulationTick = 2,
   103	                WorldSeed = 88,
   104	                GeneratorVersion = 1,
   105	            },
   106	            world,
   107	            new InventoryState(CreateItems()),
   108	            new JobSystem(),
   109	            buildings,
   110	            agents);
   111	    }

   140	            }));
   141	    }
