===== tests/Dig.Tests/BarrelAttackApplicationTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Application.WorldObjects;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     5	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     6	using Dig.Domain.World;
     7	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
     8	using Dig.Infrastructure.InMemory;
     9	using Xunit;
    10	
    11	namespace Dig.Tests
    12	{
    13	
    14	public sealed class BarrelAttackApplicationTests
    15	{
    16	    private static readonly BarrelDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    17	        new BarrelDefinitionId("world.barrel.wooden");
    18	    private static readonly ItemId Stone = new ItemId("material.stone");
    19	    private static readonly ItemId Ore = new ItemId("material.ore");
    20	    private static readonly EntityId BarrelId = Id("c1000000000000000000000000000001");
    21	    private static readonly EntityId FirstJobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000001");
    22	    private static readonly EntityId SecondJobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000002");
    23	    private static readonly EntityId FirstWorkerId = Id("c3000000000000000000000000000001");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId SecondWorkerId = Id("c3000000000000000000000000000002");
    25	    private static readonly CellId Target = new CellId(5, 6, 0);
    26	
    27	    [Fact]
    28	    public void One_hit_destroys_barrel_and_creates_one_world_unit_without_progression_side_effects()
    29	    {
    30	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Stone);
    31	        EntityId output = Id("c4000000000000000000000000000001");
    32	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    33	            FirstJobId,
    34	            BarrelId,
    35	            FirstWorkerId,
    36	            new CellId(4, 6, 0),
    37	            priority: 900,
    38	            tick: 1)).IsSuccess);
    39	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(FirstJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
    40	        Assert.True(harness.Hit.Handle(new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(FirstJobId, 3)).IsSuccess);
    41	
    42	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> completed = harness.Complete.Handle(
    43	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(FirstJobId, output, tick: 4));
    44	
    45	        Assert.True(completed.IsSuccess, completed.Error?.ToString());
    46	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.Completed, harness.Jobs.Get(FirstJobId)!.Status);
    47	        Assert.Empty(harness.Jobs.GetReservations());
    48	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Destroyed, harness.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.Lifecycle);
    49	        ItemStackSnapshot unit = harness.Inventory.GetStack(output)!;
    50	        Assert.Equal(Stone, unit.ItemId);
    51	        Assert.Equal(1, unit.Quantity);
    52	        Assert.Equal(ItemLocation.InWorld(Target), unit.Location);
    53	        Assert.Single(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    [Fact]
    57	    public void Concurrent_attacks_are_allowed_but_only_first_commit_creates_contents()
    58	    {
    59	        CellId firstWorkPosition = new CellId(4, 6, 0);
    60	        CellId secondWorkPosition = new CellId(6, 6, 0);
    61	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Ore);
    62	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    63	            FirstJobId,
    64	            BarrelId,
    65	            FirstWorkerId,
    66	            firstWorkPosition,
    67	            900,
    68	            1)).IsSuccess);
    69	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
    70	            SecondJobId,
    71	            BarrelId,
    72	            SecondWorkerId,
    73	            secondWorkPosition,
    74	            900,
    75	            1)).IsSuccess);
    76	
    77	        ReservationSnapshot[] reservations = harness.Jobs.GetReservations().ToArray();
    78	        Assert.Equal(3, reservations.Count(value => value.JobId == FirstJobId));
    79	        Assert.Equal(3, reservations.Count(value => value.JobId == SecondJobId));
    80	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    81	            value.JobId == FirstJobId
    82	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForJob(FirstJobId));
    83	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    84	            value.JobId == FirstJobId
    85	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForAgent(FirstWorkerId));
    86	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    87	            value.JobId == FirstJobId
    88	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForPosition(firstWorkPosition));
    89	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    90	            value.JobId == SecondJobId
    91	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForJob(SecondJobId));
    92	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    93	            value.JobId == SecondJobId
    94	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForAgent(SecondWorkerId));
    95	        Assert.Contains(reservations, value =>
    96	            value.JobId == SecondJobId
    97	            && value.Key == ReservationKey.ForPosition(secondWorkPosition));
    98	        Assert.DoesNotContain(reservations, value =>
    99	            value.Key == ReservationKey.ForEcologyTarget(BarrelId));
   100	
   101	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(FirstJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
   102	        Assert.True(harness.Arrive.Handle(new ArriveAtBarrelCommand(SecondJobId, 2)).IsSuccess);
   103	        Assert.True(harness.Hit.Handle(new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(FirstJobId, 3)).IsSuccess);
   104	        Assert.True(harness.Hit.Handle(new CompleteBarrelHitCommand(SecondJobId, 3)).IsSuccess);
   105	
   106	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> first = harness.Complete.Handle(
   107	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(
   108	                FirstJobId,
   109	                Id("c4000000000000000000000000000001"),
   110	                4));
   111	        Result<BarrelDestructionResult> second = harness.Complete.Handle(
   112	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommand(
   113	                SecondJobId,
   114	                Id("c4000000000000000000000000000002"),
   115	                4));
   116	
   117	        Assert.True(first.IsSuccess, first.Error?.ToString());
   118	        Assert.Equal(BarrelApplicationErrors.GenerationConflict, second.Error);
   119	        Assert.Single(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   120	        Assert.Equal(Ore, harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks.Single().ItemId);
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    [Fact]
   124	    public void Cancel_before_hit_preserves_supported_barrel_and_contents()
   125	    {
   126	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Stone);
   127	        Assert.True(harness.Start.Handle(new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
   128	            FirstJobId,

   130	            FirstWorkerId,
   131	            new CellId(4, 6, 0),
   132	            900,
   133	            1)).IsSuccess);
   134	
   135	        Assert.True(harness.Cancel.Handle(new CancelBarrelAttackCommand(
   136	            FirstJobId,
   137	            "player_cancelled",
   138	            tick: 2)).IsSuccess);
   139	
   140	        BarrelSnapshot barrel = harness.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
   141	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, barrel.Lifecycle);
   142	        Assert.Equal(Stone, barrel.ContentsItemId);
   143	        Assert.False(barrel.ContentsMaterialized);
   144	        Assert.Empty(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    [Fact]
   148	    public void Reserved_worker_is_rejected_without_exception_or_barrel_mutation()
   149	    {
   150	        Harness harness = CreateHarness(Stone);
   151	        EntityId existingJobId = Id("c2000000000000000000000000000099");
   152	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Add(new DigJobDefinition(
   153	            existingJobId,
   154	            new DigJobTarget(new CellId(1, 1, 0)),
   155	            priority: 500,
   156	            createdTick: 0,
   157	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default)).IsSuccess);
   158	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.MakeAvailable(existingJobId, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   159	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Claim(existingJobId, FirstWorkerId, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   160	        Assert.True(harness.Jobs.Start(existingJobId, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   161	
   162	        Result<BarrelAttackStartedResult> started = harness.Start.Handle(
   163	            new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommand(
   164	                FirstJobId,
   165	                BarrelId,
   166	                FirstWorkerId,
   167	                new CellId(4, 6, 0),
   168	                priority: 900,
   169	                tick: 1));
   170	
   171	        Assert.True(started.IsFailure);
   172	        Assert.Equal(JobErrors.AgentUnavailable, started.Error);
   173	        Assert.Null(harness.Jobs.Get(FirstJobId));
   174	        Assert.Equal(JobStatus.InProgress, harness.Jobs.Get(existingJobId)!.Status);
   175	        Assert.All(harness.Jobs.GetReservations(), reservation =>
   176	            Assert.Equal(existingJobId, reservation.JobId));
   177	        BarrelSnapshot barrel = harness.Barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
   178	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, barrel.Lifecycle);
   179	        Assert.Equal(Stone, barrel.ContentsItemId);
   180	        Assert.False(barrel.ContentsMaterialized);
   181	        Assert.Empty(harness.Inventory.CreateSnapshot().Stacks);
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    private static Harness CreateHarness(ItemId contents)
   185	    {
   186	        BarrelState barrels = new BarrelState(new BarrelCatalog(new[]
   187	        {
   188	            new BarrelDefinition(DefinitionId, new[] { Stone, Ore }),
   189	        }));
   190	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, Target, contents, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   191	        JobSystem jobs = new JobSystem();
   192	        InventoryState inventory = new InventoryState(new ItemCatalog(new[]
   193	        {
   194	            new ItemDefinition(Stone, "Stone", 100, isTool: false),
   195	            new ItemDefinition(Ore, "Ore", 100, isTool: false),
   196	        }));
   197	        InMemoryExecutionJournal events = new InMemoryExecutionJournal();
   198	        InMemoryBarrelRepository barrelRepository = new InMemoryBarrelRepository(barrels);
   199	        InMemoryJobRepository jobRepository = new InMemoryJobRepository(jobs);
   200	        InMemoryInventoryRepository inventoryRepository = new InMemoryInventoryRepository(inventory);
   201	        return new Harness(
   202	            barrels,
   203	            jobs,
   204	            inventory,
   205	            new StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler(barrelRepository, jobRepository, events),
   206	            new ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler(jobRepository, events),
   207	            new CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler(jobRepository, events),
   208	            new CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler(
   209	                barrelRepository,
   210	                jobRepository,
   211	                inventoryRepository,
   212	                events),
   213	            new CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler(jobRepository, events));
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    private static EntityId Id(string value) => EntityId.Parse(value);
   217	
   218	    private sealed class Harness
   219	    {
   220	        public Harness(
   221	            BarrelState barrels,
   222	            JobSystem jobs,
   223	            InventoryState inventory,
   224	            StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler start,
   225	            ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler arrive,
   226	            CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler hit,
   227	            CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler complete,
   228	            CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler cancel)
   229	        {
   230	            Barrels = barrels;
   231	            Jobs = jobs;
   232	            Inventory = inventory;
   233	            Start = start;
   234	            Arrive = arrive;
   235	            Hit = hit;
   236	            Complete = complete;
   237	            Cancel = cancel;
   238	        }
   239	
   240	        public BarrelState Barrels { get; }
   241	        public JobSystem Jobs { get; }
   242	        public InventoryState Inventory { get; }
   243	        public StartDirectBarrelAttackCommandHandler Start { get; }
   244	        public ArriveAtBarrelCommandHandler Arrive { get; }
   245	        public CompleteBarrelHitCommandHandler Hit { get; }
   246	        public CompleteBarrelDestructionCommandHandler Complete { get; }
   247	        public CancelBarrelAttackCommandHandler Cancel { get; }
   248	    }
   249	}
   250	
   251	}
===== tests/Dig.Tests/BarrelDirectCommandReservationContractTests.cs =====
     1	using System;
     2	using System.IO;
     3	using Xunit;
     4	
     5	namespace Dig.Tests
     6	{
     7	
     8	public sealed class BarrelDirectCommandReservationContractTests
     9	{
    10	    [Fact]
    11	    public void Barrel_start_returns_typed_reservation_failure_and_runtime_replaces_assignment()
    12	    {
    13	        string root = FindRepositoryRoot();
    14	        string start = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
    15	            root,
    16	            "src",
    17	            "Dig.Application",
    18	            "WorldObjects",
    19	            "BarrelAttackStartUseCase.cs"));
    20	        string direct = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
    21	            root,
    22	            "unity",
    23	            "Dig.Unity",
    24	            "Assets",
    25	            "Dig.Unity",
    26	            "Runtime",
    27	            "DigTerrainWorkSession.DirectCommands.cs"));
    28	        string playMode = File.ReadAllText(Path.Combine(
    29	            root,
    30	            "unity",
    31	            "Dig.Unity",
    32	            "Assets",
    33	            "Dig.Unity",
    34	            "Tests",
    35	            "PlayMode",
    36	            "BarrelAttackSurfacePlayModeTests.cs"));
    37	
    38	        Assert.Contains("ReservationKey.ForAgent(command.WorkerId)", start);
    39	        Assert.Contains("JobErrors.AgentUnavailable", start);
    40	        Assert.Contains("jobs.CanClaim", start);
    41	        Assert.Contains("RejectNewAttack", start);
    42	        Assert.DoesNotContain("Validated barrel attack start failed", start);
    43	        Assert.Contains("!job.IsTerminal", direct);
    44	        Assert.Contains("job.AssignedAgentId == residentId", direct);
    45	        Assert.Contains("RemoveAllRoutePlans(job.Id)", direct);
    46	        Assert.Contains(
    47	            "Direct_barrel_order_replaces_existing_job_and_claims_selected_resident",
    48	            playMode);
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    private static string FindRepositoryRoot()
    52	    {
    53	        DirectoryInfo? current = new DirectoryInfo(AppContext.BaseDirectory);
    54	        while (current != null)
    55	        {
    56	            if (File.Exists(Path.Combine(current.FullName, "Dig.sln")))
    57	            {
    58	                return current.FullName;
    59	            }
    60	
    61	            current = current.Parent;
    62	        }
    63	
    64	        throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Repository root was not found.");
    65	    }
    66	}

===== tests/Dig.Tests/BarrelStateTests.cs =====
     1	using System.Linq;
     2	using Dig.Domain.Core;
     3	using Dig.Domain.Inventory;
     4	using Dig.Domain.Jobs;
     5	using Dig.Domain.World;
     6	using Dig.Domain.WorldObjects;
     7	using Xunit;
     8	
     9	namespace Dig.Tests
    10	{
    11	
    12	public sealed class BarrelStateTests
    13	{
    14	    private static readonly BarrelDefinitionId DefinitionId =
    15	        new BarrelDefinitionId("world.barrel.wooden");
    16	    private static readonly ItemId Stone = new ItemId("material.stone");
    17	    private static readonly ItemId Ore = new ItemId("material.ore");
    18	    private static readonly EntityId BarrelId =
    19	        EntityId.Parse("b1000000000000000000000000000001");
    20	    private static readonly EntityId JobOne =
    21	        EntityId.Parse("b2000000000000000000000000000001");
    22	    private static readonly EntityId JobTwo =
    23	        EntityId.Parse("b2000000000000000000000000000002");
    24	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerOne =
    25	        EntityId.Parse("b3000000000000000000000000000001");
    26	    private static readonly EntityId WorkerTwo =
    27	        EntityId.Parse("b3000000000000000000000000000002");
    28	
    29	    [Fact]
    30	    public void First_hit_destroys_barrel_and_materializes_saved_contents_once()
    31	    {
    32	        BarrelState barrels = CreateState();
    33	        CellId cell = new CellId(4, 5, 1);
    34	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, cell, Stone, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    35	        BarrelSnapshot before = barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
    36	
    37	        Result<BarrelDestructionCommit> first = barrels.Destroy(
    38	            BarrelId,
    39	            before.Version,
    40	            JobOne,
    41	            WorkerOne,
    42	            tick: 1);
    43	        Result<BarrelDestructionCommit> duplicate = barrels.Destroy(
    44	            BarrelId,
    45	            before.Version,
    46	            JobTwo,
    47	            WorkerTwo,
    48	            tick: 1);
    49	
    50	        Assert.True(first.IsSuccess, first.Error?.ToString());
    51	        Assert.Equal(Stone, first.Value.ContentsItemId);
    52	        Assert.Equal(cell, first.Value.Cell);
    53	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Destroyed, barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.Lifecycle);
    54	        Assert.True(barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.ContentsMaterialized);
    55	        Assert.Equal(BarrelErrors.NotAttackable, duplicate.Error);
    56	        Assert.Empty(barrels.GetBuildingBlockedCells());
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    [Fact]
    60	    public void Concurrent_jobs_do_not_reserve_the_barrel_target()
    61	    {
    62	        CellId target = new CellId(5, 5, 0);
    63	        CellId firstWork = new CellId(4, 5, 0);
    64	        CellId secondWork = new CellId(6, 5, 0);
    65	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition first = CreateJob(JobOne, firstWork);
    66	        BarrelAttackJobDefinition second = CreateJob(JobTwo, secondWork);
    67	
    68	        ReservationKey[] firstReservations = first.CreateReservationKeys().ToArray();
    69	        ReservationKey[] secondReservations = second.CreateReservationKeys().ToArray();
    70	
    71	        Assert.Single(firstReservations);
    72	        Assert.Single(secondReservations);
    73	        Assert.Contains(ReservationKey.ForPosition(firstWork), firstReservations);
    74	        Assert.Contains(ReservationKey.ForPosition(secondWork), secondReservations);
    75	        Assert.DoesNotContain(firstReservations, value => value == ReservationKey.ForEcologyTarget(BarrelId));
    76	        Assert.Equal(new[]
    77	        {
    78	            JobStageKind.TravelToTarget,
    79	            JobStageKind.PerformWork,
    80	            JobStageKind.Finalize,
    81	        }, first.Stages);
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    [Fact]
    85	    public void Support_loss_relocates_barrel_without_damage_or_contents_output()
    86	    {
    87	        BarrelState barrels = CreateState();
    88	        CellId source = new CellId(3, 2, 0);
    89	        CellId landing = new CellId(3, 8, 0);
    90	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, source, Ore, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
    91	
    92	        Assert.True(barrels.BeginFall(BarrelId, landing, tick: 2).IsSuccess);
    93	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Falling, barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.Lifecycle);
    94	        Assert.True(barrels.Land(BarrelId, tick: 2).IsSuccess);
    95	
    96	        BarrelSnapshot landed = barrels.Get(BarrelId)!;
    97	        Assert.Equal(BarrelLifecycle.Supported, landed.Lifecycle);
    98	        Assert.Equal(landing, landed.Cell);
    99	        Assert.Equal(Ore, landed.ContentsItemId);
   100	        Assert.False(landed.ContentsMaterialized);
   101	        Assert.Equal(landing, Assert.Single(barrels.GetBuildingBlockedCells()));
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    [Fact]
   105	    public void Supported_barrel_blocks_buildings_but_job_has_no_movement_occupancy()
   106	    {
   107	        BarrelState barrels = CreateState();
   108	        CellId cell = new CellId(8, 4, 2);
   109	        Assert.True(barrels.Add(BarrelId, DefinitionId, cell, Stone, tick: 0).IsSuccess);
   110	
   111	        Assert.Equal(cell, Assert.Single(barrels.GetBuildingBlockedCells()));
   112	        Assert.True(barrels.Get(BarrelId)!.IsAttackable);
   113	    }
   114	
   115	    private static BarrelState CreateState()
   116	    {
   117	        return new BarrelState(new BarrelCatalog(new[]
   118	        {
   119	            new BarrelDefinition(DefinitionId, new[] { Stone, Ore }),
   120	        }));
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    private static BarrelAttackJobDefinition CreateJob(EntityId jobId, CellId workPosition)
   124	    {
   125	        return new BarrelAttackJobDefinition(
   126	            jobId,
   127	            BarrelId,
   128	            new CellId(5, 5, 0),
   129	            workPosition,
   130	            barrelVersion: 0,
   131	            contentsGeneration: 0,
   132	            priority: 900,
   133	            createdTick: 0,
   134	            retryPolicy: JobRetryPolicy.Default);
   135	    }
   136	}
   137	
   138	}
